### [1] [Rethinking KV Cache Eviction for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00391)
*Jiakun Fan,Han Meng,Yuxuan Xia,Hao Mark Chen,Dong Li,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型服务中Key-Value缓存的内存压力，现有机制倾向于从序列末尾驱逐以保留前缀共享，但代理工作负载会在后续轮次中重复重用长历史。Penelope通过保留共享前缀并从剩余历史开头驱逐KV缓存同时保留缓存后缀来解决这一问题，并协同设计了驱逐策略、KV重计算和统一的GPU-CPU缓存管理。返回请求可复用GPU或CPU内存中保留的KV缓存，仅重计算缺失段并保留后缀复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存对大语言模型服务系统造成巨大的内存压力，在容量不足时需要驱逐。现有机制通常从缓存序列的两端驱逐以保留前缀共享，但代理工作负载会在 successive turns 中反复重用长历史。靠近序列末尾的token的KV重计算成本显著高于较早的token，因为它们的注意力计算涉及更长的历史。

Method: Penelope围绕上述策略构建，通过协同设计驱逐策略、KV重计算和统一的GPU-CPU缓存管理。返回请求复用GPU或CPU内存中保留的KV缓存，仅重计算缺失段，同时保留缓存后缀的复用。其通过在剩余历史开头驱逐KV缓存同时保留缓存后缀的方式来实现。

Result: 与SGLang相比，Penelope在三个Qwen3模型规模的真实代理痕迹上评估。使用仅GPU缓存时，平均首token时间最多降低60%，作业完成时间最多降低37%；使用两层GPU-CPU缓存时， MTTFT最多降低76%，作业完成时间最多降低60%。

Conclusion: Penelope通过协同的缓存驱逐和增量重计算策略，有效保留了KV缓存的后缀复用，显著降低了代理工作负载的延迟和完成时间，展现出在GPU-only和两层GPU-CPU缓存配置下的显著性能提升。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caches impose substantial memory pressure on large language model serving systems, requiring cache eviction when capacity is insufficient. Existing mechanisms typically evict KV from the ends of cached sequences to preserve prefix sharing across requests. However, agent workloads repeatedly reuse long histories across successive turns. Recomputing the KV cache for tokens near the end of a sequence is substantially more expensive than for earlier tokens because their attention computation involves longer histories. To address this problem, we propose preserving shared prefixes and evicting the KV cache from the beginning of the remaining history while retaining the cached suffix. We build Penelope around this strategy by co-designing the eviction policy, KV recomputation, and unified GPU-CPU cache management. Returning requests reuse the KV cache retained in GPU or CPU memory and recompute only missing segments while preserving reuse of the cached suffix. We implement Penelope in SGLang and evaluate it on three Qwen3 model sizes using real agent traces. Compared with SGLang, Penelope reduces mean time to first token by up to 60% and job completion time by up to 37% with a GPU-only cache, and by up to 76% and 60% with a two-tier GPU-CPU cache.

</details>


### [2] [Understanding GPU Megakernels: Why Residency Can Mislead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00495)
*Xinwei Qiang,Ziyi Xu,Chang Chen,Zaifeng Pan,Zhongkai Yu,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究重构了两个LLM megakernel（ThunderKittens和VDCores）作为独立内核，在H100上评估持久内核与分离内核的性能差异，揭示了CTA驻留率不能准确反映重叠机会，启动重叠和预取依赖资源可用性，持久CTA能更早复用存储，而分离后继内核需获取完整CTA占用才能初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化megakernel中持久执行、程序化依赖启动（PDL）和预取带来的收益与开销，理解CTA驻留率对重叠机会的误导，以及资源共享限制对性能的影响。

Method: 在H100 GPU上，使用原始算子代码将ThunderKittens和VDCores megakernel重构为独立内核，对比持久内核与分离内核在解码步骤延迟、启动重叠、操作数加载、内存排序、预取、资源占用等方面的表现。

Result: 重构内核通过PDL将启动和操作数加载与执行重叠，TK解码步骤延迟降低5.7%，VDCores性能相当；持久执行在全算子依赖下仍从预取获益，但运行时和完成信号排序开销抵消优势；CTA驻留率双向误判重叠机会；预留SM给通信会增加GEMM波次抵消重叠收益，填充空闲SM在内存带宽饱和后收益微小。

Conclusion: megakernel的优势在于早期准备和资源复用，但受限于内核间资源共享粒度；未来GPU应支持跨内核更早的资源复用，以进一步释放持久执行和重叠的潜力。

Abstract: Megakernels let one persistent GPU program prepare dependent operators early and execute ready tasks together. We study the benefits and costs of these capabilities by reconstructing two LLM megakernels, ThunderKittens (TK) and VDCores, as separate kernels on H100 using their original operator code. Our results show that CTA residency can misrepresent opportunities for useful overlap in both directions. By overlapping startup and operand loading with programmatic dependent launch (PDL), the reconstructions reduce decode-step latency by 5.7% in TK and achieve comparable performance in VDCores when both executions enforce the required memory ordering. Under whole-operator dependencies, TK's persistent execution still gains more from prefetch, but runtime and completion-signal ordering costs outweigh this advantage. Both startup overlap and prefetch depend on resource availability. Persistent CTAs can reuse storage before retiring, whereas separate successors must acquire their full CTA footprints before initializing or loading operands. This admission constraint in both reconstructions motivates finer resource sharing across kernels. Beyond early preparation, joint execution requires ready work and spare capacity in the bottleneck resource. Reserving SMs for communication can add GEMM waves that erase overlap gains, while filling idle SMs offers little benefit once memory bandwidth is saturated. These findings clarify benefits and costs of megakernels and motivate GPU support for earlier resource reuse across kernels.

</details>


### [3] [Weights That Stay: On-Chip Megakernels for Serial Heads in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00506)
*Xinwei Qiang,Ziyi Xu,Zaifeng Pan,Chang Chen,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Weights That Stay，针对并行推测解码中轻量串行头因每步反复搬运大词表投影矩阵导致延迟的问题，将投影分片在芯片上常驻整个推测步进，并用持久 megakernel 耦合 GPU 端令牌选择与状态推进，在 SGLang 上实现跨步权重复用，提升马尔可夫推断吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行推测解码使用轻量串行头恢复块内 token 依赖，每步仅更新小状态，但仍需通过同一词表大小的投影矩阵进行投影。逐步执行会反复传输该大且不可变的投影，使算术开销低的头成为显著延迟来源。

Method: Weights That Stay 在整个串行推测走动期间保持投影分片 on-chip；持久 megakernel 将权重常驻与 GPU 侧 token 选择和状态推进耦合，步间仅交换紧凑状态；支持全或部分常驻，兼容马尔可夫头和循环头，在 H100 与 B200 上运行，并集成至 SGLang。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B 上，马尔可夫推断吞吐最高提升 12.3%；头部层面 DSpark 走动比 SGLang 最快参考路径快最高 11.6 倍。

Conclusion: 将权重常驻与完整推测走动对齐，可以将跨步权重复用转化为端到端大模型服务的吞吐增益。

Abstract: Parallel speculative drafters use a lightweight serial head to recover token dependencies within a block generated in parallel. Each head step updates only a small state, yet projects that state through the same vocabulary-sized matrix. Step-wise execution repeatedly transfers this large, immutable projection, making the head a meaningful source of latency despite its low arithmetic cost. We present Weights That Stay, an execution design that keeps projection shards on chip for the entire serial draft walk. A persistent megakernel couples weight residency with GPU-side token selection and state advancement, reusing resident weights while exchanging only compact state between steps. The design supports full or partial residency and accommodates both Markov and recurrent heads on H100 and B200. Integrated into SGLang, Weights That Stay improves throughput for Markov drafting on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, with gains of up to 12.3%. At the head level, the DSpark walk is up to 11.6$\times$ faster than the fastest measured SGLang reference path. These results show that aligning weight residency with the full draft walk translates cross-step reuse into end-to-end serving gains.

</details>


### [4] [Communication-Efficient Model-Distributed Inference for CBRS Radar Detection at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00625)
*Teng Li,Hulya Seferoglu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: The paper proposes CEDAR, an edge-distributed architecture for radar signal detection in the CBRS band (3550-3700 MHz), combining a lightweight RF-aware YOLO detector with a compression-aware model-distributed inference framework to overcome multi-scale activation-transfer bottlenecks. It demonstrates that removing two detection scales from YOLO's neck and replacing the last detection scale with RF-aware filtering eliminates cross-scale activation transfer, reducing parameter count by 35–57% and inference time by 16–33%. The accompanying CEDAR-MDI framework selects optimal partition points per activation compressor to maximize throughput gains (up to 3.27× single-device throughput) while preserving detection quality on real-world over-the-air data on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spectrogram-based YOLO detectors can localize both radar and cellular signals in the CBRS spectrum but suffer from multi-scale architectures that incur high activation-transfer costs when partitioned across edge devices via model-distributed inference (MDI). Existing partitioning strategies are suboptimal because they do not account for the communication bottleneck at each scale transition, limiting throughput improvements. The goal is to design a radar-detection architecture that is both communication-efficient and MDI-friendly so that real-time radar detection can run effectively on resource-constrained edge hardware like the Jetson Orin Nano.

Method: CEDAR consists of two components: (1) CEDAR-YOLO, which removes YOLO's multi-scale neck and two of its three detection scales, retaining only one-scale-per-resolution path with independently configurable frequency and time resolutions; and (2) CEDAR-MDI, an MDI framework that uses lightweight activation compressors and trains, for each compressor, the partition point that minimizes pipeline bottlenecks. The design optimizes both model compression and partition layout to reduce activation transfers across device boundaries.

Result: On the Jetson Orin Nano testbed with real-world over-the-air CBRS data, CEDAR-YOLO achieves a 35–57% reduction in parameter count and a 16–33% reduction in model inference time compared to full multi-scale YOLO baselines. CEDAR-MDI achieves up to 3.27× the throughput of single-device inference while maintaining identical detection quality as single-device models.

Conclusion: CEDAR's removal of redundant detection scales and its adaptive partition strategy through MDI successfully address the activation-transfer bottleneck. The resulting architecture delivers substantial hardware savings without sacrificing radar detection performance, demonstrating that edge-distributed inference for CBRS radar detection is both feasible and practically beneficial.

Abstract: The Citizens Broadband Radio Service (CBRS) shares the 3550-3700 MHz band between incumbent radar systems and commercial LTE/5G users, so radar activity must be detected accurately and promptly. Spectrogram-based YOLO detectors can localize coexisting radar and cellular signals, but running them on resource-constrained edge devices is challenging. Model-distributed inference (MDI) can increase throughput by partitioning a model across edge devices. Using an end-to-end pipeline from raw I/Q samples to on-device detection, we show that partitioning recent YOLO generations incurs high activation-transfer costs due to their multi-scale structures. To reduce these costs, we propose CEDAR, a communication-efficient, edge-distributed architecture for radar detection. Its RF-aware detector, CEDAR-YOLO, removes YOLO's multi-scale neck and two of its three detection scales, leaving a single-scale path with independently configured frequency and time resolutions, which lowers the communication cost of partitioning. Its compression-aware MDI framework, CEDAR-MDI, integrates lightweight activation compressors and selects, for each compressor, the partition that minimizes the pipeline bottleneck. On an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano testbed with real-world over-the-air data, CEDAR-YOLO reduces the parameter count by 35-57% and model inference time by 16-33%, and CEDAR-MDI achieves up to $3.27\times$ the throughput of single-device inference, with detection quality preserved in both cases.

</details>


### [5] [Ripple: Deadline-Aware CPU-GPU Sharing for Embodied Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01067)
*Jiadong Zhang,Xiaosong Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对统一内存边缘设备上 LLM/VLM 推理与时效敏感 CPU 任务的资源竞争问题，提出 Ripple CPU-GPU 协同调度系统，通过模型推导的 GPU 内存流量预算与 CPU 完成反馈调节干扰，在满足 CPU/VLM 时效目标的同时保留 LLM 最佳努力吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一内存边缘设备（如 DGX Spark）需同时运行感知与控制等时效关键 CPU 任务和 LLM/VLM 推理，但 GPU 内存流量会造成严重干扰；该平台既不暴露带宽计数器，也不支持直接带宽限制，缺乏有效的干扰隔离与时效保障机制。

Method: 提出 Ripple 调度器，将推理划分为可暂停的基于层的单元，结合模型推导的 GPU 内存体积与 CPU 任务完成反馈进行干扰调节；将每个 CPU 任务的截止时间余量转换为 GPU 字节预算，仅在所有重叠任务预算内准入推理工作；通过在线更新跟踪干扰变化，并采用截止时间感知节流与合理收窄 SM 占用保持推理进度；基于 SGLang 引擎钩子与中央调度器实现。

Result: 在 DGX Spark 上使用四种机器人 CPU 任务混合和 LLM/VLM 推理进行评估，Ripple 是唯一在多种截止时间设置下持续满足 2% CPU 截止时间错失率目标，同时满足 VLM 截止时间并保持最佳努力 LLM 吞吐的方法。

Conclusion: Ripple 表明无需依赖硬件带宽计数器，即可通过轻量级模型驱动的内存流量预算与闭环 CPU 反馈，在边缘设备上实现生成式推理与时效任务的协调，为严格时效保障与推理吞吐兼顾提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Unified-memory edge devices increasingly run LLM/VLM inference alongside time-critical CPU tasks for perception and control. GPU memory traffic can overwhelm these tasks, yet platforms such as DGX Spark expose neither bandwidth counters nor direct bandwidth regulation. We present Ripple, a CPU-GPU co-scheduler that combines model-derived GPU memory volumes with CPU completion feedback to regulate interference. Ripple divides inference into pausable, layer-based units and translates each CPU job's deadline slack into a GPU-byte budget, admitting work within the budgets of all overlapping jobs. Online updates track changing interference, while deadline-aware pacing and judicious SM footprint narrowing preserve inference progress. Implemented with SGLang engine hooks and a central scheduler, we evaluate Ripple on DGX Spark with four robotic CPU task mixes and LLM/VLM inference. It is the only evaluated approach that consistently meets the 2% CPU deadline-miss target and VLM deadlines across the tested deadline settings while retaining best-effort LLM throughput.

</details>


### [6] [When Shards Fly: Cross-Tensor In-Flight Conversion for Low-Latency, Low-Memory Weight Updates in Disaggregated RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00646)
*Jiayu Xiao,Ye Wang,Zhi Zhang,Jiarui Guo,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Minchen Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 异构LLM强化学习中，ShardFly通过编译器和持久内核协调可重用的传输规划与流式GPU执行，实现无中置缓冲的原地权重更新，跨5款模型和4个基线实现1.26–27.92×加速，响应Token吞吐量提升5.49–6.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构LLM RL系统因独立转换通道和暂存缓冲引入同步更新的延迟与GPU显存开销，需要更高效的权重同步方案。

Method: ShardFly包含Planner与Executor。Planner编译原生分片映射、量化依赖与副本路径为跨张量调度，仅为计划好的传输预留payload缓冲。Executor在单一持久传输内核中融合内存布局转换、量化、GPU主动通信与目标写入，并在副本间转换转移字节，配置稳定时复用计划与通信状态。

Result: 在最多32张H20 GPU上，针对5款模型和4个基线（veRL NCCL, Awex, veRL NIXL, WeightBridge），ShardFly实现完整BF16权重更新1.26–27.92×速度提升；在Qwen3-30B-A3B GRPO四种rollout配置中，相较veRL NCCL响应Token吞吐量提升5.49–6.71%。

Conclusion: ShardFly通过编译时规划与运行时流式执行的结合，有效消除了同步权重更新的通信与转换开销，显著提升了分布式LLM训练的效率，为可扩展的异构GPU集群提供了轻量级高性能方案。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM reinforcement learning (RL) repeatedly synchronizes policy weights between training and rollout GPU pools. Existing systems reduce communication through shard delivery and replica forwarding, yet separate conversion passes and staging buffers still add latency and GPU-memory overheads to synchronous updates. We present ShardFly, which coordinates reusable transfer planning with streaming GPU execution for synchronous, in-place weight updates. ShardFly introduces two components. The Planner compiles native shard mappings, quantization dependencies, and replica routes into cross-tensor schedules, reserving payload buffers only for scheduled transfers. The Executor runs each rank's schedule in one persistent transfer kernel, fusing memory-layout conversion, quantization, GPU-initiated communication, and destination writes while forwarding transformed bytes across replicas. Plans and communication state are reused under stable configurations and replaced between updates for coordinated rollout scaling. On up to 32 H20 GPUs, ShardFly achieves complete BF16 weight-update speedups of 1.26--27.92$\times$ across five models and four baselines: veRL NCCL, Awex, veRL NIXL, and WeightBridge. In end-to-end Qwen3-30B-A3B GRPO across four rollout configurations, ShardFly improves response-token throughput by 5.49--6.71\% over veRL NCCL.

</details>


### [7] [LADS: Lease-Aware Delivery Scheduling of Tool Results for Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00881)
*Yuxuan Hou,Linshan Jiang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LADS 是一种针对 Web 代理的工具结果调度器，通过结合缓存感知交付与有界前缀租约机制，优化并发突发工具返回下的缓存竞争。其核心在于通过促进请求尽早恢复来释放缓存保护配额，从而显著降低平均完整工作流延迟（降低 42.4% 至 44.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 代理执行多步在线任务时，并发浏览器动作会突发性完成，导致观察结果在受限路径上累积并竞争缓存状态。传统交付规则（如 FIFO 或优先短传输）忽视了尽早交付结果能够帮助请求更快恢复，从而释放缓存保护容量供其他等待请求使用的潜力。

Method: 提出了 LADS（Lease-Aware Delivery Scheduling）调度器，将缓存感知交付与有界前缀租约相耦合。其关键在于利用请求恢复时额外保护引用的退出，释放租约配额。LADS 基于当前租约所有权和估计的恢复延迟，优先进行 timely handoffs（及时交接），并在缓存优先顺序上进行有界的偏离。

Result: 与采用 FIFO 工具结果交付的 ReAct 相比，LADS 在突发工具返回下将平均完整工作流延迟降低了 44.6%，并在 Web 衍生突发重放（Web-derived burst replay）上实现了 42.4% 的延迟降低。

Conclusion: LADS 通过感知租约和估计恢复延迟来优化交付顺序，验证了通过加速单个请求恢复来动态释放缓存资源、提升系统整体效率的有效性，为 Web 代理的高效调度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Web agents complete multi-step online tasks through repeated browser actions and model calls. Their returned observations lie on the critical path to subsequent decisions. When concurrent browser actions finish in bursts, observations can accumulate on a constrained return path to a shared inference service, while waiting histories compete for reusable cache state. Conventional delivery rules prioritize short transfers or immediate cache reuse but overlook how sending a result earlier can help a request resume sooner and free cache-protection capacity for other waiting requests. We present LADS (Lease-Aware Delivery Scheduling), a tool-result scheduler that couples cache-aware delivery with bounded prefix leases. Its key insight is that resumption lets extra protection references retire, making lease quota available to protect another waiting history. LADS prioritizes timely handoffs using current lease ownership and an estimated resumption delay, with bounded departures from Cache-first order. Compared with ReAct using first-in, first-out (FIFO) tool-result delivery, LADS reduces mean complete-workflow latency by 44.6% under bursty tool returns and by 42.4% on Web-derived burst replay.

</details>


### [8] [Token-as-a-Utility: A Model-Agnostic Architecture for Metering and Provisioning AI Inference Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01371)
*Yichiet Aun,Soung-Yue Liew,Yu Liang Lai,Jing Jie Tan,Dong Chen,Purnomo Husnul Khotimah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Token-as-a-Utility 架构，实现大语言模型推理的可互操作公用事业服务，通过模型无关的推理计量、服务感知的配额分配和运营商间结算，解决了原始 token 计数无法一致衡量资源使用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 token 计数受模型架构、上下文长度、tokenization 及并发服务条件影响，无法同时满足可加性、独立性和精确成本反映性。文中证明在批量服务下，单一每请求计量不可能三者兼顾，从而将配额容量、计量消费和约定质量分离为 distinct service products。

Method: 提出 Reference Inference Units (RIU) 作为消耗单位，源自预填充和解码需求；结合占用指数运营商评级在不改变公共单位的情况下共享服务成本；利用版本化推理记录支持跨运营商审计和重新计价；在 NVIDIA RTX 5000 Ada GPU 上测量资源模型，并使用经校准的痕迹驱动模型评估多租户行为。

Result: 实验显示，基于占用的评级将声明能量误差控制在 9% 以内，而固定占用评级下误差最高达 70.5%。在校准多租户实验中，租户误差从 flat token counting 的 7.36 倍降至 RIU 的 2.39 倍，进一步降至 1.06 倍后经占用-based reconciliation 基本消除。

Conclusion: 研究证明，可移动的推理计量可与负载依赖的成本恢复和基于合同的配额相结合，支持跨运营商的异构推理服务，为大语言模型的公共实用设施账户体系提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Token-based subscriptions are emerging as a commercial model for large language model inference, but extending them into interoperable utility services requires comparable consumption accounting. Raw token counts do not consistently measure inference resource use because demand varies with model architecture, context length, tokenisation, and concurrent serving conditions. This paper presents Token-as-a-Utility, a model-agnostic architecture integrating inference metering, service-level-aware provisioning, and inter-operator settlement. We prove that, under batched serving, no per-request meter can be additive, independent of co-resident traffic, and exactly cost-reflective at once. This motivates separating provisioned capacity, metered consumption, and contracted quality into distinct service products. Consumption is expressed in Reference Inference Units (RIUs) derived from prefill and decode demand, while occupancy-indexed operator ratings recover shared serving costs without altering the common unit. Versioned inference records support auditing and re-rating across operators; software-enforced capacity leases and quality-constrained routing enable provisioning on shared accelerators. The resource model is measured on three NVIDIA RTX 5000 Ada GPUs, and multi-tenant behaviour is evaluated with a trace-driven model calibrated to them. For the reference model, occupancy-indexed rating limits statement energy error to 9% under measured traffic, compared with up to 70.5% under fixed-occupancy rating. In calibrated multi-tenant experiments, tenant mispricing decreases from 7.36 times under flat token counting to 2.39 times with RIUs and 1.06 times after occupancy-based reconciliation. These findings demonstrate how portable inference accounting can be combined with load-dependent cost recovery and contract-based provisioning to support heterogeneous inference services across operators.

</details>


### [9] [Clockwork: Scheduling Compound AI Systems in Space Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00909)
*Milos Gravara,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Clockwork 是一个用于在低轨（LEO）星座空间数据中心上、在端到端延迟 SLO 约束下调度复合 AI（Compound AI）工作流的框架。它通过 Castor 组件按快照联合选择活跃轨道平面与组件放置，并通过 Pollux 组件跨时间连接候选状态以构建低成本的调度序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨星座空间数据中心为复合 AI 工作流在观测源附近执行提供了机会。但由于卫星轨道运动导致可用卫星动态变化，组件放置和轨道平面激活存在时间上的耦合成本，需要将其规划为随时间推移的序列决策，而现有方法难以高效处理这种动态耦合性。

Method: Clockwork 框架包含两个核心组件：i) Castor 针对每个星座快照，联合选择活跃的轨道平面和工作流组件的放置位置；ii) Pollux 结合预测的轨道演化，连接不同时间点 Castor 生成的候选状态，从而构建出跨时间的低成本调度方案。

Result: 在双壳层星座的实验评估中，Castor 能够在 300 毫秒的延迟 SLO 下满足 81% 实例的调度需求；Pollux 相比于启发式基线，平均降低了 56% 的总调度成本，最高降幅达 63%。

Conclusion: Clockwork 框架通过将动态的星座资源规划分解为快照内的联合选择与跨时间的序列优化，成功实现了在复杂空间环境下的高效、低成本复合 AI 工作流调度。

Abstract: Space data centers built on LEO constellations create an opportunity to serve Compound AI workflows close to where observations are captured. Serving such a workflow for an area of interest (AoI) requires deciding which orbital planes to provision and where to place each workflow component, while orbital motion continuously changes which satellites can serve the AoI. Provisioning must therefore be planned as a sequence of decisions over time, and these decisions are coupled because changing component placements or activating new planes incurs costs. To address this, we present Clockwork, a framework for scheduling Compound AI workflows on space data centers under end-to-end latency SLOs. Clockwork has two components: i) Castor jointly selects active planes and component placements for each constellation snapshot, while ii) Pollux connects Castor's candidate provisioning states across predicted orbital evolution to construct a low-cost schedule over time. On our experimental two-shell constellation, Castor serves 81% of instances at a 300 ms SLO, while Pollux reduces mean total scheduling cost by 56% on average and by up to 63% compared to heuristic baselines.

</details>


### [10] [Local Zero-Shot Latency Prediction for Low-End GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02811)
*Arno Geimer,Beltran Fiz,Dimitris Stripelis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，利用本地主机GPU通过修改设备参数来复制较低性能目标GPU的执行性能，从而实现零-shot延迟预测，无需昂贵的跨多设备追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的延迟预测方法依赖于在目标硬件上收集昂贵的执行痕迹，主要集中在少量高端云GPU上，难以覆盖消费级GPU。

Method: 通过选择性修改设备参数，在本地GPU上重现目标GPU的执行性能，将延迟预测任务重新定义为寻找最佳本地配置以复制目标设备性能，实现零-shot预测。

Result: 在60个计算机视觉模型在21款消费级GPU上的真实测量中，方法实现均绝对百分比误差(MAPE) 14.21%，优于25%以上的基线模型，相对预测误差降低43.6%。预测与测量的延迟在平均斯皮尔曼相关性0.961 across training tasks，准确保留了跨设备的性能排序。

Conclusion: 本文展示了本地GPU配置可作为目标GPU性能的有效替代品，为跨硬件的零-shot延迟预测提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Quantifying execution latency is an important consideration across Machine Learning. Existing latency-prediction approaches typically train ML models on traces collected from workload execution on target hardware. Collection of representative traces across many consumer GPUs is expensive both in organizing the devices and in manpower, causing existing work to mainly focus on a limited set of high-end GPUs available in cloud computing. To offset this cost, we show that a local host GPU can be configured to reproduce the execution performance of less powerful target GPUs. Through selectively modifying device parameters, we obtain locally measured latencies that closely approximate those observed on the target hardware. We reformulate the latency-prediction task: instead of fitting a prediction model to a dataset of execution traces, we identify local configurations that best reproduce target devices' performance, enabling zero-shot prediction. We evaluate our approach against real measurements from 60 computer-vision models executed on 21 consumer GPU models. Our method achieves a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 14.21%, compared with more than 25% for comparable baselines, reducing relative prediction error by 43.6%. Predicted and measured latencies exhibit an average Spearman correlation of 0.961 across training tasks, demonstrating that the method also accurately preserves performance rankings across devices.

</details>


### [11] [Autotuning GPU Thread Block Sizes for Large-Scale Stencil Applications Using the OPS DSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00968)
*Kevin Antonio Nava Garcia,Istvan Zoltan Reguly*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: A transparent per‑kernel block/workgroup‑shape autotuning extension for OPS automatically selects optimal launch configurations, achieving up to 1.5x speedup and improving 923 of 995 test cases across multiple NVIDIA and AMD GPUs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Default or user‑chosen global block/workgroup shapes often do not match the optimal configuration for different kernels, problem geometries, or GPU architectures, limiting performance of long‑running stencil applications on HPC GPUs.

Method: The extension dynamically explores valid block/workgroup shapes during execution, records the fastest configuration for each kernel, and enables pre‑computed replay of these optimal shapes in subsequent runs.

Result: Across 459 kernels on 3 NVIDIA and 2 AMD GPUs, replaying the best per‑kernel shapes improved 923/995 cases, with median speedups of 1.02‑1.18x and several solvers exceeding 1.5x; input problem shape can change runtime by 0.8‑34.6%, and timing variability depends on shape, runtime contribution, and backend behavior.

Conclusion: Automatic per‑kernel block/workgroup‑shape selection enhances OPS performance on GPUs when both shape sensitivity and timing stability are considered together.

Abstract: Large-scale structured-mesh stencil applications can run for hours or days on modern HPC GPU platforms, making launch-configuration choices important. OPS enables high-level stencil codes to target multiple GPU architectures, but its GPU backends rely on a default or user-selected global block/workgroup shape, even though different kernels, problem geometries, and architectures may favor different configurations. This paper presents a transparent per-kernel block/workgroup-shape autotuning extension for OPS. The extension explores valid block/workgroup shapes during execution, records the fastest configuration for each kernel, and enables precomputed replay of the selected configurations in subsequent runs. We evaluate the approach on structured-mesh proxy and academic research applications, covering 459 unique kernels across 3 NVIDIA and 2 AMD GPUs. The evaluation reports speedup distributions, representative wall-time summaries, timing variability, and input problem shape effects. Replaying the best per-kernel launch shapes improves 923 of 995 measured application/input/GPU cases over the default OPS shape. Depending on the GPU, the median speedup ranges from 1.02x to 1.18x, with individual configurations of LAPL2D, TTI3D, and OpenSBLI-based TGV solvers exceeding 1.5x. The results also show that performance sensitivity is not limited to launch shape. At fixed cell count, changing the input problem shape changes precomputed-replay runtime by 0.8-34.6% in representative cases, while kernel-level timing variability depends on the interaction between runtime contribution, input problem shape, and GPU/backend behavior. Overall, automatic per-kernel block/workgroup-shape selection improves OPS performance across GPUs, provided shape sensitivity and timing stability are interpreted together.

</details>


### [12] [Investigating ET-SoC-1 Vector and Parallel Performance with sGEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01045)
*Richard Neale,Nick Brown*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: The Ainekko ET‑SoC‑1, a 2048‑hart RISC‑V SIMD accelerator, was evaluated with single‑precision sGEMM using two kernels to study operand delivery versus arithmetic width, showing high performance relative to CPUs and GPUs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To investigate how operand delivery and arithmetic width affect performance of massively parallel RISC‑V accelerators for dense linear algebra.

Method: Implemented a register‑blocked PS‑SIMD baseline with two‑step software pipelining and a TensorFMA32 kernel using per‑core tensor micro‑operation sequencing and double‑buffered scratchpad; measured performance on the ET‑SoC‑1 and compared against multiple CPU and GPU references.

Result: Both kernels reached similar arithmetic peak, but the TensorFMA32 achieved higher throughput due to reduced operand stalls, outperforming CPU references and matching or exceeding GPU performance on the sGEMM workload.

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that efficient operand delivery is crucial for realizing the compute potential of RISC‑V SIMD accelerators, and that the ET‑SoC‑1 can deliver competitive performance for large‑scale matrix multiplication.

Abstract: The Ainekko (formerly Esperanto) ET-SoC-1 is a massively parallel RISC-V accelerator. Its compute array comprises 1024 dual-threaded in-order ET-Minion cores (2048 harts) operating at 600 MHz, each core providing an eight-lane 256-bit packed-single (PS) SIMD datapath shared by its two harts, with access to LPDDR4X memory at a nominal 133 GiB/s. This paper presents a performance investigation using single-precision general matrix multiplication (sGEMM) as the benchmark kernel. Two device kernels are implemented and evaluated on the ET-SoC-1, these are a register-blocked PS SIMD baseline with two-step software pipelining, and a TensorFMA32 kernel that drives the same FMA datapath through the per-core tensor micro-operation sequencer, with double-buffered A scratchpad loading. Because both kernels are bounded by the same arithmetic peak, the comparison isolates the effect of operand delivery from that of arithmetic width. Performance is compared against CPU references (SpacemiT K3, Intel i5-11600, AMD Ryzen 9 9950X and the Arm Cortex-X925/A725 CPU of an NVIDIA DGX Spark) and two GPUs (NVIDIA GB10 Blackwell and AMD RDNA4 RX 9060 XT).

</details>


### [13] [STLT: A Novel System for Energy-Aware Distributed Inference in the Edge-Cloud-Space Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01233)
*Andrija Stanisic,Milos Gravara,Juan Luis Herrera,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: STLT是一种两阶段系统，用于LEO卫星与地面站之间的能量感知分布式推理，通过离线分析缩小分割点搜索空间，在线轻量级选择动态适应环境，显著降低能耗和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星用于地球观测任务（如野火检测），板载DNN推理可减少下行数据量，但严重的能量约束迫使在计算与传输间权衡。现有静态分割点或复杂预测模型无法适应动态空间环境或引入过大开销。

Method: 两阶段设计：1) 离线分析器表征DNN架构，识别有利分割点集合，压缩搜索空间；2) 在线轻量级算法根据当前环境状态（如能量、信道）从预计算集合中动态选择最优分割点。

Result: 在真实卫星遥测数据集上评估：搜索空间平均减少30.7%，决策延迟降低25.1%，能耗降低27.5%，相比最先进基线完成推理任务数提升20.1%，总能耗降低8.8%。

Conclusion: STLT通过离线预分析与在线轻量决策相结合，有效解决了动态空间环境下卫星-地面协同推理的能量-延迟权衡问题，为资源受限LEO卫星提供了实用的自适应分布式推理方案。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit satellites are pivotal for Earth observation tasks such as wildfire detection, capturing imagery for analysis by Deep Neural Networks (DNNs). On-board analysis reduces downlink volume, but severe energy constraints force a trade-off between computation and data transmission. Splitting the DNN between satellite and ground turns this balance into a design choice, where the split point determines combined computation and downlink energy. Existing approaches typically rely on static split-point decisions that fail to account for dynamic space conditions, or on complex algorithms, historical data, or prediction models that introduce significant overhead. To address these shortcomings, we present STLT, a two-stage system for energy-aware distributed inference between LEO satellites and a ground station. First, an offline analyzer characterizes the DNN architecture to identify a set of favorable split points, consequently reducing the search space. Second, an on-board algorithm performs lightweight energy-aware split selection, dynamically choosing from this pre-computed set based on the current state of the environment. We evaluate STLT on a real-world satellite telemetry dataset, showing that it reduces the split-candidate search space by 30.7% on average, lowers decision latency by 25.1% and energy by 27.5% on average, and delivers up to 20.1% more inference tasks than a state-of-the-art satellite-ground co-inference baseline while consuming 8.8% less energy overall.

</details>


### [14] [Beyond Leaderless: Randomized Consensus Against Mobile Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01323)
*Ittai Abraham,Kaya Alpturer,Pranav Garimidi,Joachim Neu,Tim Roughgarden,Ertem Nusret Tas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Multi-Leader Fixed-View Simplex (ML-FVS) 协议，解决在同步环境下面对移动式崩溃和永久适应性拜占庭故障的共识协议终止问题，通过多个领导者和隐藏阈值信标实现高效的共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共识协议在面对移动式崩溃攻击时难以终止，特别是在同步模型下，攻击者可以移动到不同的副本并反复崩溃其中一个副本，导致协议无法完成共识。

Method: ML-FVS 协议将固定视图 Simplex 扩展为多领导者模型，每个领导者可广播提案。在投票截止时，副本对已见的提案进行投票，隐藏阈值信标在投票后揭示领导者的优先级顺序，从而将每个投票映射到最高优先级的提案。

Result: 在恒定崩溃预算和 Δ 延迟的拜占庭腐化下，获得 O(Δ) 的期望延迟；在立即拜占庭腐化下仍能保持活性。此外，在随机网络延迟和随机故障下，多个领导者有效减少了视图失败，即使理性领导者延迟提案以增加奖励。

Conclusion: ML-FVS 协议在保持 Simplex 共识的简单结构和良好情况下的低延迟的同时，有效防御移动式崩溃攻击，提供了良好的理论保证和实际性能表现。

Abstract: Many consensus protocols fail to terminate under attack from an adversary that has the power to move between replicas and repeatedly crash one of them, even in synchrony. We present Multi-Leader Fixed-View Simplex (ML-FVS) to defend against such an adversary while keeping the simple structure and good-case latency of Simplex consensus. Our protocol extends fixed-view Simplex with multiple leaders, each of which may broadcast a proposal. At the voting deadline, each replica votes on the set of proposals it has seen so far. A hidden threshold beacon revealed after the votes gives a priority order on the multiple leaders, resolving each vote to its highest-priority proposal. Our model combines permanent adaptive Byzantine faults with mobile crashes. For a constant crash budget and $Δ$-delayed Byzantine corruption, we obtain $O(Δ)$ expected latency over repeated views whose leaders start honest. With immediate Byzantine corruptions, the protocol still achieves liveness. We also evaluate the protocol under random network delays and stochastic faults, finding that multiple leaders reduce view failures even when rational leaders delay their proposals to increase rewards.

</details>


### [15] [Layer-wise CPU-GPU Scheduling for LLM Inference on Memory-Limited Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01393)
*Naoto Sugiura,Yuta Tokusashi,Hiroki Matsutani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在解码阶段将部分层放在CPU计算并异步传输下一层权重到GPU的方法，以隐藏权重传输延迟，加速大模型在单GPU消费级设备上的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单GPU环境下，模型权重超出显存容量导致需频繁传输权重，传输延迟占推理时间约98%，成为主要瓶颈。观察到解码阶段CPU计算某层时间可短于权重传输时间，利用这一计算时间隐藏传输可提升效率。

Method: 将CPU/GPU计算与权重传输的调度建模为延迟最小化问题，采用松弛时序约束的近似模型进行优化，实现在解码阶段部分层在CPU上计算的同时异步传输下一层权重到GPU。

Result: 在消费级桌面环境下，OPT-30B推理比HuggingFace Accelerate加速3.5倍，比DeepSpeed Zero-Inference加速6.3倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有效利用CPU计算资源掩盖权重传输开销，显著提升大模型在资源受限单GPU设备上的推理性能，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: As large language model inference environments expand toward consumer devices, it remains challenging to efficiently run models that exceed GPU memory capacity in single-GPU settings. The latency of transferring weights that do not fit in GPU memory accounts for about 98\% of inference time and remains a major obstacle. In this paper, we focus on the fact that during the decode phase, layer computation time on the CPU can be shorter than weight transfer time, presenting an opportunity to hide weight transfers by utilizing this computation time. Therefore, we propose a method to hide transfer latency by executing certain layers on the CPU during the decode phase while asynchronously transferring the next layer's weights to the GPU in the background of that computation. To realize this scheduling, we formulate the placement of CPU/GPU computation and weight transfers as a latency minimization problem and perform optimization using an approximation model with relaxed temporal constraints. Evaluation results confirm that in a consumer desktop environment, our approach accelerates OPT-30B inference by 3.5x compared to HuggingFace Accelerate and 6.3x compared to DeepSpeed Zero-Inference.

</details>


### [16] [CacheWeave: Learning to Recompute for Efficient Position-Independent KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01735)
*Yueyang Pan,Qianchao Zhu,Yuliang Liu,Teng Ma,Sanidhya Kashyap*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheWeave 提出了一种针对位置无关缓存（PIC）的模型原生块选择与高效重计算框架，通过训练紧凑的块选择器，在极低的重计算预算下（5%）实现了接近完整预填充的精度（平均相对精度损失仅 1.6%），并显著降低了首 token 延迟（TTFT），大幅提升了推理服务的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置无关缓存（PIC）虽然能跨位置和请求复用 KV 缓存，但由于独立编码忽略了跨块交互，导致模型精度下降。现有的 PIC 训练或基于规则的重计算方法无法稳定恢复完整精度。虽然 Oracle 引导的重计算能改善精度，但如何高效、准确地选择需要重计算的块仍是一个关键挑战。

Method: CacheWeave 首先将完整骨干网络适配到 PIC 并冻结，然后基于模型自身的 oracle 训练一个原生块选择器，利用紧凑的缓存块特征和问题隐藏状态（无需提取注意力分数）选择对齐 KV 块的连续 token 块。配套的运行时系统 CacheWeave-Serve 支持私有/共享缓存，在 GPU 上完成选择与重计算，并针对混合注意力模型分段管理滑动窗口 KV 缓存。

Result: 在三个模型和四个任务上的实验表明，在 5% 的重计算预算下，CacheWeave 的平均准确率分别比 CacheBlend 和 ProphetKV 高出 7.2 和 3.5 个百分点。在系统性能上，32-token 块配置下，单请求 TTFT 最多缩短 16.1 倍（相比完整预填充）和 1.7 倍（相比 ProphetKV），负载下的 SLO 好吞吐量提升了最多 7.8 倍。

Conclusion: CacheWeave 通过模型原生的块选择机制与高效的系统级优化，成功在保持位置无关缓存高复用率的同时，最小化了精度损失。它在极低计算开销下实现了精度与推理速度的极佳平衡，为高效的大模型推理服务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Position-independent caching reuses chunk KV caches across positions and requests. Independent encoding, however, omits cross-chunk interactions and can degrade accuracy. In our study, neither PIC training nor rule-based recomputation, alone or combined, consistently recovers full-prefill accuracy. Oracle-guided recomputation on PIC-trained models improves accuracy at the same budget, motivating better selection. We present CacheWeave, which adapts the full backbone to PIC, then freezes it and trains a model-native block selector on the adapted model's oracles. The selector uses compact cached chunk features and question hidden states without extracting backbone attention scores. It selects contiguous token blocks aligned with KV blocks to support efficient recomputation and limit copy-on-write amplification. Its runtime, CacheWeave-Serve, supports private and shared KV caches, keeps selection and recomputation on the GPU, and stages sliding-window KV for hybrid attention models. Across three models and four tasks, CacheWeave outperforms CacheBlend and ProphetKV by 7.2 and 3.5 percentage points on average at a 5% recomputation budget, with a mean relative accuracy loss of 1.6% to full prefill. With 32-token blocks, CacheWeave-Serve cuts single-request TTFT by up to 16.1x over full prefill and 1.7x over ProphetKV, and raises SLO goodput under load by up to 7.8x.

</details>


### [17] [Computation in Anonymous Directed Dynamic Networks: Exact Worst-Case Termination Time and Network-Wise Intractability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01761)
*Giuseppe A. Di Luna,Giovanni Viglietta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: We establish the exact worst‑case round complexity for counting agents in anonymous dynamic directed networks with designated leaders, providing tight bounds of n, 2n‑1, or 2n‑2 depending on n and ℓ, with polynomial local computation and intractability of network‑wise optimization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agents lack identifiers, the total population n is unknown, only ℓ leaders are known, and the communication topology changes arbitrarily while staying strongly connected; thus we need a distributed algorithm that determines n with minimal rounds.

Method: A distributed algorithm where each agent solves at most one linear system of up to n variables per round, works with loops and parallel arcs, and halts after the derived number of rounds, guaranteeing that every agent outputs n.

Result: The algorithm achieves worst‑case round bounds of n if n=ℓ, 2n‑1 if n>ℓ and ℓ=1, and 2n‑2 if n>ℓ and ℓ≥2, which are proven tight over all directed multigraphs; local computation is polynomial (solving a linear system per round), while deciding if an agent already has sufficient information is coNP‑complete.

Conclusion: Exact counting with optimal worst‑case rounds is computationally feasible with polynomial local work, but determining network‑wise optimality is intractable under standard complexity assumptions.

Abstract: We determine the exact round complexity of Counting in anonymous directed dynamic networks. Agents have no identifiers, but $\ell\geq1$ of them are designated as leaders. Every agent knows $\ell$ and its own leader status, while the population size $n$ is unknown and has to be determined by the agents. Communication proceeds in synchronous rounds; the directed communication graph may change arbitrarily and unpredictably from one round to the next, but is assumed to remain strongly connected. We solve Counting with a distributed algorithm that makes every agent output $n$ and halt after a number of rounds equal to
  $\begin{cases} n&\text{if }n=\ell\\ 2n-1&\text{if }n>\ell\text{ and }\ell=1\\ 2n-2&\text{if }n>\ell\text{ and }\ell\geq2. \end{cases}$
  The algorithm works even for networks with loops and parallel arcs and even when agents learn their outdegree only after sending. We prove that this bound is worst-case tight over directed multigraphs for every $n$ and $\ell$ with $1\leq\ell\leq n$. Moreover, the local computation is polynomial (provided that each communication multigraph has at most $2^{\operatorname{poly}(n)}$ arcs): in every round, each agent solves at most one linear system with up to $n$ variables. By standard techniques, solving Counting also enables the evaluation of any computable function of the multiset of agents' input values within $2n-1$ communication rounds.
  The bound above optimizes the worst case over all networks. For an individual network, we show that even deciding whether an agent already has enough local information to determine $n$ is $\mathsf{coNP}$-complete (provided that the numbers of parallel arcs are encoded in binary). Thus, worst-case-optimal Counting admits polynomial local computation, whereas exact network-wise optimization is intractable under standard complexity assumptions.

</details>


### [18] [Apto: Self-Adaptive CPU-GPU Hybrid MoE Inference on Shared PCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01859)
*Zihang Zhong,Jiali Wang,Rong Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Apto，一种自适应CPU/GPU混合推理系统，通过动态调度专家执行来应对CPU竞争导致的负载不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合系统假设资源独占，预先划分CPU/GPU任务，当有其他应用竞争CPU时，会导致两条执行路径严重失衡。

Method: Apto使用双端队列，CPU和GPU执行器从队列两端获取专家任务，根据执行进度自适应调整划分；结合亲和力感知调度，将偏好专家放在相应端，并采用可取消尾部竞速减少尾部延迟。

Result: 与仅用GPU和现有混合基线相比，Apto在独立执行下预填充和解码阶段平均加速2.96倍和1.52倍；在并发CPU负载下加速5.97倍和3.45倍；实际编码代理应用加速2.02倍。

Conclusion: Apto通过动态自适应调度有效提升了MoE模型在资源受限PC上的推理性能，具有实用价值。

Abstract: CPU/GPU hybrid inference enables Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models to run on memory-constrained PCs by keeping all expert weights in host memory and executing activated experts across both processors. Existing hybrid systems, however, assume exclusive resource access and determine an upfront CPU/GPU partition, so CPU contention from colocated applications can severely imbalance the two execution paths. This paper presents Apto, a self-adaptive hybrid inference system that applies dynamic scheduling to expert execution. The CPU and GPU executors claim experts from opposite ends of a dual-ended queue as they progress, allowing the CPU/GPU work split to adapt to their execution progress without predicting contention. Apto further uses affinity-aware scheduling to expose each executor's preferred experts at its queue end and cancellable tail racing to mitigate final-expert stragglers. Compared with state-of-the-art GPU-only and hybrid baselines, Apto achieves average prefill and decode speedups of 2.96$\times$ and 1.52$\times$ under standalone execution, and 5.97$\times$ and 3.45$\times$ under concurrent CPU workloads. It also accelerates real-world coding-agent applications by 2.02$\times$ on average.

</details>


### [19] [Rethinking Model Aggregation for Federated Learning with Domain Skew: A Knowledge-Preserving Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01893)
*Haotian Wu,Xu Ji,Qiheng Li,Li Xiong,Zibin Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 联邦学习中域偏移挑战共享模型适应不同视觉分布，FKPL通过两阶段框架结合域专家训练与原型校准，实现特征保存与全局预测器整合，在多数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域偏移导致统一模型难以捕获分布差异，域专家能分别建模这些分布，提供通过整合表示构建统一预测器的机会。

Method: FKPL采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过一-shot子空间聚类对客户端分组进行内聚训练，专家编码器拼接冻结保留特征映射；第二阶段客户端将拼接特征与匹配类别的域原型插值后上传虚拟特征，服务器在这些特征上训练全局线性分类器。

Result: 在Office-Caltech-10、PACS和DomainNet四域子集上分别取得73.01%、67.00%、64.51%的平均准确率。DomainNet上的跨域标准差为7.50个百分点，为所评估方法中最低。原型校准相较于联邦分类训练，在Office-Caltech-10和PACS上平均准确率分别提升9.99和14.94个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，原型校准是将保存的域表示整合到全局预测器中的有效方式。

Abstract: Domain skew challenges federated learning by requiring a shared model to accommodate distinct visual distributions. Domain-specific experts can capture these distributions separately, creating an opportunity to integrate their representations in a unified predictor. We propose Federated Knowledge-Preserving Learning (FKPL), a two-stage framework that combines domain-oriented expert training with prototype-based classifier calibration. First, a one-shot subspace-based clustering procedure groups clients for intra-cluster training. The resulting expert encoders are concatenated and frozen, preserving each learned feature mapping during global integration. Second, clients interpolate their concatenated features with class-matched domain prototypes and upload the resulting virtual features. The server trains a global linear classifier on these features, concentrating classifier optimization at the server after a single feature upload. FKPL achieves average accuracies of 73.01\%, 67.00\%, and 64.51\% on Office-Caltech-10, PACS, and a four-domain subset of DomainNet, respectively. On DomainNet, its cross-domain standard deviation is 7.50 percentage points, the lowest among the evaluated methods. With the same concatenated extractor, prototype-based calibration improves average accuracy over federated classifier training by 9.99 and 14.94 percentage points on Office-Caltech-10 and PACS. These results identify classifier calibration as an effective way to combine preserved domain representations into a global predictor.

</details>


### [20] [DynSL: Resource-Adaptive Model Training across Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03209)
*Boris Radovič,Istemi Ekin Akkus,Marco Canini,Ruichuan Chen,Alice Dethise,Ivica Rimac,Veljko Pejović*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DynSL 是一个动态分割学习框架，通过持续监控系统指标并周期性求解优化问题，在客户端和服务器之间动态分配模型层，从而在异构边缘设备上加速训练同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式训练虽简化流程但引发隐私和合规担忧，全本地训练又可能超出设备算力和内存限制。现有分割学习方法多依赖静态分割或简化假设，难以捕捉真实部署中的异构性和动态变化。

Method: DynSL 框架持续监控系统指标，周期性求解考虑客户端和服务端计算及通信开销的优化问题，当收益为正时重新配置分割点，使卸载策略能自适应设备能力和系统条件的变化。

Result: 在 24 台异构边缘设备上的大量实验表明，DynSL 相比最强自适应基线将训练时间减少高达约 30%，相比次优均匀分割减少超过 50%，且精度无损失甚至有时有所提升。

Conclusion: DynSL 能有效适应不断变化的边缘环境，在不牺牲模型精度的前提下显著降低训练时间，为大规模异构边缘分割学习提供了实用的动态优化方案。

Abstract: Training models over data distributed across edge devices presents a trade-off: centralizing data simplifies training but raises privacy and regulatory concerns, while full local training may exceed the devices' compute and memory capacity. Split learning addresses this challenge by partitioning the model between clients and the server, allowing part of the computation to be offloaded. However, existing approaches typically rely on static split configurations or simplified assumptions that fail to capture the heterogeneity and complex dynamics of real deployments. We present DynSL, a framework that dynamically allocates model layers between clients and the server by continuously monitoring system metrics and periodically solving an optimization problem that accounts for client and server computation and communication. When beneficial, the system reconfigures the split, enabling the offloading strategy to adapt to changing device capabilities and system conditions. Extensive experiments on a testbed of 24 heterogeneous edge devices show that DynSL reduces training time by up to $\sim\!$30% over the strongest adaptive baseline and by more than 50% over a suboptimal uniform split, with no loss -- and sometimes a gain -- in accuracy.

</details>


### [21] [Ascend-SpMM: Accelerating Sparse Matrix-Dense Matrix Multiplication on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01936)
*Jiahe Wang,Yingxia Shao,Shuhan Wang,Qingbo Ge,Hongzheng Li,Yucheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对昇腾NPU上稀疏矩阵-稠密矩阵乘法（SpMM）优化不足的问题，提出了一套适配其物理解耦架构的系统化框架Ascend-SpMM，显著提升了SpMM性能及端到端GNN训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMM是图神经网络和科学计算的核心算子，但在昇腾NPU上的优化研究较少。现代NPU具有物理解耦的Cube/Vector架构和单标量指令调度，带来了独特的硬件优化挑战，亟需专门的框架来提升效率。

Method: 提出内存中心的代价模型，量化硬件亲和性并指导冲突受控、感知密度的任务映射策略：将稠密区域映射到高吞吐Cube通路，将稀疏残差映射到灵活Vector通路，并给出跨处理器冲突的理论上界。进一步开发高度定制的子核，利用层级感知流水线和基于环缓冲区的隐式同步来掩盖内存延迟并减少指令流水线停顿。

Result: 大量实验表明，Ascend-SpMM相对于最强NPU基线在各矩阵上的几何平均加速比为12.70-20.55倍，端到端GNN训练加速最高达6.8倍，且性能与最先进的GPU实现相当。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了昇腾NPU上SpMM优化的空白，验证了针对物理解耦NPU架构进行算子定制的有效性，为类似AI加速器上的稀疏计算优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Sparse Matrix-Dense Matrix Multiplication (SpMM) is a fundamental kernel in graph neural networks and scientific computing, yet its optimization on Ascend NPUs remains largely unexplored. The physically decoupled Cube/Vector architecture and single-threaded scalar instruction dispatch of modern NPUs introduce unique challenges. We present Ascend-SpMM, a novel and systematic framework tailored to the physically decoupled architecture of Ascend NPUs.We introduce a memory-centric cost model that quantifies hardware affinity and guides a conflict-bounded, densityaware task-mapping strategy. The strategy maps dense regions to the high-throughput Cube path and sparse residuals to the flexible Vector path, with a theoretical bound on cross-processor conflicts. To execute these workloads with high efficiency, we further develop highly customized sub-kernels. These kernels leverage hierarchy-aware pipelining and round-robin ring buffer-based implicit synchronization to effectively mask memory latency and reduce instruction pipeline stalls. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Ascend-SpMM achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 12.70-20.55X over the strongest evaluated NPU baseline for each matrix, accelerates end-to-end GNN training by up to 6.8X, and delivers performance competitive with state-of-the-art GPU implementations. The source code of Ascend-SpMM is publicly available at https://github.com/IDMG-Lab/Ascend-SpMM.

</details>


### [22] [Fairway: Load-Aware KV Cache Reuse for LLM Prefill Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02082)
*Hamidreza Alikhani,Nazmul Takbir,Matin Khajavi,Rajat Bhattacharjya,Sangeetha Abdu Jyothi,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Fairway is a prefill scheduler that balances KV cache reuse and fairness: it uses a dual‑lane approach with a Fast lane for high‑reuse requests and a Fair lane for others, adapting to queue length to keep low median TTFT while improving tail latency during overloads.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing scheduling strategies either prioritize KV reuse (lowering median TTFT but starving expensive requests) or use FCFS (fairer tails but higher median). Need a scheduler that maintains both performance and fairness across varying loads without manual tuning.

Method: Design an adaptive reuse‑prioritization scheduler using a virtual dual‑lane queue: a Fast lane that serves requests with the longest KV reuse, and a Fair lane that ensures waiting expensive requests are not starved. The scheduler dynamically shifts allocation based on queue length relative to request prefill times.

Result: Fairway reduces p99 TTFT up to 8.6× over prior prefix‑aware schedulers, matches or beats baseline median TTFT, and during load spikes lowers tail latency to about 6× better than FCFS while preserving median performance.

Conclusion: Fairway successfully balances KV cache reuse benefits with fairness, delivering superior tail latency and stable median performance across diverse workloads and overload conditions.

Abstract: LLM-based applications often employ agentic workflows and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which build long prompts from shared context. When prefixes are shared across requests, serving engines reuse the key-value (KV) cache to skip parts of the prefill computation. Reuse is typically graded: the uncached work of requests in a queue can differ by up to 100x. In this setting, the order of scheduling determines the time-to-first-token (TTFT) for the queued requests. KV reuse-first scheduling, which prioritizes the requests with the longest KV cache hit, gives the lowest median TTFT but starves expensive requests under load spikes; first-come-first-served (FCFS) scheduling bounds the tail but raises the median TTFT. We present Fairway, a prefill scheduler built on adaptive reuse prioritization: it favors KV cache reuse while queues are short, and shifts toward fair allocation as requests wait longer relative to their own prefill time. Fairway employs a virtual dual lane model: a KV reuse-first Fast lane and a Fair lane atop the serving engine's waiting queue that enables speeding up of cheap reuse requests without starving expensive ones. This model automatically adapts to the workload without any per-workload tuning. Fairway lowers the tail TTFT at no cost to the median in transient overload. Across workloads and load levels, Fairway matches or beats the best baseline, with a p99 TTFT up to 8.6x lower than prior prefix-aware schedulers. During load spikes, it keeps the tail of FCFS at a 6x lower median TTFT.

</details>


### [23] [RegionFence: Adaptive Concurrency Control for Blockchain Parallel Transaction Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02164)
*Jiaying Feng,Linpeng Jia,Yi Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RegionFence是一种自适应并发控制机制，通过将交易划分为细粒度的'区域'并在运行时动态选择乐观或悲观策略，结合区块内/跨区块在线学习预测冲突，在保持共识序列等效性的前提下显著提升区块链并行执行吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链并行执行引擎多采用单一乐观(OCC)或悲观(PCC)并发控制策略，导致整个交易需重新执行或延迟；数据库领域的混合/自适应机制因区块固定串行顺序、运行时才知访问集、真实负载差异大等原因难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出三大关键设计：1) 区域(Regions)：将控制粒度从整笔交易细化至仅触及热点键的部分；2) 栅栏(Fences)：运行时动态决定每个区域采用乐观或悲观方式处理冲突；3) 区块内与跨区块在线学习：预测潜在冲突以指导调度。实现于go-ethereum，并在真实以太坊主网区块上评估。

Result: 在64核上较单核执行整体加速9.2倍；跨过去五年不同时期区块，速分别比OCC高2.9-5.4倍、比PCC高3.9-9.4倍。

Conclusion: RegionFence通过细粒度自适应并发控制与在线学习，有效解决了区块链并行执行中的冲突开销问题，在真实负载下实现了大幅性能提升，同时保证与串行共识顺序执行结果一致。

Abstract: Smart contracts have turned blockchains into general platforms, and their throughput is increasingly limited by transaction execution. Parallel execution can relieve this bottleneck. However, most existing engines apply a single policy within a block, typically optimistic (OCC) or pessimistic (PCC) concurrency control, and thus re-execute or delay whole transactions. Although some database studies have proposed hybrid and adaptive mechanisms, these mechanisms cannot be applied directly to blockchains because the block fixes the serial order, accesses emerge only at run time, and real workloads vary widely. We propose RegionFence, an adaptive concurrency control mechanism for blockchain parallel transaction execution, based on three key designs. First, we propose regions to refine the granularity of control from whole transactions to the parts that touch contended keys. Second, we design fences to decide at run time how each region handles a conflict. Third, we introduce intra-block and inter-block online learning to anticipate likely conflicts. At the same time, the system still produces the same result as serial execution in consensus order. We implement RegionFence in go-ethereum and evaluate it on real Ethereum mainnet blocks from different periods of the past five years. Experimental results show that RegionFence achieves an overall speedup of 9.2x over single-core execution on 64 cores and outperforms OCC by 2.9-5.4x and PCC by 3.9-9.4x in speedup across these periods.

</details>


### [24] [Exact Shortest Paths and Maximum Flow in Planar Networks in the CONGEST Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02256)
*Sebastian Senge*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在CONGEST模型下，带非负权的exact单源最短路和有向平面网络中的exact max st-flow均在Õ(D²)回合内解决，下界为Ω(D)。利用arXiv:2606.31192的近似距离标签作为黑盒，在Õ(D)回合内解决exact有向SSSP。这依赖于对虚拟源查询的lemma以及纠正后的discretization（arXiv:2210.16351）。所有Õ(D)以及D·n^{o(1)}的进一步界限均基于这些标签。通过arXiv:2303.00811的reduction，带权SSSP或负环检测在高概率下需D·n^{o(1)}回合。通过加权arXiv:2411.11718的面不相交图，使其距离成为dual距离，将dual网络中的距离问题转化为hop diameter为O(D)的平面网络问题。这使得在有向平面网络中exact max st-flow在min{Õ(D²), D·n^{o(1)}}回合内（高概率）解决，且当s,t在同一面时为Õ(D)回合。无向平面网络中min st-cut和min cut取Õ(D)回合。为此需在s,t处修复arXiv:2608.09500的辅助图，并证明其递归可能漏掉最小割（例如11个顶点网络报告37而实际最小割为15）；每层再进行两次最短路计算使其exact。虚拟源lemma、面不相交图加权和纠正discretization已在Lean 4 with Mathlib中机器核实；其余论证仅在纸上完成，无数值检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在改进平面网络中 shortest path 和流问题的分布式复杂度，在CONGEST模型下从Õ(D²)降至Õ(D)或D·n^{o(1)，并利用最近的distance labels实现exact结果。

Method: 通过虚拟源查询lemma对distance labels的利用，纠正arXiv:2210.16351的discretization，利用face-disjoint graph的weighting将dual距离问题转化为平面网络问题，以及通过arXiv:2303.00811的reduction处理negative weights。所有关键lemma已在Lean 4 with Mathlib中机器核实。

Result: 证实exact directed SSSP在Õ(D)回合内可解；exact max st-flow在有向平面网络中取min{Õ(D²), D·n^{o(1)}}（s,t同一面时为Õ(D)）；无向平面网络中min st-cut/min cut取Õ(D)回合。并发现先前算法的递归可能漏掉最小割，通过每层两次最短路计算修正。

Conclusion: 核心技术（虚拟源lemma、面不相交图加权、纠正discretization）已机器核实，其余论证为纸证，未使用数值检验，为CONGEST模型下平面网络优化提供了完整的exact算法框架。

Abstract: In the CONGEST model, exact single-source shortest paths (SSSP) with nonnegative weights and exact maximum $st$-flow in directed planar networks have both been solved in $\tilde O(D^2)$ rounds, where $D$ is the hop diameter, while the lower bound is $Ω(D)$. Given the approximate distance labels of a recent preprint (arXiv:2606.31192), which we use as a black box, we solve exact directed SSSP in $\tilde O(D)$ rounds. It rests on a lemma that answers queries from a virtual source with the labels, and on a corrected discretization of the reduction of exact to approximate distances (arXiv:2210.16351). All further bounds of the form $\tilde O(D)$ and $D\cdot n^{o(1)}$ use these labels. Through the reduction of arXiv:2303.00811, SSSP with negative weights, or the detection of a negative cycle, takes $D\cdot n^{o(1)}$ rounds with high probability. We weight the face-disjoint graph of arXiv:2411.11718 so that its distances are dual distances, which turns distance problems in the dual into problems in a planar network of hop diameter $O(D)$. This gives exact maximum $st$-flow in directed planar networks in $\min\{\tilde O(D^2),D\cdot n^{o(1)}\}$ rounds with high probability, and in $\tilde O(D)$ rounds with $s,t$ on a common face. In undirected planar networks, the value of a minimum $st$-cut and a minimum cut take $\tilde O(D)$ rounds. For this we repair the auxiliary graphs of arXiv:2608.09500 at $s$ and $t$, and we show that its recursion, as we read it, can miss the minimum cut, also with exact shortest paths: on a network with $11$ vertices it reports $37$ for a minimum cut of $15$. Two more shortest-path computations per level make it exact. The virtual-source lemma, the weighting of the face-disjoint graph and the corrected discretization are machine-checked in Lean 4 with Mathlib; all other arguments are proved on paper only. Numerical checks are not used in any proof.

</details>


### [25] [Time-Aware Urban Flood Routing with Sticky Edge Closure on Forecast Depth: A Hanoi Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02479)
*Mai Anh Tran,Vy Nguyen,Vova Nguyen,Minh Hoa Bui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个实时的洪水路径规划引擎，它根据每15分钟更新一次的2小时水位预报，计算每种车辆类型的路径，以避免在行程中进入在到达时已关闭的道路。与仅检查出发时条件的传统方法相比，新方法虽然在3.95%的行程中会产生略长的路径（增加的 median  travel time 为9.2秒），但避免了7.8%的路径在到达时遭遇水淹。该引擎可直接集成到现有的GraphHopper路由服务器中，供交通部门和导航平台使用，支持按车辆类型设置深度限制和超前裕量等政策参数。然而，由于水位数据来自未经实测验证的水动力学模拟，且降雨数据为重放而非实时预报，因此结果可能存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的洪水路径规划工具通常仅依赖静态的初始路况快照，导致在到达目的地时道路可能已被淹水，影响通勤者和交通管理机构的安全。因此，需要一个时间敏感的路径规划系统，能够动态预测路径在行程期间的变化，避免在到达时进入危险路段。

Method: 研究人员开发了一个基于GraphHopper的生产级路由引擎，它每15分钟更新一次，利用未来两小时的水位预报，根据特定车辆类型的深度限制来标记沿途的"粘性"道路（即一旦关闭则保持关闭状态）。该系统对每个查询计算最短路径，同时保持计算速度足够快以支持实时请求。

Result: 在河内西部一场 recorded storm 的测试中，仅检查出发时条件的路由服务商有7.8%的路径在到达时遭遇水淹。 而使用新方法计算的路径在3.95%的行程中略长（median  travel time 增加了9.2秒），但显著降低了水淹风险。

Conclusion: 时间敏感的水位预报和粘性道路闭塞机制可以显著提高洪水事件下的路径规划安全性，并在合理增加行程时间的情况下避免危险。该引擎可直接用于实际交通管理，支持按车辆类型设置深度限制和超前裕量等政策。 然而，由于水位预测模型尚未经过实测验证，且降雨数据为重放而非实时预报，因此该方法仍存在一定局限性，需要进一步的验证和改进。

Abstract: During urban flash floods, commuters and guiding agencies require awareness towards which roads remain passable when a vehicle actually arrives, rather than relying solely on which links appear open at initial departure. Most existing flood-routing tools apply a single static snapshot to each route; meaning that corridors that appear clear initially can become flooded before arrival. This paper presents a routing engine that ingests a two-hour water forecast, renewed every 15 minutes, and plans each journey around roads that the forecast closes for that specific vehicle class. Under this formulation, any road that closes mid-trip remains closed for that query, which keeps route calculation fast enough for live requests while operating inside a production GraphHopper routing server. Testing on a recorded storm in western Hanoi revealed that a router checking only departure conditions sent 7.8% of routes into roads flooded upon arrival. In comparable trips, the proposed rule produced longer routes in 3.95% of cases, requiring a median of only 9.2 s of additional travel time. Agencies and navigation platforms can employ this engine to schedule trips by vehicle type, allowing operators to establish depth limits and lookahead margins as policy parameters. However, several limitations must be noted, as the water depths originate from a hydrodynamic simulation that was not verified against measured water levels, the rainfall was replayed rather than forecast live, and depth cutoffs reflect policy settings rather than measured physical thresholds.
  Keywords: flood routing, time-dependent shortest path, sticky edge closure, forecast water depth, vehicle depth limit, Hanoi

</details>


### [26] [When Cache Pressure Becomes a Trap: Metastability in Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02543)
*Chengzhi Lu,Boxi Zhou,Yanying Lin,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Prefix-reuse-dependent LLM serving with PD disaggregation can fail metastably after brief surges; cache-population feedback is the sustaining mechanism; Basin, a priority-based scheduling and reservation scheme, recovers goodput by shedding excess demand.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Efficient prefix reuse in LLM agents requires heavy prefill load sharing across engines (PD disaggregated), where a brief surge can disrupt prefix cache reuse. The authors aim to understand the dynamic failure mechanism and design a recovery strategy.

Method: 1) Empirical analysis on SGLang: showed surge-induced metastable failure of PD disaggregation via population-cache feedback; 2) Fluid model identifying conditions for the feedback (cache capacity, tool time, base load); 3) Basin: session-level fixed priority + predicted context reservation against an adaptive admission budget + idle decode memory to extend cache + shedding excess demand. Evaluated on H200 1P3D and simulations.

Result: Removing residual burst sessions restored service within minutes. On H200 1P3D, Basin achieved 1.20–1.70x goodput vs Continuum and 2.35–3.59x vs HiCache alone; 1.20–2.05x in simulations across six surge scenarios.

Conclusion: The vulnerability is population-cache feedback dynamics; Basin's proactive shedding and context reservation successfully maintains or improves goodput under surges.

Abstract: LLM agents repeatedly send a growing conversation to a model, making prefix reuse central to efficient serving. We show that a brief surge can disrupt this reuse and trigger metastable failure in prefill--decode (PD) disaggregated engines: service remains degraded after arrivals return to a load the engine previously sustained. On SGLang, a ten-minute surge reduced goodput by about 88% over the following hour. Removing the residual burst sessions restored service within minutes.
  The sustaining mechanism is population-cache feedback. Slower calls prolong sessions and enlarge their aggregate context, or working set; the resulting cache misses increase recomputation and further delay departures. A fluid model identifies the resource conditions for this feedback and explains how cache capacity, tool time and base load affect vulnerability. Across 36 simulated deployments, it flags all 17 cache-first cases. Controlled simulations hold the post-surge state fixed and show that changing subsequent arrivals alone reverses the recovery outcome. Once the cache collapses, low-reuse departure capacity sets the scale of the load reduction needed for recovery.
  Guided by these dynamics, we build Basin, which gives each session a fixed priority, reserves their predicted peak contexts against an adaptive admission budget, extends the cache with idle decode memory, and sheds excess demand. On real H200 1P3D deployments under surges, including long-context SWE-bench Pro sessions, an arrival-time prototype achieves 1.20--1.70x the goodput of Continuum and 2.35--3.59x that of HiCache alone. In simulation, the predictor-driven policy delivers 1.20--2.05x the strongest baseline's goodput across six surge scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [OctEx: Billion-Scale Vector Search by Precomputed Lattice Neighbor Lists on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02556)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 OctEx 的 GPU 向量搜索系统，通过在 GPU 内存中预计算邻居列表来避免 CAGRA 等算法中的串行多轮近似最近邻搜索。OctEx 使用数百个随机投影数据集副本和分层网格来存储每个点的候选向量，并在固定波数中并行读取。最终结果使用 int8 草图进行初步排名，然后进行一次精确重排序。评估表明，在高召回率下，OctEx 在最多 1 亿个向量的 7 个数据集上比 CAGRA 快 2~46 倍，在 Deep1B 和 SIFT1B（10 亿向量）上的速度提高 1.5~2 倍，并大幅超越其他基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最快的 GPU 索引（如 CAGRA）在实现高召回率时依赖于串行多轮遍历，这限制了查询速度。研究者希望通过利用 GPU 的高带宽内存 (HBM) 存储预计算的邻居信息，在单一步骤中并行读取候选向量，从而加速高召回率的近似最近邻搜索。

Method: OctEx 的设计思想是：(1) 在 GPU 内存中存储数百个随机投影数据集副本；(2) 每个副本将向量四舍五入到规则网格点的最近点，并为每个填充的网格单元存储该单元中心附近的最临近向量列表；(3) 查询同时读取所有副本中对应单元的列表，并在固定波数中提取最佳击中的预计算邻居列表；(4) 使用 int8 草图对所有候选进行初步排序，然后进行一次精确的重排序；(5) 通过 CUDA 图将整个批次搜索分配给服务器上的所有 GPU，并将索引的不同部分根据访问频率分配到 HBM 或主机内存。

Result: 在 recall 0.95‑0.995 的情况下，OctEx 在 7 个数据集（最多 1 亿向量）上比 CAGRA 快 2.0‑46.4 倍，在 Deep1B 和 SIFT1B（10 亿向量）上的速度比 CAGRA 快 1.46‑2.05 倍。OctEx 还比 GGNN、PathWeaver、FlowANN、IVF-PQ 和 IVF-RaBitQ 快 5.4‑343 倍。索引构建时间在 44‑47 分钟内完成。

Conclusion: OctEx 展示了一种新的 GPU 向量搜索方法，通过在 GPU 内存中预计算邻居列表并并行处理，显著提升了高召回率近似最近邻搜索的速度，并在各种大规模数据集上证明了其优越性。

Abstract: Approximate nearest-neighbor search on GPUs serves retrieval-augmented generation, recommendation, and deduplication over datasets of up to billions of vectors. The fastest GPU indexes at high recall, such as CAGRA, walk proximity graphs in chained rounds, each of which waits for the distances of the previous one. High recall therefore costs many dependent rounds per query. Our insight is to spend the HBM of the GPUs on precomputed neighbor lists, so that a query reads its candidates in one parallel step instead of a chain of rounds. This paper turns the above insight into a GPU vector search system, namely OctEx, which holds hundreds of randomly projected copies of the dataset in GPU memory. Every copy rounds its vectors to the nearest points of a regular grid whose cells are nearly round and stores for every populated cell the list of vectors nearest to its center. A query reads the lists of its cells in all copies at once and then, in a fixed number of waves, the precomputed neighbor lists of its best hits. OctEx ranks the union of all hits by an int8 sketch before one exact rerank. OctEx runs the whole search of a batch as CUDA graphs over all GPUs of a server and places every part of its index in HBM or host memory by its access frequency. On four H100 GPUs at recall 0.95 to 0.995, OctEx outperforms CAGRA by 2.0x to 46.4x on seven datasets of up to one hundred million vectors, and by 1.46x to 2.05x on the one billion vectors of Deep1B and SIFT1B, whose indexes it builds in 44 to 47 minutes. OctEx also outperforms GGNN, PathWeaver, FlowANN, and the cuVS inverted file indexes IVF-PQ and IVF-RaBitQ by 5.4x to 343x.

</details>


### [28] [Rose: Efficient Embodied RL through Graph Optimization and GPU Compute Resource Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02705)
*Wen Zeng,Ke Hong,Xu Fu,Zixiao Huang,Zehao Wang,Zhuoran Zhu,Yuanqing Wang,Bo Dai,Zhen Guo,Hao Lin,Chunyang Zhu,Boxun Li,Chao Yu,Quanlu Zhang,Guohao Dai,Zhenhua Zhu,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Rose 是一款面向具身强化学习的运行时感知系统，在保持同步训练语义的前提下，自动实现细粒度 GPU 工作重叠与资源管理，提升训练吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身 RL 中策略推理、仿真和模型训练的 GPU 需求高度异质，现有系统只在粗粒度组件边界重叠，子组件阶段和嵌套循环内仍存在大量低活跃 GPU 窗口；手动利用这些窗口需要繁琐的依赖分析和工作负载专用协调，且跨进程 GPU 分时与 SM 竞争可能抵消重叠收益。

Method: Rose 通过轻量可编程接口暴露执行层级和状态访问，跟踪运行时数据就绪与 GPU 活动，采用两阶段优化：第一阶段由窗口导向图优化器识别低活跃窗口并进行图变换，跨组件与循环层重叠互补工作；第二阶段基于 NVIDIA MPS 的松弛感知资源管理器利用分支同步松弛调整进程级 SM 配额，减轻关键路径干扰；两阶段结合本地剖析与端到端迭代验证，以最小调优开销保证吞吐增益。

Result: 在多种策略、仿真器、RL 算法和 GPU 平台上，Rose 相比最先进系统实现 1.20--1.92× 的训练吞吐提升，并使平均 SM 活跃度提高 23.8--39.3%。

Conclusion: Rose 表明自动细粒度重叠与 MPS 基于的 SM 配额管理能有效提升具身 RL 训练效率，并具有较好的跨平台迁移性与低调优开销。

Abstract: Embodied reinforcement learning (RL) coordinates policy inference, simulation, and model training with highly heterogeneous resource demands. Although individual components frequently underutilize GPU compute during execution, existing systems only overlap work at coarse component boundaries (e.g., pipelining inference and simulation across simulator groups), leaving substantial low-activity GPU windows within subcomponent phases and nested loops. Exploiting these fine-grained windows manually requires tedious dependency analysis and workload-specific coordination. Furthermore, even when independent work is scheduled concurrently, cross-process GPU time slicing and streaming multiprocessor (SM) contention can negate the benefits of overlap.
  We present Rose, a runtime-aware system that automates fine-grained overlap and GPU resource management for embodied RL while preserving synchronous training semantics. Through a lightweight programmable interface that exposes execution hierarchy and state accesses, Rose tracks runtime data readiness and GPU activity to perform a two-stage optimization. First, a window-guided graph optimizer identifies low-activity windows and applies graph transformations to overlap complementary work across components and loop levels. Second, leveraging NVIDIA Multi-Process Service (MPS) for cross-process GPU concurrency, a slack-aware resource manager tunes process-level SM quotas by exploiting branch synchronization slack to mitigate interference on the critical path. Both stages combine local profiling with end-to-end iteration validation to guarantee throughput gains with minimal tuning overhead. Across diverse policies, simulators, RL algorithms, and GPU platforms, Rose achieves 1.20--1.92$\times$ training throughput improvement and increases average SM activity by 23.8--39.3\% compared with the state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [29] [Dispatch Control for Priority-Based, Multi-Tenant Workflows under Multi-Resource External Rate Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02926)
*Stanislav Kosorin,Trever Schirmer,Leonard Wossnig,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Backpressure是一个Worker端的 admission gate，通过原子性地检查每个请求的代价并对共享 admission state 进行公平调度，当调用被拒绝时释放 Worker slot 并等待耐久计时器，避免等待阻塞执行容量。在三个优先级类别的工作负载下，公平性（Jain's index）从 0.60 提升至 0.89，高优先级尾延迟缩减约 8 倍。当 Token 成为绑定限制时，所有基于请求的 baseline 优先级均等，而 Backpressure 保持优先级顺序并实现近似 0.99 的 dominant-resource fairness。 admission 开销仅一次共享状态往返，未被绑定时无可测量延迟，且在 shared state unavailable 时 fail open。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Provider 的 API 每个组织的 Cap 很低，且独立绑定在 requests 与 tokens 每分钟上。运营方无法重新配置上限，也无法在请求失败前观测到饱和情况，因此经典的 fair-queuing 假设不再成立。现有的 rate-limit throttling 会阻塞 worker slot，导致其他 tenant 袥REFIX。

Method: Backpressure 作为一个 worker-side admission gate，原子性地检查每个调用的 cost 对共享 admission state 进行检查，并基于请求和 tokens 的公平性进行调度。被拒绝的调用会释放其 worker slot 并等待一个 durable timer，从而不再阻塞执行容量。

Result: 与 naive retry、LiteLLM 以及 Temporal 内置的 throttle 相比，在三个优先级类别的工作负载下，Jain's index 从 0.60 提升至 0.89；高优先级尾延迟缩减约 8 倍。当 tokens 是 binding limit 时，所有 request-based baseline 优先级均等，而 Backpressure 保持优先级顺序并达到 ~0.99 的 dominant-resource fairness。 admission 开销仅一次共享状态往返，未被绑定时无可测量延迟，且 shared state unavailable 时 fail open。

Conclusion: Backpressure 在共享 LLM rate cap 下有效实现了优先级感知的公平调度，开销极低且在未被绑定时无延迟影响，是一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Workflow engines orchestrate pipelines of activities, which often call external APIs, on a fixed pool of worker slots, the threads that run them. When an API enforces a hard rate limit shared by all tenants and priorities, a throttled call that retries after a backoff keeps its worker slot and starves other tenants. Large Language Model provider APIs are particularly complex: their per-organization caps are low and bind independently on requests and tokens per minute. The operator can neither reconfigure the cap nor observe saturation before a request fails, so classical fair-queuing assumptions do not hold. We present Backpressure, a worker-side admission gate. It checks each call's cost atomically against shared admission state and enforces fairness over requests and tokens. A denied call releases its worker slot and waits on a durable timer, so waiting no longer blocks execution capacity. We compare against a naive retry, the AI gateway LiteLLM, and the built-in throttle of the workflow engine Temporal. On a workload with three priority classes, Backpressure raises fairness (Jain's index over each queue's fraction of its entitled share) from 0.60 to 0.89, where 1.0 is ideal. It also cuts high-priority tail latency roughly 8x. When tokens are the binding limit, all request-based baselines serve every priority equally, whereas Backpressure keeps the priority order and reaches a dominant-resource fairness of ~0.99. Admission costs one round trip to shared state, adds no measurable latency when the cap does not bind, and fails open if shared state is unavailable.

</details>


### [30] [Introducing Cluster Efficiency to LLM Serving: Pooling Economical Accelerators over Fast Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02975)
*Youhe Jiang,Pavle Glušac,Binhang Yuan,Eiko Yoneki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 快速互连使经济型加速器在长上下文大模型服务中实现成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要评估集群效率（每美元/每焦耳的 token）并证明低成本加速器通过高带宽互连可保持成本优势。

Method: 基于代码代理工作负载和加速器扩展特性，在 Tenstorrent Galaxy 集群（Blackhole 芯片）上实现集成通信引擎、环形传输、计算通信重叠和设备端解码回放，并与八卡 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 服务器在三年获取及电费成本模型下进行对比；同时模拟 TCP 级通信评估其对吞吐的影响。

Result: Galaxy 在 BF16 和 8-bit 三种密集模型、8K‑256K token 上下文中，实现 1.07‑2.24× 的每美元 token 效率，而 TCP 模拟导致吞吐下降 23.5‑67.4% 并消除成本优势。

Conclusion: 高速互连将经济型加速器的人均资源优势转化为长上下文大模型的成本高效服务。

Abstract: Long-context large language model (LLM) serving places substantial demands on compute, memory capacity, and memory bandwidth. Economical accelerators can offer these resources at lower cost, but their limited per-device capacity requires pooling across devices, making communication overhead critical. We study cluster efficiency, measured in tokens per dollar and per joule, and show how low-latency, high-bandwidth interconnects enable economical accelerators to retain their cost advantage. Guided by a characterization of coding-agent workloads and accelerator scaling, we realize this design on the Tenstorrent Galaxy, a cluster of Blackhole chips. The serving stack combines integrated communication engines, pipelined ring transfers, communication-computation overlap, and device-side decode replay to reduce collective overhead. Across three dense models in BF16 and 8-bit configurations with 8K-256K-token contexts, the Galaxy achieves 1.07-2.24$\times$ the tokens per dollar of an eight-GPU RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell server under a three-year acquisition-and-electricity cost model. Emulating TCP-class communication on the same chips reduces throughput by 23.5-67.4% and eliminates the cost advantage in every evaluated configuration. These results demonstrate how fast interconnects translate the per-dollar resource advantages of economical accelerators into cost-efficient long-context LLM serving.

</details>


### [31] [Same Function, Different CPU: Quantifying Hardware Heterogeneity in Commercial FaaS Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02990)
*Jonas Heisterberg,Natalie Carl,Trever Schirmer,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了跨四个商业FaaS平台（AWS Lambda、Google Cloud Run Functions、Azure Functions、Alibaba Function Compute）上硬件异质性对函数性能的影响。通过方差分解，发现CPU异质性能解释4%至97%的性能方差，在不同平台间差异显著。AWS Lambda上CPU组成每周漂移，且SHA-NI指令集可用性加剧性能差距；Azure Functions存在平台级二态执行机制掩盖CPU效应；慢速CPU调用成本可达快速CPU的三倍，转化为直接成本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究揭示用户无法感知的FaaS硬件'彩票'效应及其对性能和成本的影响，旨在评估FaaS平台异构性对用户体验的经济影响，并为开发者提供选择建议。

Method: 对四个主流FaaS平台进行跨区域、配置和时间点的性能测量。采用方差分解方法隔离CPU异质性对性能方差贡献度。通过实验观察CPU类型组成变化及指令集扩展（如SHA-NI）可用性差异的影响。

Result: AWS Lambda CPU异质性解释4%-97%性能方差，阿里云因SHA-NI缺失拉大差距，AzureFunctions平台级机制产生二态分布掩盖CPU效应。单周CPU漂移和指令集扩展可用性是不同时空因素，加上计费按时长，慢速CPU成本最高达快速的3倍。

Conclusion: FaaS平台的硬件异质性显著影响性能方差和用户成本，建议在相同延迟要求下优先选择不同区域/时间位置以获取更稳定且有利的性能表现。

Abstract: Serverless computing, particularly Function-as-a-Service (FaaS), abstracts operational concerns such as scaling, scheduling, and resource management from users. This abstraction, however, conceals a heterogeneous hardware fleet on which function execution environments are scheduled behind a unified function interface. Consequently, otherwise identical invocations may run on different CPU types that differ in performance, a hardware lottery that users can neither see nor control. Since the resulting variability is entangled with other sources, such as noisy neighbors or diurnal effects, its contribution to overall performance variability and how this contribution differs across providers remain unclear.
  In this paper, we systematically study hardware heterogeneity and its impact on function performance across four widely used commercial FaaS platforms, covering regional, configurational, and temporal differences. Using variance decomposition, we find that CPU heterogeneity alone explains 4 % to 97 % of the performance variance on AWS Lambda and Google Cloud Run Functions. On Azure Functions, in contrast, we identify a platform-level mechanism that produces bimodal execution durations and overshadows CPU effects. We observe that the CPU composition on AWS Lambda shifts within a single week. Additionally, we reveal that on Google Cloud Run Functions and Alibaba Function Compute, the availability of instruction set extensions such as SHA-NI differs between CPU types, widening the performance gap further. Since all platforms bill by duration, performance gaps between CPU types translate directly into cost, with invocations on the slowest CPU type costing up to three times as much as those on the fastest.

</details>
