We present AgenticMobility, an open-source experimental platform for studying how mobility AI agents and other AI agents interact, adapt through repeated experience, and collectively shape mobility outcomes. Persistent agents retain identities and relevant states across rounds, with explicit interfaces defining information access, admissible actions, and decision schedules; linked records trace decisions, execution, and feedback.
  In the current implementation, large language model (LLM) agents act as travelers choosing routes and departure times and as a manager periodically adjusting link charges within MATSim. A preliminary demonstration spans 100 simulated days in each of five configurations, with one run per configuration and a shared simulation seed. In the joint traveler--manager run, all 1,179,300 finalized target decisions satisfied action constraints; execution checks verified implementation of accepted choices and preservation of personal memories across days. Trajectories show stable route choices alongside departure-time adjustments and differences between controlled-trip and population-wide outcomes.
  The platform supports experiments on individual adaptation, collective outcomes, and management interventions by varying information access, retained experience, and management feedback. Source code is available at https://github.com/Agentic-City-Research/Agentic-Mobility

</details>


### [64] [Towards a Theory for the Design of Rules in Multi-Agent Systems: the case of Homogeneous Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02260)
*Matteo Penlington,Lorenzo Fiaschi,Raj Kiriti Velicheti,Andrea Censi,Emilio Frazzoli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出系统无关的规则设计理论，解决多剂分散代理系统的规则设计难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多剂交互的分散代理系统规则设计长期未被系统性解决，现有依赖具体规则，缺乏通用理论。

Method: 定义规则设计问题(RDP)并引入同质规则设计问题(HRDP)，提出Rule Branch and Bound (RuleBnB)算法。

Result: 在自动驾驶案例中展示了HRDP求解的实际价值，证明方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 本工作提供系统无关的规则设计框架，为复杂多剂代理系统的规则制定提供有效手段。

Abstract: The regulation of systems composed of many interacting decentralised agents is a long-standing problem. This problem has typically been resolved by the imposition of rules. Rules resolve the limitations of sub-optimal agents whilst simultaneously aligning the behaviour of the agents with the demands of society. Traffic laws, for example, let a driver cross a junction without seeing around the corner, and transport protocols such as TCP let applications share a network without knowing of one another. The problem is more pressing today, as autonomous systems are deployed in society. In this work, we establish the basis of a system-agnostic theory of rule design for multi-agent systems. We first state the rule design problem (RDP) in its generality. It relates a rule to the set of system performances, defined by the regulator, that the agents deployed under it can attain. However, it is not yet possible to solve the RDP in full generality. As such, we introduce the homogeneous rule design problem (HRDP), in which the agents in each environment are identical, and among other interesting properties, show that under worst-case aggregation the two problems have the same solution, bypassing the combinatorial complexity of the RDP. To solve the RDP under worst-case aggregation we propose Rule Branch and Bound (RuleBnB), an anytime branch-and-bound algorithm. Finally, we illustrate the value of solving the RDP on an autonomous driving case study.

</details>


### [65] [Scientific Liquidity Agents: An Agent-Based Model of Knowledge Arbitrage in Scientific Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02539)
*Koutian Wu,You Liu,Yue Liu,Zhiping Li,Ergan Shang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出科学流动性代理（SLA）模型，通过基于代理的方法模拟科学知识在不同研究情境间的流动与复用，研究知识套利过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学知识在迁移中获得新用途，理解这一过程需要兼顾专业知识生产与连接知识的中介活动，现有研究缺乏将研究者角色与知识转移摩擦显式化的过程导向模型。

Method: 构建基于代理的模型SLA，包含领域专家与认识论套利者；采用信念-欲望-意图架构、基于先决条件的问题图、动态交互网络与持久出版登记表；开展10代理和18代理的两次探索性仿真。

Result: 两次仿真分别生成68篇和274篇登记论文；小型运行中套利者参与26个接触对中的16个，使社区连成一个分量，而仅凭主题相似性会分裂为七个分量；产出丰富但未记录任何成功复制，验证成为瓶颈；已解决问题集与各学科在社区中的先决条件可达性对应。

Conclusion: SLA将知识流动性（跨领域知识工艺品的移动与复用）转化为可由代理科学模拟与度量的量，为科学生产与知识交换提供过程导向的解释框架。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge gains new uses when ideas, methods, and results move between research contexts. Understanding this process requires a model of both specialized knowledge production and the intermediary activities that connect it. We introduce Scientific Liquidity Agents (SLA), an agent-based model of knowledge arbitrage in scientific communities. SLA represents domain specialists and epistemic arbitrageurs within a shared environment of research problems, researcher interactions, and scientific artifacts. Individual decisions connect to collective state through a belief-desire-intention architecture, a prerequisite-based problem graph, a dynamic interaction network, and a persistent publication registry. The framework makes researcher roles and knowledge-transfer frictions explicit, providing a process-oriented account of scientific production and exchange. Two exploratory simulations with 10 and 18 agents produce 68 and 274 registered papers. In the smaller run, arbitrageurs take part in 16 of the 26 contact pairs that join this community into one connected component, while topical similarity alone splits it into seven. The same run yields three similarity edges, 26 contact pairs, and two coauthorship pairs, so measured community structure depends on what counts as a link. Paper production is abundant, but no recorded replication succeeds, which places the bottleneck in verification rather than production. Solved-problem sets correspond to the prerequisite reachability of the disciplines represented in each community. SLA makes knowledge liquidity, the movement and reuse of artifacts across domains, a quantity that agentic science can simulate and measure.

</details>


### [66] [Organizational Learning in Materials Discovery Campaigns: Which Databases, Calculations, and Agent Teams Pay Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02571)
*Kinston Ackölf,Taylor D. Sparks*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了数据库合并对材料研究活动的影响，发现过多数据库可能反而有害，建议精选少量来源以提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示“数据越多越好”的误区，探索如何有效整合多源数据以提升材料筛选效率。

Method: 通过组织视角追踪各数据源贡献，结合带隙排名案例和自适应团队实验，评估不同策略。

Result: 结果表明，全量合并排名不如精选子集；自适应团队在初期能找到更多低能化合物；成员排序一致时合并效果更佳。

Conclusion: 结论是应合并少数贴合测量的来源，每个数据管道单独计数，并合理计算成本。

Abstract: More databases can make a materials campaign worse. Through an organizational lens, we trace each source's contribution. One band-gap campaign ranks seven anion families from archived measured gaps, one vote per source. In the first half of its budget, all seven pooled rank worse (rank agreement 0.737; 1 = measured order) than the best pool of three (0.851). As a stability check, choosing the pool on half the runs and testing on the rest kept the gap. Splitting Materials Project afterwards points to its transition-metal oxides and fluorides. Two databases from one pipeline add nothing over one, but pooling gives their shared ordering two votes. New calculations repay their cost early only when two or three campaigns reuse them. Flat language-model agent teams trailed a random-pick baseline (mean 0.876, sd 0.096, 20 searches). An adaptive Al-Mg-Si team, mostly interatomic potentials, was scored on unseen HSE06 calculations. In its first quarter of compute it found 49% of the ten lowest-formation-energy compounds, against at most 40% for fixed teams and 73% for MACE-MP-0 alone. Merging members whose orderings agreed carried most of its gain. This suggests pooling a few sources that fit the measurements, counting each pipeline once and charging for computation.

</details>


### [67] [Coati: Host-Grounded Execution for Installable Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02644)
*Yudou Ying,Pu Shi,Hanlei Shi,Yuhua Zheng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Coati，一款作为Android应用安装在用户手机上的开源GUI智能体。它通过在手机宿主端记录和验证操作效果、结果屏幕及步骤进度，结合VerifyLoop验证机制、AppMemory应用级程序记忆和路由快速执行器，在116个AndroidWorld任务上实现了86.5%的平均成功率，并显著缩短了任务执行时间、减少了模型token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的移动GUI智能体多运行在通过ADB连接的独立电脑上，普通用户难以直接使用。本研究旨在将智能体直接部署为手机端应用，利用手机宿主本地的直观证据（如操作实际效果、界面变化）来克服纯模型自我评估的局限性，从而提升智能体在日常真实应用中的可靠性和执行效率。

Method: Coati作为Android应用在手机本地运行，核心方法包括：(1) VerifyLoop：对照主机实际证据（操作效果和结果屏幕）检查操作结果和完成声明，将无法审计的完成标记为未验证；(2) AppMemory：基于界面观察构建每个应用的程序性记忆，并在后续访问中复用，简化重复深层路径；(3) 路由快速执行器：让轻量级选择模型执行简单、高置信度的步骤，并由主机重复执行大型模型规划一次的循环步骤，实现高效的任务路由与执行。

Result: 实验表明，以Gemini 3.8 Flash为骨干，Coati在AndroidWorld任务上的三轮成功率达86.5%，大幅超越Artemis（77.6%）等对比智能体。VerifyLoop有效减少了约三分之一的虚假完成声明。快速执行器将任务中位时间缩短39%，使四个循环任务中的三个提速2.8-5.4倍并减少6-9倍的模型token。AppMemory将陌生应用的重复深层路径从18.5步缩减至6-7步。在Android 16上，路由执行完成了所有27次基准运行，关键路径上的推理调用减少了76%。

Conclusion: 宿主端的本地证据（而非模型的自我评估）是构建可靠、高效且可直接安装在用户手机上的GUI智能体的核心。Coati通过端侧验证、记忆复用和任务路由，证明了本地化、以用户为中心的智能体架构能显著提升移动自动化任务的执行速度和可信度。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents can carry out tasks across apps, yet most run on a separate computer that drives a phone over the Android Debug Bridge (ADB), out of reach of ordinary users and of the apps through which most people reach Web services. We present Coati, a source-available agent that users install as an Android app. Its runtime on the phone, the host, records what a model alone cannot see: the effect of each action at its target, result screens across apps, and the progress of repeated steps. VerifyLoop checks action outcomes and completion claims against host evidence and reports a completion it cannot audit as unverified. AppMemory builds per-app procedural memory from observations that the interface supports and reuses it on later visits. A routed fast executor lets a lightweight selection model take simple, confident steps and lets the host repeat loops of simple steps that the large model plans once. On all 116 AndroidWorld tasks, with Gemini 3.8 Flash as every agent's backbone, Coati succeeds on 86.5% of the tasks over three trials, ahead of the strongest open-source agent Artemis (77.6% with the same accessibility-only access, 76.5% even when allowed ADB) and far ahead of FinalRun and Midscene (about 59%), and VerifyLoop cuts false completion claims by about a third at comparable success. In everyday use, the fast executor cuts the median time of phone tasks by 39% at comparable success and runs three of four loop tasks 2.8-5.4x faster with 6-9x fewer model tokens, while AppMemory shortens a repeated deep route in an unfamiliar app from 18.5 steps to 6-7. On an Android 16 phone, routed execution completes all 27 benchmark runs with 76% fewer Reasoning calls on the critical path. Host evidence, not model self-assessment, makes an installable agent reliable and fast.

</details>


### [68] [Multi-Modal Multi-Agent System for Bridge Operation and Maintenance based on Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02738)
*Xiangyun Luo,Yunkai Zhang,Yong Huang,Stephen Wu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种知识增强的自主形式化方法，并构建了桥梁运维多模态多智能体系统（BOMMA），旨在解决桥梁运维中资源异构、缺乏物理力学规律关联以及专家隐性知识难以形式化等关键瓶颈，实现了可解释、可追溯的智能调度与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁运维对交通安全和结构长期性能至关重要，但当前面临三大瓶颈：缺乏统一架构协同异构资源；模型推理缺乏与物理力学规律的明确链接；以及专家的隐性知识难以形式化和复用。

Method: 提出了知识增强的自主形式化方法，构建了BOMMA系统。该系统在协同多智能体工作流中整合了异构诊断动作、物理力学信息表征、长期记忆以及结构化经验知识，实现了可解释、可追溯的调度、分析与决策。

Result: 在3,492条多源指令上进行测试，简单任务和复合任务的调度召回率分别达到85.37%和99.40%，运维动作推理的BERT F1-score达到95.35%。消融实验证实了各组件的有效贡献。

Conclusion: 本研究弥合了通用大语言模型推理与专业桥梁运维需求之间的鸿沟，为构建高效、可信的桥梁智能运维系统提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Bridges are critical transportation infrastructure, and reliable operation and maintenance (O&M) is essential for traffic safety and long-term structural performance. However, bridge O&M faces key bottlenecks: no unified architecture coordinates heterogeneous resources; model inferences lack clear links to physical-mechanical laws; and experts' tacit knowledge remains difficult to formalize. This study proposes a knowledge-enhanced autonomy formalization methodology and apply it to construct the Bridge O&M Multi-Modal Multi-Agent System (BOMMA), which integrates heterogeneous diagnostic actions, physics-mechanics-informed representations, long-term memory, and structured experiential knowledge within a coordinated multi-agent workflow, enabling interpretable and traceable scheduling, analysis, and decision-making. On 3,492 multi-source instructions, the proposed method achieves scheduling recalls of 85.37% for simple and 99.40% for composite tasks, with 95.35% BERT F1-score for O&M action reasoning. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of the components in the autonomy. This study bridges general LLM reasoning and specialized bridge O&M requirements.

</details>


### [69] [AGiR: Mitigating Gift Over-Reliance in Mixed-Motive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02875)
*Woohyeon Byeon,Seongmin Kim,Jiwon Jeon,Woojun Kim,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对混合动机游戏（MMGs）中现有馈赠方法导致的“馈赠过度依赖”问题，提出了一种允许代理自适应拒绝部分接收馈赠的增强框架，以平衡合作与竞争，降低过度依赖并提升α公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有馈赠方法容易使代理过度依赖输入馈赠，忽略获取奖励的行为，甚至被其他代理利用，导致陷入牺牲型策略锁定，损害系统公平性。

Method: 提出一种与馈赠方法无关的拒绝增强馈赠框架，引入自适应拒绝机制，允许代理在策略更新信号中选择性拒绝部分接收的馈赠，支持去中心化学习，可作为插件接入现有训练流程。

Result: 在多种MMG环境和馈赠方案中，该机制持续降低馈赠过度依赖，防止牺牲策略锁定，并提升α-公平性，且无需大幅修改现有训练流程。

Conclusion: 自适应拒绝机制能有效平衡MMGs中的合作与竞争，为现有馈赠方案提供轻量化、去中心化的改进路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider mixed-motive games (MMGs) which model various real-world multi-agent scenarios where self-interested agents must both cooperate and compete. We identify an issue with existing gifting methods, where agents become overly dependent on incoming gifts and fail to learn reward-yielding behaviors, a phenomenon we call gift over-reliance. To address this, we propose a refusal-augmented gifting framework that balances cooperation and competition in MMGs. Our approach introduces an adaptive refusal mechanism that allows agents to selectively reject a portion of received gifts from their policy-update signals, thereby mitigating gift over-reliance and preventing the agents from being exploited by other agents. This mechanism is gifting-method-agnostic, serving as a plug-in for existing schemes while enabling decentralized learning. We demonstrate that across diverse MMG environments and gifting schemes, our mechanism consistently reduces gift over-reliance and prevents lock-in to sacrificing policies, improving $α$-fairness as a result, without necessitating major changes to existing training pipelines.

</details>


### [70] [Cloudra: Geometric Reasoning and Scientific Validation for Autonomous Reaction Mechanism Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02909)
*Chengyi Yang,Qianxin Zhou,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Cloudra是一个多智能体框架，通过Delivery DAG约束化学对象，实现自主反应机制探索，在CloudraBench上表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM智能体在自主反应机制探索中空间关系误判、混淆计算结构与目标物种/步骤的问题。

Method: 提出Cloudra多智能体框架，利用Delivery DAG管理化学对象（物种节点携带几何和键合拓扑，元素步边携带过渡态和反应连接），结合化学信息学算法建立分子身份和原子对应，确定性例程测量和转换结构，频率和IRC计算确定定点和连接物种，智能体利用这些对象测试步骤、比较路径、修订机制。

Result: 在50个任务的200次运行中，Guided工作流平均综合得分0.805，Standard基线0.584；Hard-tier任务Guided均分从0.689提升至0.958；自一致性组件在40个非不可能任务中Guided达24次满分，Standard仅7次。

Conclusion: Cloudra通过结构化化学对象和确定性计算约束LLM智能体，显著提升了自主反应机制探索的准确性和自一致性，尤其在困难任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Autonomous reaction-mechanism exploration requires proposing reaction steps, preparing three-dimensional structures, and determining which reactant and product a computed saddle point connects. Large language model (LLM) agents can misjudge spatial relationships and confuse a computed structure with the species or step they intended to study.
  We present Cloudra, a multi-agent framework that constrains mechanistic output through a Delivery DAG of chemical objects. Species nodes carry geometries and bonding topology; elementary-step edges carry transition states and reaction connections between species. Cheminformatics algorithms establish molecular identities and atom correspondences. Deterministic routines measure and transform structures to prepare candidate geometries. Frequency and IRC calculations determine the stationary points found and the species they connect. Agents use these objects to test proposed steps, compare pathways, and revise the mechanism.
  CloudraBench evaluates the framework's end-to-end mechanism-calculation capability across 50 tasks covering elementary reactions, multistep mechanisms, stereochemical competition, and computational method limitations. Across 200 runs under four workflow configurations, the primary Guided workflow achieves a mean composite score of 0.805, compared with 0.584 for the same-model Standard baseline. On Hard-tier tasks, the Guided mean increases from 0.689 under Standard to 0.958. Under Guided, the self-consistency component reaches its maximum score in 24 of the 40 non-Impossible tasks, compared with seven under Standard. Behavioral analyses examine how the agents use bond and dihedral measurements, optimized structures, and IRC endpoints to revise local mechanistic assignments.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [DIMWIT: Directly Invoking Models Without Intensive Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01463)
*Saarth Deshpande,Neeraja J. Yadwadkar,Venkat Arun*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 绕过任务特定策略的开发，直接利用预训练模型根据当前系统状态、期望结果和可用选择进行资源管理决策。实验在微服务自调、DRAM尺寸设置和视频编码三个场景下进行，小型决策模型在满足延迟目标的同时，分配的副本数量仅比基于策略的自调器多10%，比前沿LLM多13%；DRAM尺寸设置中，相较于静态供给，DRAM使用量最多降低27.5%，且保持至少95%基线性能；视频编码中，编码速度比前沿LLM快最高4.3倍，VMAF质量指标仅下降1.1个不可察感分数。个体资源管理决策可能比开发通用策略简单得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资源管理者需要在使用前开发特定任务的策略（启发式、优化过程或任务训练模型），且随着工作负载或系统变化可能需要修订。本文提出绕过策略开发，直接利用预训练模型根据系统状态做出资源管理决策。

Method: 在微服务自调、DRAM尺寸设置和视频编码任务上进行评估，使用前沿LLM和两个小型决策模型。通过让模型在给定当前系统状态、期望结果和可用选择的情况下直接做出决策。

Result: 实验结果显示：在两个微服务应用上，小型决策模型满足延迟目标，其分配的副本数量在10%以内的误差范围内接近基于策略的自调器，在13%以内接近前沿LLM。在DRAM尺寸设置中，小型决策模型相较于静态供给最多降低27.5%的DRAM使用量，且保持至少95%的基线性能。在视频编码中，编码速度比前沿LLM提升最高4.3倍，VMAF质量指标仅下降1.1个不可察感分数。

Conclusion: 与开发通用策略相比，进行个体资源管理决策可能简单得多。

Abstract: Resource managers typically encode what to do in a task-specific policy---a heuristic, an optimization procedure, or a model trained for the task---that must be developed before use and may need revision as the workload or system changes. We propose bypassing policy development and instead asking pretrained models to make resource-management decisions directly, given the current system state, desired outcome, and available choices. We evaluate this approach on microservice autoscaling, DRAM sizing, and video encoding, using a frontier LLM and two small decision models. On two microservice applications, a small decision model meets latency targets while using within 10% of the replicas allocated by policy-based autoscalers and within 13% of the frontier LLM. In DRAM sizing, a small decision model reduces DRAM usage by up to 27.5% relative to static provisioning while maintaining at least 95% of baseline performance. In video encoding, it completes encodes up to 4.3$\times$ faster than the frontier LLM, with an imperceptible 1.1-point decrease in Video Multi-Method Assessment Fusion (VMAF), a metric of perceptual video quality. Making individual resource-management decisions may be considerably simpler than developing a general strategy for making them.

</details>


### [72] [HPC-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for High Performance Computing Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02752)
*Bowen Cui,Junyu Yin,Tejas Ramesh,Leo Lim,Oscar Hernandez,Keren Zhou*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: HPC-Bench introduces a performance-oriented benchmark for evaluating LLMs as optimizers of computational kernels in real HPC workloads. It includes 114 workloads across 16 motifs and uses an automated pipeline with correctness verification plus Fast@k and Speedup@k metrics. Testing three LLMs on CPU, OpenMP, and CUDA shows that demanding speedup requirements drastically lower the odds of finding both correct and fast optimizations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM benchmarks focus on code generation correctness but ignore the low‑level, memory‑access, and context‑sensitive nature of HPC optimizations. A dedicated benchmark is needed to assess LLMs as performance optimizers in realistic HPC scenarios.

Method: We built HPC-Bench: a fully automated, end‑to‑end evaluation pipeline covering 114 workloads in 16 HPC motifs. It verifies program correctness and reports Fast@k and Speedup@k under a fixed sampling budget. Three representative LLMs were evaluated across serial CPU, OpenMP, and CUDA implementations.

Result: The evaluation reveals substantial limitations: LLMs rarely produce both correct and high‑speed optimizations, and stricter speedup targets further reduce success probability. The benchmark highlights a significant performance gap compared to human‑crafted optimizations.

Conclusion: HPC-Bench provides a standardized, performance‑grounded framework for benchmarking LLMs in HPC optimization. It exposes current shortcomings and offers a clear direction for improving LLM‑based code and performance tuning in high‑performance computing.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code generation and reasoning, motivating extensive benchmarks for evaluating functional correctness. However, performance optimization in High-Performance Computing (HPC) poses fundamentally different challenges that are not captured by existing evaluations: HPC performance depends on low-level code, intricate memory access patterns, and computational kernels embedded within sophisticated execution contexts. We introduce HPC-Bench, a performance-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLMs as optimizers of computational kernels in realistic HPC workloads, including 114 workloads across 16 HPC computational motifs. HPC-Bench provides a fully automated, end-to-end evaluation pipeline that combines program-level correctness verification with two correctness-gated metrics, Fast@k and Speedup@k, to characterize reliability and speedup under a fixed sampling budget. Our evaluation of three representative LLMs across serial CPU, OpenMP, and CUDA settings shows that stricter speedup requirements substantially reduce the probability of finding a correct and fast optimization. HPC-Bench is available at https://github.com/Deep-Learning-Profiling-Tools/HPC-Bench.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Claude for Excel is Transformative for Basic Spreadsheet Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00163)
*Thomas A. Grossman,Min-San Serena Hsu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究利用Claude Excel插件针对五个半结构化问题构建并分析了电子表格模型，评估了其在建模、电子表格工程、分析和洞察生成方面的表现，结果显示Claude的能力非常出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI（特别是结合Excel插件的Claude）在处理半结构化商业/量化问题中的能力，探讨其对分析师生产力和教育模式的影响。

Method: 使用Claude Excel插件针对五个半结构化问题（包括一个多 Exhibit 的教学案例）构建模型，并从建模、电子表格工程、分析和洞察生成四个维度评估其性能。

Result: Claude表现出极其出色的能力，仅需简单的提示即可生成高质量的电子表格模型，并能进行广泛的敏感性和情景分析、生成合理的英文洞察，甚至无需特定提示就能构建优化模型（Solver-aware）。

Conclusion: 分析师的生产力预计将大幅提高。商学院的量化课程以及人类专业知识的培养方式将不得不做出改变。

Abstract: We used the Claude Excel plug-in to build and analyze spreadsheet models for five semi structured problem statements (including a test-piece teaching case with data in multiple exhibits) and evaluated performance on modeling (what is to be coded), spreadsheet engineering (quality of coding), analysis (algorithms applied to the spreadsheet), and insight generation. Claude's ability to create a high-quality spreadsheet model from a semi structured problem statement is astoundingly excellent. Given very simple prompts, Claude generates extensive, accurate sensitivity and scenario analyses and reasonable plain English insights. Claude is Solver-aware and builds optimization models without specific prompting. Analyst productivity gains will likely be high. Business school quant courses will have to change. The way we develop human expertise will have to change.

</details>


### [74] [VerusWeave: A Benchmark for Proof Repair in Concurrent Verus Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00287)
*Pedro Ribeiro,Martim Afonso,André Vasconcelos,João F. Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper introduces VerusWeave, a benchmark for concurrent proof repair in Verus (a Rust formal verification extension). It provides 75 focused tasks and 17 full-module variants from four open-source projects, testing whether coding agents can fill gaps left by removing proof annotations while preserving executable code and specifications. Initial evaluation of four coding agent harnesses showed 75-85% success on focused tasks but only 29-47% on full-module variants, demonstrating significant difficulty in autonomous module-wide repairs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing Verus benchmarks offer limited coverage of concurrency-specific examples connecting atomic operations to abstract protocols. The authors aimed to close this gap by creating a specialized benchmark that focuses on the challenges of repairing concurrent proofs—removing and refilling proof annotations while maintaining code correctness.

Method: VerusWeave is derived from four open-source projects and contains 75 focused tasks and 17 full-module variants. Each task removes selected proof annotations and requires agents to fill these gaps, keeping the executable code and specifications intact. The evaluation harness validates repairs using Verus and a change checker, recording solutions, execution traces, and resource usage. Four general-purpose coding agent harnesses were initially evaluated.

Result: The initial evaluation achieved 75-85% success rate on focused tasks and only 29-47% on full-module variants, highlighting the significant challenge of autonomous module-wide repairs for concurrent code.

Conclusion: VerusWeave serves as a reusable benchmark foundation for evaluating automated proof construction techniques specifically for concurrent software verification.

Abstract: Concurrent software underpins critical infrastructure, including operating systems and databases, yet thread interactions can have bugs that bypass conventional testing. Verus extends Rust with formal verification capabilities, enabling developers to specify, implement and prove the correctness of concurrent code in the same language. Although coding agents have been explored for automated proof construction, existing Verus benchmarks offer limited coverage of concurrency-specific examples that connect atomic operations to abstract protocols.
  To address this gap, we introduce VerusWeave, the first benchmark dedicated to concurrent proof repair, derived from four open-source projects. It comprises 75 focused tasks and 17 full-module variants that exercise features used to verify low-level concurrent code. Tasks remove selected proof annotations and require agents to fill gaps, while preserving executable code and specifications. The evaluation harness validates repairs using Verus and a change checker, recording solutions, execution traces, and resource usage. An initial evaluation of four general-purpose coding-agent harnesses achieved 75-85% success on focused tasks and 29-47% on full-module variants, highlighting the challenge of autonomous module-wide repairs. VerusWeave provides a reusable foundation for evaluating automated proof construction for concurrent software.

</details>


### [75] [Software Change Request in AI-Orchestrated Software Evolution: Literature Review and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00294)
*Muhammad Nadeem,Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Nadeem Majeed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了从移动应用评论中识别软件变更请求（SCR）的方法，探讨机器学习与深度学习在SCR识别与分类中的应用，发现数据集规模从5,000至超过1800万评论不等，主要使用随机森林和支持向量机，深度学习应用有限，F1-score不一致（0.08至1），现有工具如SURF和ChangeAdvisor实现部分自动化，并提出了方法比较分类学、研究缺口及标注框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用商店持续产生海量SCR流，自动化提取与分类对于应对移动应用快速增长至关重要，但面临规模有限、分类方案不一致、数据共享受限及研究不足等挑战。

Method: 本研究进行系统性文献综述，收集并分析相关研究，探究机器学习与深度学习在SCR识别与分类中的应用，涵盖的数据集规模从5,000到超过1800万条评论，并比较了各类模型的性能与现有工具的自动化水平。

Result: 研究发现主要使用随机森林和支持向量机，深度学习技术采用有限，F1-score结果范围从0.08到1，现有SCR工具如SURF和ChangeAdvisor实现了部分自动化。综合后提出了SCR方法的比较分类学，并识别出研究缺口与标注框架的重要性。

Conclusion: 一致的分类方案、共享数据集和标注框架对于推进软件变更请求识别和改进移动应用维护至关重要。

Abstract: In the emerging paradigm of holistic AI-orchestrated software maintenance and evolution, large-scale app stores continuously generate exponentially vast Software Change Requests (SCRs) streams through user feedbacks. These streams enable intelligent software evolution. Automating SCR extraction and classification is crucial with rapid growth of Mobile Apps (MA). However, significant challenges exist such as scare large-scale datasets, inconsistent classification schemes, limited data sharing and insufficient research. This study presents a first and thorough systematic survey of SCR identification from MA Reviews (MAR). In particular, we investigated how Machine Learning (ML) and Deep Learning (DL) enhanced identification and classification of SCR. This study has found pertinent datasets varying sizes from 5,000 to more than 18 million reviews. The key findings highlight the dominant use of Random Forest and Support Vector Machines, limited adoption of deep learning techniques, inconsistent F1-score results ranging from 0.08 to 1 and partial automation in existing SCR tools such as SURF and ChangeAdvisor. Finally, we have performed a synthesis of the studies to presents a comparative taxonomy of SCR methodologies, identify research gaps and annotation frameworks. We contend that classification schemes, shared datasets and annotation frameworks are essential for advancing SCRs and thereby improving the maintenance of mobile applications.

</details>


### [76] [Hardware-Aware Conditional Code in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00296)
*Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Li Li,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Android应用中硬件相关if语句的使用情况进行了大规模实证研究，揭示了其普遍性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅覆盖特定兼容性问题或品牌行为，但应用逻辑中硬件信息的广泛使用尚未被全面探索，因此本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过静态分析50,000个应用，识别依赖硬件API的if语句，并提取其条件和分支代码；随后采用LLM辅助的开放编码过程，构建硬件相关条件代码用途的结构化分类。

Result: 结果显示，95%的成功分析应用至少包含一个此类语句，平均每应用74个，表明硬件相关逻辑非常普遍；用途包括功能可用性检查、UI适配、模拟器检测、设备分层和资源管理等。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了Android应用中硬件信息使用的广泛性，并提供了其用途的分类学，对理解应用行为和优化开发实践具有重要意义。

Abstract: Android OS runs on a highly diverse set of devices, spanning entry-level phones and flagships with substantially different hardware characteristics, including memory capacity, processors, displays, cameras, and sensors. Through APIs, apps can query device information at runtime, such as memory, hardware features, and manufacturer, among other device properties, allowing developers to execute different code paths on different devices. Existing studies cover only specific compatibility bugs or brand-specific behavior; however, the broader use of hardware information in app logic has not been comprehensively explored.
  We address this question with a large-scale empirical study of hardware-related if statements in Android apps. We statically analyze 50,000 apps to identify the if statements whose conditions depend on the values of hardware-related APIs, and extract their conditions together with the code of their branches. Our results show that 95% of successfully analyzed apps contain at least one such statement, with an average of 74 per app, which suggests that hardware-related logic is widespread. We then apply an LLM-assisted open-coding process to the extracted code to build a structured taxonomy of the purposes behind hardware-related conditional code. The resulting characterization shows that developers use hardware information for feature availability checks, UI adaptation, emulator detection, device tiering, and resource management, among others.

</details>


### [77] [Autoformalization for Ambiguous Inputs: Precision, Coverage, and Cost across SQL and Logical Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00340)
*Henry Shen,Xinling Liao,Adarsh Vatsa,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了七种自动形式化工作流在SQL和一阶逻辑基准上的表现，针对模糊输入比较了F1分数和token成本，发现自动形式化本身并不能保证对模糊性的鲁棒性，需要联合设计准确性、模糊处理和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将自然语言请求转化为可执行产物，同时处理模糊性和推理成本，现有方法在模糊输入下表现不一，需要系统评估。

Method: 选取七种自动形式化工作流，在AMBROSIA、AmbiQT、AmP等数据集上进行实验，比较F1分数和token消耗，分析结构化解释、配对评审、清晰/模糊子集等策略的效果。

Result: 原始模型在AMBROSIA上F1为53.35%，AmbiQT为60.85%；DRIFT在AmP上相对原始模型提升15.98 F1点，但token成本为2.03倍；LoC使用18.65倍token但F1更低；AutoCedar中结构化解释使F1下降6.44-6.95点，token使用增加2.2倍；Kimi K3配对评审带来 modest 增益；在清晰-模糊分析中AutoCedar相对稳定，F1下降11.46 vs 16.68，但总体F1较低。

Conclusion: 单纯自动形式化不能保证对模糊性的鲁棒性，所测试的改进没有统一有效方案，准确性、模糊处理和推理成本需联合设计。

Abstract: Agentic systems translate natural-language requests into executable artifacts while managing ambiguity and inference cost. We evaluate seven autoformalization workflows on SQL and first-order logic benchmarks for ambiguous inputs. Across five SQL workflows, the raw model leads with F1 of 53.35% on AMBROSIA and 60.85% on AmbiQT. On AmP, DRIFT gains 15.98 F1 points over the raw model at 2.03 times its token cost, whereas LoC uses 18.65 times the raw model's tokens with lower F1. Within AutoCedar, structured interpretation lowers F1 by 6.44-6.95 points and raises AMBROSIA token use 2.2-fold. Paired reviewer comparisons show modest Kimi K3 gains of 1.93 points on AMBROSIA and 2.04 on AmbiQT. Clear-ambiguous AMBROSIA analysis shows greater relative stability for AutoCedar, with a smaller F1 drop than the raw model (11.46 vs. 16.68 points), alongside lower aggregate F1 (50.16% vs. 53.35%). Autoformalization alone does not guarantee robustness to ambiguity: the tested additions offer no uniformly effective remedy, making accuracy, ambiguity handling, and inference cost joint design requirements.

</details>


### [78] [AdapBench: Adaptive Early-Stopping and Prioritization for Efficient Microbenchmarking in Cloud-based CI/CD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01255)
*Sebastian Koch,Jonas Heisterberg,Nils Japke,Trever Schirmer,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AdapBench is an online scheduling system that dynamically prioritizes and early‑stops microbenchmarks to cut execution time while maintaining high detection accuracy. It saves up to 42% of run time on Java projects and 85% on Go projects with negligible overhead and zero false positives.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Microbenchmark suites used for continuous benchmarking are slow, causing delays and higher costs. Existing methods run benchmarks uniformly, missing opportunities to early‑stop or reorder them based on real‑time evidence of performance changes.

Method: AdapBench proposes a runtime scheduling approach that continuously evaluates each benchmark, selects the most informative ones first, and stops a benchmark early once sufficient data supports accepting or rejecting a performance hypothesis. It implements an online decision‑making algorithm to prioritize and early‑stop benchmarks, tested on eight Java and four Go projects.

Result: - Maintains 90% change detection and false‑positive rate comparable to uniform runs on Java projects.
- Reduces aggregate execution duration by 42% on Java projects with scheduling overhead <0.001%.
- Achieves 100% change detection with zero false positives on Go projects, saving 85% of full run time.

Conclusion: AdapBench demonstrates significant runtime savings without sacrificing detection quality, offering a practical solution for faster continuous benchmarking in cloud‑based software development.

Abstract: Cloud-based software systems frequently release new versions, a fact further accelerated through agentic coding. Each new release, however, brings the risk of performance regressions which can irritate end users, increase infrastructure costs, and increase carbon footprints. Therefore, developers strive to catch such regressions in testing stages, usually through so-called continuous benchmarking. The standard way of doing this is using microbenchmark suites which, however, take notoriously long to execute. Optimizing the execution of microbenchmark suites is, hence, an active field of research. In this paper, we propose AdapBench, an online scheduling system for reducing the execution duration of microbenchmark suites. AdapBench is the first approach which dynamically makes at-runtime decisions for prioritizing the most informative benchmarks and early-stopping each one once enough evidence has accumulated to accept or reject a performance change hypothesis. We evaluate our prototype on eight popular Java projects, commonly used by microbenchmark research, and four additional Go projects. At matched 90% change detections and false-positive rate compared to an optimistic uniform run, AdapBench saves 42% of the aggregate execution duration on the Java projects, with negligible scheduling overhead (below 0.001% of the total runtime). On the Go projects, AdapBench finds every performance change with zero false positives while saving 85% of the full run.

</details>


### [79] [Agent Stack for ML Training Lifecycle: Generation, Validation, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00384)
*Jaideep Ray,Daniel Tang,Zejun Lin,Haoyue Tang,Lijuan Zhang,Tao Huang,Tommy Li,Aditya Jain,Ankit Goyal,Harsh Jain,Zhuo Zhi,Youmin Han,Santosh Jha,Shuying Liang,Yujie Ai,Jonathan Hung,Ethan Lyu,Abi Liu,Siddharth Singh,Feng Wen,Mahesh Ravindran,Souvik Ray,Yitong Zhou,Animesh Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LinkedIn部署的双代理栈AutoTorch和AutoResearch显著加速模型开发与迁移，实现4倍端到端时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型开发涉及重复的架构设计、平台集成、调试、训练和评估，耗时较长，需要自动化工具提升效率。

Method: 提出两个独立可用的代理循环：AutoTorch用于模型生成和迁移（TensorFlow迁移、XGBoost替换、新模型开发），包含可执行验证钩子检查平台API合规性、可训练性和转换保真度；AutoResearch用于模型优化，通过专家引导或自主实验探索架构和训练配置变更。外部编排器管理并行试验、实验历史、候选选择和时间预算；共享运行时保持执行状态并独立分配GPU。

Result: AutoTorch在115次工作流运行中完成92次（80%），其中77次（83.7%）满足训练后质量要求。AutoResearch在100条生产管道中有28条提升了保留数据上的主要任务指标。工程团队估计端到端迁移时间从约8周缩短至2周，实现4倍加速。

Conclusion: 该代理栈通过自动化生成、验证和优化流程，大幅缩短模型开发周期，提高成功率，展示了在大规模生产环境中部署AI代理的可行性和价值。

Abstract: Production model development requires repeated architecture design, platform integration, debugging, training, and evaluation. We present an agent stack deployed at LinkedIn with two independently usable agent loops: AutoTorch for model generation and migration, and AutoResearch for model optimization. AutoTorch generates PyTorch models and training pipelines for TensorFlow migration, XGBoost replacement, and new-model development. Executable validation hooks check platform API compliance, trainability, and conversion fidelity on sampled data, guiding iterative repair before engineer review and full-scale distributed training. AutoResearch explores architecture and training-configuration changes through expert-steered or autonomous experimentation. An external orchestrator manages parallel trials, experiment history, candidate selection, and wall-clock budgets across agent sessions. A shared runtime preserves execution state independently of agent reasoning and allocates GPUs separately from agent execution. Across ranking, content understanding, and recommendation workloads, AutoTorch completed generation and full-scale training in 92 of 115 workflow runs (80%). Of these, 77 (83.7%) produced models meeting post-training quality requirements. In a separate cohort, AutoResearch improved the primary task metric on held-out data for 28 of 100 production pipelines. Engineering teams estimate that the stack reduced elapsed end-to-end migration time from approximately eight weeks to two weeks, a 4x speedup.

</details>


### [80] [DERED: Enhancing Third-Party Library Detection in Binaries via Deceptive Reuse Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00574)
*Shengjia Chang,Shouguo Yang,Xiao Deng,Qi Meng,Dan Wang,Bin Wang,Baojiang Cui,Shaocong Feng,Haoran Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个名为‘欺骗性重用’的新概念，指的是一种共享通用TPL代码的接收软件之间的误导性重用关系，并提出了一种名为DERED的新方法，用于增强TPL检测功能，区分真实重用和欺骗性重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPL检测方法难以有效区分正常代码重用和欺骗性重用，这可能导致误报或漏报。因此，有必要引入一种能够识别欺骗性重用的新型方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为DERED的检测方法，通过分析共享TPL代码的软件之间的关系和模式来识别欺骗性重用，从而提高TPL检测的准确性。

Result: DERED方法在检测真实重用和欺骗性重用方面表现优于先前的检测技术，具有更高的检测率和更低的误报率。

Conclusion: 该研究为提高TPL检测的有效性提供了一个有前景的解决方案，有望进一步提升软件安全分析的可靠性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the concept of deceptive reuse a misleading reuse relationship between recipient software that share common TPL code and proposes a novel approach, DERED, to enhance TPL detection by distinguishing between real reuse and deceptive reuse.

</details>


### [81] [AgentPRO: Coordinating Compiler Optimizations for LLM Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00628)
*Jie Ren,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentPRO是一个编译器框架，通过重组生成区域和操作，优化LLM代理的工作流，减少冗余的模型调用和上下文处理，实现显著的Token和时间节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在执行过程中反复调用模型以生成动作并解释工具结果，造成重复的上下文处理和生成开销。实际上，执行动作所需的价值不必全部在生成前被模型观测到，这为优化提供了机会。

Method: AgentPRO使用层级化表示组织计划职务、生成区域和具体操作，区分执行依赖与生成所需的观测。通过区域转换将兼容职务合并为更少的模型请求，通过操作转换减少冗余上下文和工具工作。转换协同利用初始计划、当前观测和历史执行画像，采用即时、自适应和画像引导优化。运行时守卫检查基于状态的假设并保留备用路径。

Result: 在AppWorld代理上，针对115个历史基线成功执行的任务，AgentPRO相比基线平均令牌消耗减少41.85%，平均组件时间减少6.61%，成功完成110个任务。

Conclusion: 通过区分依赖与观测、合并兼容职务以及消除冗余上下文，AgentPRO有效降低了LLM代理的运行开销，展现出强大的优化潜力。

Abstract: LLM agents repeatedly return to a model to generate actions and interpret tool results, incurring repeated context processing and generation overhead. Yet a value needed to execute an action need not be observed by the model to generate that action. This distinction creates an optimization opportunity: reconsider where model decisions are necessary, together with the work performed between them.
  We present AgentPRO, a compiler framework for optimizing agent workflows as execution information becomes available. AgentPRO organizes planned duties, generation regions, and concrete operations in a hierarchical representation, distinguishing execution dependencies from observations required for generation. Region transformations combine compatible duties into fewer model requests, while operation transformations reduce redundant context and tool work. These scopes interact: eliminating a redundant read can remove an observation requirement that otherwise separates generation regions. AgentPRO coordinates transformations using initial plans, current observations, and historical execution profiles through just-in-time, adaptive, and profile-guided optimization. Runtime guards check state-dependent assumptions and retain fallback paths.
  We implement guarded workflow transformations for AppWorld agents. On 115 tasks selected by successful historical baseline executions, automatic AgentPRO reduces all-attempt mean token consumption by 41.85% and mean component time by 6.61% relative to those baselines, successfully completing 110 of 115 tasks.

</details>


### [82] [The Testability Gap: A Pre-Registered Census of Naming versus Implementation in 59 Cognitive-Architecture LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00651)
*Jia Chen,Ying Xu,Ni Zhang,Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册的普查协议，审计了GitHub上59个最热门的声称具有认知架构的Python仓库及5个经典架构，发现存在显著的可测试性缺口：22%的仓库无代码引用，42%存在悬空引用，且绝大多数悬空引用（85.5%）从未在任何提交中存在过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现单个认知架构LLM系统中模块名称与计算相互独立，本文旨在量化该差距在整个系统类别中的普遍程度。

Method: 采用预注册的普查协议（样本框架、指标、验收标准在下载前冻结），调查GitHub上59个星标最多的声称认知架构的Python仓库，加上5个经典架构作为历史对照，测量可审计性缺口和完整性缺口，并对悬空引用进行Git生命周期分类。

Result: 22%仓库无代码引用；42%仓库至少有一个悬空引用，子组中位数8%，上四分位数19.6%，最大79.5%；三个预注册假设中三个被证伪：完整性缺口非普遍（58%仓库无可检查引用或引用全解析），声明密度不预测悬空率（rho=-0.10），经典架构对照组无显著优势（p=0.059）；仅1.7%仓库提供可执行的自我证伪标准；85.5%的悬空引用为“幽灵”路径（从未存在）。

Conclusion: 认知架构LLM系统普遍缺乏可测试性，文档与代码严重脱节，且大多数悬空引用源于从未实现的路径，表明该领域存在系统性的工程质量问题。

Abstract: A companion line of work audited individual cognitive-architecture LLM systems in depth and found that module names and module computations are empirically independent claims. This paper asks how far that gap extends across the system class. We pre-registered a census protocol (sample frame, metrics, and acceptance criteria frozen before any sample was downloaded) and surveyed the 59 most-starred Python repositories declaring cognitive architecture on GitHub, plus five classic architectures as a historical control. We measure a testability gap. First, an auditability gap: 22% of repositories document no code reference whatsoever. Second, an integrity gap: 42% of repositories have at least one dangling reference, and within that subgroup the median fraction is 8%, the top quartile 19.6%, and the maximum 79.5%; one 148-star system documents paths of which a majority do not exist. Three of four pre-registered hypotheses were falsified: the integrity gap is not universal (58% of repositories either offer no checkable references or offer references that all resolve); claim density does not predict dangling rate (rho = -0.10); and the classical-architecture control does not significantly outperform (p = 0.059 with five controls, all at zero dangling). Exactly one repository in 59 (1.7%) ships executable falsification criteria for its own claims. A second pre-registered round classified every dangling path by git lifetime: 85.5% of dangling references (159 of 186) are ghosts - paths that never existed at any commit - against 14.5% that were once real and later removed.

</details>


### [83] [Characterizing and Predicting the Longest CI Builds in Machine Learning Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00667)
*Yonghui Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Mohamed Sami Rakha,Grant Dick*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了107个开源机器学习项目的269,930次GitHub Actions构建，发现最长的18%构建占总耗时的80.3%，并探讨了其特征与预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习项目的持续集成（CI）构建通常耗时较长，而最长构建占据了大部分CI时间，因此理解其成因并实现提前预测具有重要意义。

Method: 通过统计描述、关联分析和机器学习方法，提取47个特征（包括构建历史、工作流结构和ML项目属性），使用XGBoost等四种分类器进行预测，并采用时间顺序验证评估模型性能。

Result: 最长构建主要由框架和基础设施类ML项目产生，与近期同一工作流的构建耗时及项目最近20次构建的中位数高度相关；工作流结构特征（如任务数量、控制流复杂度）比配置特征影响更大。最佳模型XGBoost的F1得分为0.775，AUC-ROC为0.955，显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 构建历史和CI工作流结构信息可有效预测ML项目中的最长构建，为优化CI资源分配和提前干预提供了可行手段。

Abstract: Continuous integration (CI) builds in machine learning (ML) projects tend to run longer than in other projects, yet little is known about the longest builds, which consume most CI time. This paper characterizes the longest builds in ML projects, analyzes how CI workflow properties are associated with them, and evaluates whether they can be predicted before execution. We study 269,930 GitHub Actions builds from 107 open-source ML projects. The top 18% longest builds account for 80.30% of total build time and fail in 39.09% of cases. Build duration differs significantly across ML project types. The framework and infrastructure ML projects have the longest upper tail and produce 98% of the longest builds, whereas projects that use existing ML libraries have the lowest median build duration. The longest builds are most strongly associated with the recent history of the same workflow, as the duration of its last build and the median duration of the project's last 20 builds show large effect sizes. The longest builds are more consistently associated with CI workflow structure than with configuration. The number of jobs, control flow complexity, coupling complexity, and maximum steps per job show medium effect sizes, whereas caching, matrix settings, and Docker builds show small or negligible effects. Framework-specific workflows have the largest percentage of builds among long builds. Platform-specific workflows produce the largest number of the longest builds. Finally, 47 features describing build history, workflow structure, and the ML project outperform a traditional baseline with all four classifiers. In a chronological evaluation, the best model (XGBoost) reaches an F1-score of 0.775, compared with 0.682 for the baseline, and an AUC-ROC of 0.955. These results show that build history and CI workflow structure help identify the longest builds in ML projects before they run.

</details>


### [84] [Getting a Grip: Operating Human-Agent Software Teams Through Governed Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00689)
*Michael Granitzer,Kanishka Ghosh Dastidar,Andreas Einwiller,Jelena Mitrović,Saber Zerhoudi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为 yoars 的治理系统，通过消息协议来规范由人类和多个编码智能体组成的协作团队。通过926次控制实验和为期四周的实地研究，系统评估了房间规则、安全防御（如防范恶意后门）以及治理效率。结果表明，优化消息启动规则能显著降低协调失败率，但角色和投票机制在流程控制上并不可靠，审计中存在误报，且人类决策仍是团队协作中的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发日益由多人指导多个来自不同供应商的编码智能体（coding agents）的团队完成。这类团队需要明确谁可以向谁请求什么、哪些消息启动工作、哪些决策具有约束力，以及谁导致了变更，因此需要一种基于消息协议的标准化治理机制。

Method: 提出了 yoars 系统，作为人和智能体认证主体之间交换类型化、寻址消息的中继，记录在包含目标、决策规则和路由的房间中。通过926次控制实验（其中899次可分析，部分包含恶意成员植入后门场景）以及一项涉及最多19个智能体和2个人的为期四周的实地研究进行评估。

Result: 1. 修改消息启动规则并限制重复消息后，协调失败率从42%降至13%（p = .0063）；2. 指定的角色和投票未能可靠地产生预期流程，对话中的防御无法有效阻止后门植入；3. 审计员在25个攻击者说服队友写入后门的案例中全部识别出了说服者，但也误报了40个诚实运行中的24个；4. 实地研究产生了7457次操作，人类协调员的审批成为主要瓶颈，而智能体响应迅速，上下文持有占用了主要的代币成本。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了人机团队治理中的关键挑战（如人类注意力瓶颈、审计误报、工具失败等），总结了7条经验教训和一份检查表，并公开了语料库、脱敏房间账本和分析脚本，为未来人机协作治理提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Software is increasingly developed by teams in which several people direct many coding agents from different vendors. Such teams must establish who may request what from whom, which messages start work, which decisions bind, and who caused a change. We propose to govern a human-agent team through its message protocol and present yoars, a relay in which humans and agents are authenticated principals that exchange typed, addressed messages in rooms holding goals, decision rules, routing and an attributable, append-only record. This report gives the architecture, the full controlled study and the field material. In 926 controlled runs (899 analyzable), small agent teams built programs under varied room rules, some with a hostile member planting a backdoor. Revising which messages start work, bundled with limits on repeated messages, was followed by a drop in total coordination failure from 42% to 13% (p = .0063, adjusted p = .057 over nine registered tests). Instructed roles and votes did not reliably produce the intended process, and no defense inside the conversation reliably prevented the backdoor. An auditor read the records of 96 runs with a confirmed backdoor. In the 25 runs where the attacker had persuaded a teammate to write it, the auditor named the persuader in all 25, but it also flagged 24 of 40 honest runs. In a four-week field study, up to nineteen agents and two people developed a distributed system through yoars, producing 7,457 acts. Human attention and decision making emerged as the critical resource: requests waited mainly for the human-run coordinator and for approvals, while agents answered within minutes. Most governance requirements emerged from operation, and holding context dominated token cost. We report twenty-six failures of our own instruments, derive seven lessons and a checklist, and release the corpus, the redacted room ledger and the analysis scripts.

</details>


### [85] [Trustworthy Execution of Industrial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02300)
*Zhongjie Wang,Mingyi Liu,Xiang He,Xiao Wang,Shuang Yu,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种在单一企业治理域内实现可信工业Agent执行的框架，包括八个控制域和四层架构，通过分离工具返回与实际工程效果、证据与判断等来确保安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业Agent对持久工程对象的修改缺乏验证，导致无法确认变更是否发生、证据是否充分或历史结果是否适用。

Method: 采用八个控制域、四层架构和三条控制链，通过分离工具返回与实际效果、证据与工程判断，并使用封闭集规范符合性检查、本地回归修复等方式进行实验验证。

Result: 通过OpenFOAM、CAD/CAE等原型，展示了完整覆盖但判断失败的案例，指出缺失的强制检查、被丢弃的负事实和生产者‑消费者映射不一致等问题，同时表明0/29危险提交仅反映规范符合性而非安全概率。

Conclusion: 完整的检查清单和跨模块事实保存是扩展该框架的前提，需在实现路径与实证证据之间明确区分。

Abstract: Industrial agents increasingly modify persistent engineering objects rather than merely generate answers. Authentication and successful calls cannot establish whether intended changes occurred, evidence supports a conclusion, or historical results remain applicable. We present a framework for trustworthy industrial-agent execution within a single enterprise governance domain. Eight control domains assign responsibilities across authority, planning, composition, tool execution, actual effects, evidence, baseline and use governance, and recovery and evolution. A four-plane architecture and three control chains connect these responsibilities through typed objects and version-bound interfaces. The specification separates tool returns from actual engineering effects, evidence coverage from engineering judgment, and historical lifecycle state from current applicability. Multi-agent handoffs are an architectural extension examined through deterministic interface tests. Distinct prototypes examine selected mechanisms using conformance tests, ablations, uncertainty stress, CAD/CAE execution, and local persistence. An OpenFOAM fixture produces COMPLETE coverage with FAIL judgment; an expired-job case requires readmission despite completed computation. CAD and recovery counterexamples expose omitted mandatory checks, discarded negative facts, and inconsistent producer-consumer mappings. Targeted repairs support local regression conclusions. The reported 0/29 hazardous commits measure closed-set specification conformance, not a safety probability. We distinguish the complete framework, implemented paths, and empirical evidence, identifying check-inventory completeness and cross-module fact preservation as prerequisites for extending these results.

</details>


### [86] [Verification-Gated Redraw and Repair for Local Small Language Models: A Paired Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00707)
*Fedir Rykhtik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究比较了单次回答、重抽样和诊断修复三种策略在Ornith和Gemma模型上的表现，发现三者无显著差异，且公共检查不能保证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨当模型生成的程序出错时，应选择重新抽样还是诊断修复来恢复。

Method: 采用受控、资源受限的实验设计，在72个任务（HumanEval+、SQL、shell）上对两个模型分别实施三种策略，仅使用公共检查进行在线决策，每个任务每条轨迹一次。

Result: 重抽样将成功率从51.4%提升至59.7%（Ornith）和55.6%至62.5%（Gemma），诊断修复分别达到52.8%和63.9%；经Holm校正后，四种配对比较均不显著（最小调整p=0.125）。公共检查错误接受25个Ornith和11个Gemma候选，表明接受不等于正确。

Conclusion: 本研究提供有限样本的实验记录，不能推断策略等价性、模型排名或通用可靠性，强调需更多独立重复实验。

Abstract: Executable checks can reject incorrect model-generated programs, but rejection alone does not determine whether fresh sampling or diagnostic repair is preferable. We present a controlled, resource-bounded conceptual replication and extension of established recovery policies: a single answer (N), one verification-triggered redraw (R), and one diagnostic repair (D), for Ornith 1.5 9B and Gemma 4 E4B served locally in nominal 4-bit MLX format. The frozen evaluation contains 72 tasks: 40 HumanEval+ problems, 16 SQL tasks and 16 shell tasks. Policies share initial responses and use only public checks for online decisions; every candidate is model-generated. Each task has one stochastic trajectory per model, so the study does not estimate variation across independent sampling blocks. Redraw increases observed final-test success from 51.4% to 59.7% for Ornith and from 55.6% to 62.5% for Gemma. Diagnostic repair reaches 52.8% and 63.9%, respectively. None of four internally prespecified paired contrasts is significant after Holm correction (minimum adjusted p = 0.125). Public checks falsely accept 25 Ornith and 11 Gemma candidates, demonstrating that acceptance is not a correctness guarantee. A post-review audit of initial answers gives perfect-retry upper bounds of 76.4% and 87.5%, respectively: accepted initial errors cannot trigger recovery under the fixed gate. This one-block, resource-bounded study provides a finite-cohort experimental record, not policy equivalence, a general model ranking or universal reliability

</details>


### [87] [Architecting Fairness-Aware LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00723)
*Alessandra Parziale,Valeria Pontillo,Fabio Palomba,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三种可复用设计模式的公平性保障架构，并将其实例化为多智能体框架，在运行时评估并迭代优化LLM响应，以解决LLM在决策关键系统中的公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地用作决策支持工具，其可能产生刻板、歧视或不公平的响应。公平性不再能作为下游评估步骤，而必须成为交互本身的架构关注点。现有公平性方法多为独立评估技术，缺乏在运行时验证和缓解违规的可复用机制。

Method: 提出基于三种可复用设计模式的公平性保障架构，将其实例化为多智能体框架，在交付前对LLM响应进行评估和迭代优化。

Result: 在涉及不同LLM家族的八种配置中进行初步评估，结果显示该架构在所有配置中均能识别公平性违规并迭代生成合规响应，但不同模型家族组合下的行为表现有所差异。

Conclusion: 实验结果为所提模式的联合实例化提供了初步证据，并迈出了将非功能性属性架构化到基于LLM系统中的第一步。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as decision-support tools but may generate stereotypical, discriminatory, or unfair responses. As LLMs become embedded in decision-critical software systems, fairness can no longer be treated as a downstream evaluation step, but must become an architectural concern of the interaction itself. Yet current fairness approaches operate as standalone evaluation techniques, lacking a reusable mechanism to verify and mitigate violations at runtime. We propose a fairness-assurance architecture grounded in three reusable design patterns, instantiated as a multi-agent framework that evaluates and iteratively refines LLM responses before delivery. A preliminary evaluation across eight configurations involving different LLM families shows that the architecture identifies fairness violations and iteratively produces compliant responses in all configurations, with behavior differing depending on the model-family composition. These results provide initial evidence for the joint instantiation of the proposed patterns and a first step toward architecting non-functional properties into LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [88] [SNAPPerf: Software-Aware Tabular Foundation Model for Retraining-Free Performance Prediction of Configurable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00944)
*Amine Lbath,Massih-Reza Amini,Vadim Okun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SNAPPERF is a sample底垣引没对可防设置软件的表格模型模型，在异租数据上一次预稿，每次新更陈的软件只需要的法规图细料来豕分、无需进行每软件域对模型，尺对该模型正在㥓3际营依陆中12各进几应用系统，在6xxx6舒域，4個性性资，SNAPPERF永漏个失屏比度比度比度为37% (<XXX) <27> <27>, <27> <54% 小量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current performance models for configurable software require training on measured configurations for each system and workload, making them costly to update as software changes frequently. The need is for a model that can generalize across systems without per‑system retraining.

Method: Develop SNAPPERF: a sample‑efficient tabular foundation model pretrained on synthetic configuration‑performance data. During inference, a few measured configurations serve as context to predict the performance of the rest, eliminating any per‑system training or hyperparameter tuning.

Result: Evaluated on 39,866 configurations from 12 real‑world systems across 6 domains and 4 performance metrics, SNAPPERF reduces prediction error by 38% over the prior state‑of‑the‑art with the same measurement budget, and by 25% over the best generic tabular foundation model. It achieves state‑of‑the‑art accuracy using 54% fewer measurements on average.

Conclusion: Results demonstrate that software‑related knowledge can be encoded in pretraining data rather than in per‑system models, opening a new research direction in software‑aware pretraining.

Abstract: Modern software systems expose hundreds of configuration options whose combinations can substantially affect execution time, memory and energy usage. Measuring software performance resulting from a configuration set requires running a realistic workload. Since the configuration space grows exponentially, exhaustive benchmarking is infeasible. Prior work therefore trains models on a small set of measured configurations to predict the performance of the rest. Yet these models are specific to a particular software version, hardware platform, and workload, so changes require new measurements and retraining, and such changes are becoming more frequent with coding agents.
  We challenge this per-system training assumption with SNAPPERF (Software-aware, No-retraining, Adaptive Prediction), a sample-efficient tabular foundation model specialized for configurable software. SNAPPERF is pretrained once on synthetic data. Each new update only requires a few measured configurations as context to predict the rest without any per-system training or hyperparameter tuning. Across 39,866 configurations from 12 real-world systems, spanning 6 domains and 4 performance metrics, SNAPPERF reduces prediction error by 38% over the prior state of the art at the same measurement budget, and by 25% over the best general-purpose tabular foundation model. It matches the state of the art's accuracy with 54% fewer measurements on average. These results suggest that knowledge about software can be encoded in pretraining data rather than in per-system models, opening a new research direction in software-aware pretraining.

</details>


### [89] [Dependable Agents Need Hard and Fuzzy Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01043)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文主张可靠智能体需同时具备“精确硬规则”与“模糊学习规则”，并通过购买、权限委托、数据访问三个领域的实验验证两者结合能有效防止越权并保留精确边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能读懂自然语言请求，但规则执行不稳定且易受压力影响；传统代码能精确执行规则，却无法理解自然语言。单一方式会在可明确界定的限制或不可精确表述的判断上出现越权或误判。

Method: 在购买、权限委托和数据访问三个领域，使用外部团队撰写的1340个情节进行测试；在提议与动作之间设置硬编码防护层，并对183,204个提议进行穷举检索；采用“概念编程”让小型模型学习四条判断规则，并在未见过的措辞上评估误判率；将学习到的规则置于防护层之前，分别对比纯模型、纯防护层和组合方案。

Result: 纯模型自行决定时，购买情节中多达151/340次执行未授权操作，权限委托情节中269/470次，最强模型在所有条件下都发生越权；硬编码防护层零越权，穷举检索也印证这一点；外部编写规则并映射到防护层的测试中，仅在事先写下的边界出现2次越权。概念编程训练的小型模型标记出96条无法用精确规则发现的违规购买中的95条，误报4/108条合法请求；组合方案在192条违规请求中仅漏拦1条，而防护层单独分别漏拦77条和70条，且防护层仍保持所有精确限制；提示式前沿模型表现相当，说明小型教导规则可胜任，而非一定更优。

Conclusion: 可靠智能体应同时具备精确执行明确限制的硬规则与处理不可精确表述判断的模糊学习规则；两者结合可显著降低越权风险，同时对硬边界保持绝对把控，且小型模型通过少量规则教导即可承担判断层工作。

Abstract: Language models let software read requests in ordinary language, but they keep rules as people do: usually, and less so under pressure. Conventional code keeps rules exactly, but it cannot read a request. We argue that dependable agents need both, because the two kinds of rule cover overlapping and unequal regions of what an agent might do. Code holds the limits that can be stated exactly. Learned fuzzy rules cover the judgment that cannot. We test the hard layer in three domains, purchasing, delegation of authority and data access, on 1,340 episodes written by authors outside our team. Models left to decide for themselves executed unauthorized actions in up to 151 of 340 purchasing episodes and 269 of 470 delegation episodes, and the strongest model breached in every condition. A hard guard between proposal and action executed none, and an exhaustive search of 183,204 proposals agreed. In a further test, where an outsider wrote the owner's rules and a program mapped them to the guard, two episodes breached at a boundary we had written down in advance. We then test the pair. Concept Programming instills a named rule in a model and measures its errors on wording it never saw. A small model taught four judgment rules this way flagged 95 of 96 rule-breaking purchases that no exact rule can see, and 4 of 108 legitimate requests. Placed in front of the guard, it left 1 of 192 rule-breaking requests unstopped, where the guard alone left 77 and 70, and the guard still held every exact limit. A prompted frontier model did about as well, so the claim is that a small taught rule can do the job, not that it does it better.

</details>


### [90] [From Stories to Bugs: Story-Based Semantic Fuzzing of Interactive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01173)
*Yeshayahu Weiss,Achiya Elyasaf,Gera Weiss*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 文章提出基于行为的语义模糊测试方法，通过语义约束的行为故事组合作为搜索对象，有效保持合法前缀并暴露并发敏感的业务逻辑故障。在Magento和四个状态性REST API上的实验验证了该方法在深度状态触达和故障复现方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂交互系统隐藏的业务逻辑故障沿深状态依赖行为和并发敏感两个轴出现，现有方法难以同时处理长有效前缀和操作顺序约束。需要一种新方法来重达深层状态并暴露业务逻辑缺陷。

Method: 提出 story-based semantic fuzzing，将语义约束的行为故事组合作为主要搜索对象。利用Behavioral Programming实现语义绑定和状态约束，保持合法前缀；通过事件有效性、调度选择和重放暴露失败相关的交错。配合overlap-aware ranking和控制顺序干预，提升故障复现率。

Result: 在Magento上，overlap-aware ranking将间歇性Shipping Fee race的复现率从3.6%提升至41.2%；控制顺序干预使第二个故障签名从100/100初始执行降至0/200受限执行。对于REST研究，版本化端到端管线以冻结的公开OpenAPI合同作为唯一输入，自动生成Basic/Complex故事、依赖结构和界面绑定。在共享黑盒任务下，Basic和Complex确认了0/11和11/11语义故障类别，而RESTler和EvoMaster确认了2/11和0/11。配对绑定消融实验显示有绑定时产生250/250有效依赖操作，无绑定时0/250，补充前缀控制将差异局限于合法关系状态的保持。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，搜索可合法的语义组合是达达深层状态并暴露并发敏感业务逻辑故障的统一且有效的表示。该方法在不同系统上均表现出色，验证了其统一性和有效性。

Abstract: Complex interactive systems hide business-logic failures along two complementary axes: deep state-dependent behaviors that require long valid prefixes and concurrency-sensitive failures that depend on the ordering of otherwise legal operations. We present story-based semantic fuzzing, a methodology that treats semantically constrained compositions of behavioral stories as primary search objects. Implemented through Behavioral Programming, semantic binding and state constraints preserve legal prefixes, while event eligibility, schedule selection, and replay expose failure-relevant interleavings. We evaluate the methodology on Magento and four stateful REST APIs. On Magento, overlap-aware ranking increased reproduction of a sporadic Shipping Fee race from 3.6% to 41.2%; a controlled ordering intervention reduced a second failure signature from 100/100 initial executions to 0/200 constrained executions. For the REST study, a versioned end-to-end pipeline took each frozen public OpenAPI contract as its sole benchmark-specific model-generation input and automatically generated the Basic and Complex stories, dependency structure, and interface bindings. Under a shared black-box task and sequence-local evaluation contract, Basic and Complex confirmed 0/11 and 11/11 semantic fault classes, while RESTler and EvoMaster confirmed 2/11 and 0/11. A paired binding ablation produced 250/250 valid dependent operations with binding and 0/250 without it, while supplementary prefix controls localized the difference to preservation of legal relational state. Together, the studies show that searching admissible semantic compositions provides a unified and effective representation for reaching deep states and exposing concurrency-sensitive business-logic failures.

</details>


### [91] [Do Coding Agents Catch Their Own Bugs? (They Don't.)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01181)
*Shaswat Patel,Michel Liao,Peter Henderson,He He,Valerie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 介绍了一个名为Foresight-SWE的新基准，用于评估编码代理在解决软件工程任务时预测和修复未明确提及的问题的能力。该基准包含来自4种编程语言和35个知名开源仓库的212个样本，每个样本包含两个合并请求（PR），第二个PR修复了第一个PR引入的缺陷。通过计算代码代理在完全解决原始任务的同时避免引入此缺陷的比率（FRR），研究对11个大型语言模型进行了评估，发现FRR范围仅为6.13‑24.53 △，表示模型在解决由第一个PR引起的问题方面表现不佳。这表明了在代码训练、代码代理对齐以及代码评审工具等方面存在的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准往往无法评估代理在软件工程任务中预测和修复未明确提及的问题（如漏洞或其他缺陷）的能力。因此，需要一个新的评估框架来测试代理的“前瞻性”推理能力。

Method: 创建了Foresight-SWE基准，其中每个样本包含两个PR：第一个PR引入缺陷，第二个PR修复这些缺陷。通过计算代码代理在解决原始任务的同时避免引入这些缺陷的比率（FRR）来评估11个大型语言模型。

Result: 评估结果显示，大型语言模型的FRR范围仅为6.13‑2.53△，表明模型在解决由第一个PR引起的问题方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: Foresight-SWE为评估代码代理的“前瞻性”能力提供了一个新颖的框架。这一低性能结果强调了在代码训练、代码代理对齐以及代码评审工具等方面进行改进的必要性，以实现更安全、更可靠的代码生成。

Abstract: In software development, engineers need to foresee and fix potential problems with their code that are not explicitly specified in requirements---such as pre-emptively considering vulnerabilities or other bugs from a code change. But current benchmarks often do not measure these future-looking consequences. We introduce $\textbf{Foresight-SWE}$, a benchmark for measuring whether coding agents can anticipate and address unstated problems during software engineering tasks. Foresight-SWE consists of 212 samples spanning 4 languages and 35 popular open-source repositories. Each sample contains two pull requests (PRs), the second of which resolves defects the first one introduced. To measure whether agents exhibit foresight, we define the $\textbf{foresight resolve rate (FRR)}$ as the fraction of instances where an agent fully resolves the original task while also avoiding the defect identified by a later PR. We evaluate eleven large language models and find that FRR ranges from only 6.13% to 24.53%, indicating that models largely fail to address problems introduced by the first PR change. This raises important questions about how models are trained and aligned in coding contexts, as well as how code review tools should be improved for more fail-safe coding. Foresight-SWE shifts evaluations in this direction.

</details>


### [92] [A Domain Driven Design Based Reference Architecture for IoT Systems Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01196)
*Yousef Abuseta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向物联网（IoT）系统工程的综合参考架构，系统地融合了领域驱动设计（DDD）与事件驱动架构（EDA）原则，以实现业务逻辑与解耦、事件驱动微服务的和谐统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网（IoT）系统因其运行环境的动态性、波动性和不确定性而面临严峻的架构复杂性挑战。传统单体架构僵化，难以满足可扩展性等关键质量属性。微服务虽提供了可扩展的解决方案，但其技术边界需与领域业务精确对齐，同时需要降低架构耦合、提升集成能力并优化设备能效。

Method: 通过领域驱动设计（DDD）将复杂业务领域战略分解为模块化的子域，使其直接映射到独立的微服务；利用事件驱动架构（EDA）作为核心运营支撑，以最小化服务间的架构耦合，增强系统可集成性，并优化设备能源效率。

Result: 构建了一套系统化的物联网系统工程参考架构，实现了DDD与EDA原则在微服务架构中的深度融合与协调。

Conclusion: 该参考架构为工程化构建稳健、灵活且高效的物联网系统提供了系统化的解决方案，通过业务与技术的精准对齐，有效应对了物联网环境的内在复杂性。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm continues to gain significant traction across diverse do-mains, primarily driven by its transformative and socio-economic benefits. However, engineering robust IoT systems remains a formidable challenge, largely due to the inherent dynamism, volatility, and uncertainty of their operational environments. To mitigate this architectural complexity, microservices have emerged as a viable alternative to traditional, rigid monolithic frameworks, offering a scalable mechanism to fulfill critical quality attributes. To maximize the efficacy of a microservices architecture, its technical boundaries must align precisely with domain-specific business requirements. Domain-Driven Design (DDD) addresses this need by strategically decomposing complex domains into modular, highly manageable subdomains that map directly to individual microservices. Furthermore, to minimize architectural coupling, enhance system integrability, and optimize device energy efficiency, Event-Driven Architecture (EDA) serves as a critical operational enabler. This paper proposes a comprehensive reference architecture for IoT systems engineering, systematically leveraging DDD and EDA principles to harmonize business logic with decoupled, event-driven microservices.

</details>


### [93] [Toward Bounding Coding-Agent Autonomy in Software Maintenance: A Pilot Study of Boundary Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01265)
*Heeyong Eun,Seungyeon Chung,Hyejee Park,Youngeun Lee,Sukhyun Hong,Hyewon Jung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Boundary Contracts明确了代码编辑任务中各种文件的角色，实验显示提供该信息可显著提升代码选择F1、覆盖率及可接受补丁数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主编码代理在维护任务中的自主权增强，单靠上下文无法判定文件是否可编辑或是否必要，需明确文件的操作角色。

Method: 在真实企业仓库中对40项维护任务进行实验，比较有无Boundary Contract信息的相同基于Codex的编码代理，测量宏观编辑选择F1、必要行为覆盖率及可接受补丁数，并进一步在21项任务中控制相同文件的修改，评估是否仅因文件定位提升。

Result: 提供Boundary Contract后，宏观编辑选择F1从0.440提升至0.682，必要行为覆盖率从0.429提升至0.673，可接受补丁数从9项提升至18项，且在控制相同文件的21项任务中仍有显著差异，表明角色信息独立于文件定位对提升作用。

Conclusion: 本研究提供初步证据，说明在仓库级维护中，如何生成、验证、修订和强制编码代理的操作边界是未来研究的关键问题。

Abstract: As coding agents gain greater autonomy to modify software repositories, relevant context alone does not define their operational authority. An artifact may be relevant to a maintenance task while being inappropriate to edit; conversely, another artifact may be required even when it is not explicitly mentioned in the request. We explore Boundary Contracts, a machine-readable abstraction that makes task-specific artifact roles (editable, inspect-only, required, and protected) explicit before repository modification begins.
  We conduct a pilot study on 40 maintenance tasks from a real enterprise repository, comparing the same Codex-based coding agent with and without Boundary Contract information. The boundary-informed condition improves macro edit-selection F1 from 0.440 to 0.682, required-behavior coverage from 0.429 to 0.673, and the number of strictly acceptable patches from 9 of 40 to 18 of 40. To probe whether these gains are explained only by improved file localization, we additionally analyze 21 tasks in which both conditions modify at least one identical file. Differences remain in edit selectivity and patch outcomes, providing initial evidence that explicit artifact roles may influence maintenance behavior beyond localization alone.
  This pilot does not establish the general effectiveness of Boundary Contracts. Rather, it provides initial evidence for a broader research question: how should the operational boundaries of coding agents be generated, validated, revised, and ultimately enforced as repository-level maintenance becomes increasingly autonomous?

</details>


### [94] [Causality-Based Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01292)
*Melane Navaratnarajah,Aidan Dakhama,Tom Yaacov,David A. Kelly,Karine Even-Mendoza,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于实际因果关系理论的新型故障定位方法（CBFL），利用因果责任和距离指标来提高定位的可解释性和修复效率。该方法在工具 SoftReX 中实现，实验表明其性能显著优于基于变异的故障定位（MBFL）工具，并大幅减少了所需评估的变异体数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统基于变异的故障定位（MBFL）在可解释性和修复效率上的局限，通过引入实际因果关系理论，形式化定义代码位置在失败测试上下文中的因果贡献（因果责任），从而提供更清晰、更易修复的故障定位指引。

Method: 结合实际因果关系理论定义代码位置的“因果责任”，利用距离指标对修复方案进行排序（优先最小修复并惩罚导致测试失败的变异）。在 SoftReX 工具中实现了该方法，并提出了一种基于程序结构和表示的复杂变异生成策略，以提高定位准确性和评估效率。

Result: 在相同变异体集合下，SoftReX 在平均倒数排名（MRR）和 Top-1 指标上比最先进的 MBFL 工具提升了 16%。此外，所提出的变异生成策略在保持竞争力性能的同时，将所需评估的变异体数量减少了 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: 基于因果关系的故障定位（CBFL）方法通过 SoftReX 工具，提供了一种更具可解释性、更准确且更高效的故障定位方案，其性能和效率均优于现有的最先进方法。

Abstract: We introduce Causality-Based Fault Localization (CBFL), a novel approach to fault localization grounded in the theory of actual causality. We use the notion of causal responsibility to formalize and define the contribution of a given location in the source code under the context of a failing test. We also utilize a distance metric to prioritize minimal fixes and penalize broken tests, leading to more interpretable and fixable fault localization. We implemented CBFL in a tool, SoftReX, and our evaluation results demonstrate that SoftReX outperforms state-of-the-art Mutation-Based Fault Localization (MBFL) tools based on Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) and Top-1 by 16% on both metrics, when using the same set of mutants. We also propose a sophisticated mutation generation strategy based on the structure and representation of the program to improve the accuracy of our ranked list, which outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 2.6x in terms of required mutants evaluated while remaining competitive in performance.

</details>


### [95] [WFDB-x: Conformance-Tested Implementations of the WFDB Software Package in Seven Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01358)
*Reza Sameni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WFDB-x 是一个跨语言、高性能的 WFDB 生理波形格式重新实现，支持七种编程语言，通过大量一致性与单元测试确保输出与原始 C 库匹配，并移除了可能导致误读的隐式参数与默认值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WFDB 软件仅限 C、Python 和 MATLAB/Octave，在边界情况和校验和计算上可能产生不同结果，且采样频率、增益等默认值可能导致录音被无声地误解读。因此需要一个跨语言、行为一致的重新实现来支持可重复研究。

Method: 在人工指导与评审下，利用 AI 辅助原生重新实现了 WFDB 软件包 10.7.0，覆盖 Python、C++、Rust、Julia、R、MATLAB/Octave 和 Kotlin；每种实现均提供库函数、高级接口和命令行工具。使用 1098 个一致性用例、877 个遗留模式用例、单元测试及差分模糊测试与原始 C 程序比对；保留遗留模式以维持原始行为，并故意修改以移除隐式波形参数、限制文件和网络访问、防止标注丢失。

Result: 七种实现在测试的 Linux 平台上全部通过每个一致性和遗留模式用例，输出完全匹配；单元测试包含 9353 个断言。C++、Rust 和 Julia 读取记录的速度比原始 C 库快 1.5 到 6.9 倍；Python 实现写入记录的速度比现有 wfdb-python 库在基准测试中快 9.2 倍。

Conclusion: WFDB-x 提供了经过测试的开源基础，可在七种语言中一致地读写生理记录，无需共享编程环境即可支持可重复研究与软件开发；同时公开了 AI 辅助开发过程及 Token 使用情况，可供其他项目参考。

Abstract: The WFDB format is widely used to store and exchange physiological waveforms and annotations. However, existing software implementations are limited to C, Python, and MATLAB/Octave, can produce different results in corner cases and checksum calculations, and have defaults such as sampling frequency and gain, that can lead to silent misinterpretation of recordings. We developed WFDB-x, a cross-language, performance-optimized reimplementation of WFDB in seven programming languages, with extensive unit tests, to provide consistent WFDB processing across programming languages. We reimplemented the WFDB Software Package 10.7.0 natively in Python, C++, Rust, Julia, R, MATLAB/Octave, and Kotlin, using AI-assistance under human direction and review. Each implementation has library functions, a high-level interface, and command-line tools. We compared their outputs with those of the original C programs using 1098 conformance cases, 877 legacy-mode cases, unit tests, and differential fuzzing. Deliberate changes remove implicit waveform parameters, restrict file and network access, and prevent annotation loss. A legacy mode retains the original behavior. All seven implementations passed every conformance and legacy-mode case on the tested Linux platform, with matching outputs. The unit tests contain 9353 assertions. C++, Rust, and Julia read records 1.5-6.9 times faster than the original C library, while the Python implementation wrote records 9.2 times faster than the existing wfdb-python library in the benchmark. WFDB-x provides a tested, open-source basis for reading and writing physiological recordings consistently across seven languages, supporting reproducible research and software development without requiring a shared programming environment. We also share details of the AI-assisted development process and token utilization, which may be useful for other applications.

</details>


### [96] [CrossSWE: Benchmarking Cross-Repository Joint Development for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01484)
*Silin Chen,Yuling Shi,Heng Lian,Zihan Zhang,Xiaodong Gu,Haotian Lin,Zhensu Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CrossSWE is a new benchmark evaluating LLMs on joint changes across 165 repositories (‑71 real‑world instances, 20 domains, 13 languages). It shows that current LLMs (best GPT‑6.1 Sol) achieve only ~58% Pass@1, with cross‑repository inconsistency being the dominant failure mode (~44%).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing SWE benchmarks focus on single‑repo tasks, but real‑world development often spans multiple interdependent repos, requiring coordinated changes, dependency reasoning, and interface consistency. A benchmark that reflects this cross‑repository complexity was needed to expose current model limitations.

Method: Researchers constructed CrossSWE comprising 171 joint‑repo instances. Each instance specifies an intended behavior change that requires coordinated modifications across several repos. Models are evaluated by attempting to implement the change with only partial (single‑repo) modifications; full success requires correct changes in all relevant repos. Five state‑of‑the‑art LLMs (including GPT‑6.1 Sol) were tested under Pass@1 evaluation.

Result: The strongest model, GPT‑6.1 Sol, scored only 58.48% Pass@1. Across all models, the most frequent failure category was cross‑repository inconsistency (44.2% of errors), indicating difficulty in maintaining interface and dependency consistency when changes span multiple repos.

Conclusion: Cross‑repository development poses a significant challenge for current LLMs. The CrossSWE benchmark demonstrates large gaps in coordinated reasoning and consistency maintenance, highlighting the need for models that can reason about and modify multiple interdependent codebases simultaneously.

Abstract: As coding agents achieve increasingly high success rates on software engineering benchmarks, recent efforts have sought to increase task complexity through longer horizons and more extensive code modifications. However, real-world software development often spans multiple interdependent repositories, introducing additional challenges in dependency reasoning, change propagation, and interface consistency. Rather than further increasing complexity within individual repositories, we introduce CrossSWE, a benchmark for cross-repository joint development comprising 171 real-world instances spanning 165 repositories, 20 technical domains, and 13 programming languages. CrossSWE verifies the necessity of coordinated changes by testing whether partial repository modifications are insufficient to achieve the intended behavior.
