Our evaluation of five LLMs reveals substantial challenges, with the strongest model, GPT-6.1 Sol, achieving only 58.48% Pass@1. Furthermore, cross-repository inconsistencies emerge as the most prevalent failure mode (44.2%), highlighting the difficulty of maintaining consistency across interdependent repositories.

</details>


### [97] [Impossible Code, Barely Changed Score: A Replay Study of Judge-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01572)
*Masoud Fetanat*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估语言模型作为代码生成裁判的鲁棒性，发现基于判断者的高分可能掩盖输出质量的大幅下降，建议同时报告语法检查通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被用于在不运行代码的情况下评判生成代码，但需确保评估能敏感反映答案质量的实质性变化。

Method: 在421个编程问题上重复实验，对比基础模型Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct与两种GRPO训练版本（基于执行测试奖励CE-trained和基于强语言模型裁判奖励SLJ-trained），测量Pass@k分数及语法错误率。

Result: 训练后检查点产生无效Python的比例达63%-67%，但强裁判的Pass@32仅下降约3个百分点；随尝试次数增加，基础模型与训练模型在裁判评分下越来越相似；128次尝试时，裁判接受至少一个SLJ-trained答案的问题比例为96.44%，而执行测试仅28.27%。

Conclusion: 高裁判分数可能隐藏严重输出损伤，建议在报告基于裁判的评分时同时给出通过Python语法检查的答案比例，以提高评估的敏感性和可靠性。

Abstract: Language models are often used to judge generated code without running it. A useful evaluation should reveal substantial changes in the answers it scores. We examine this requirement by repeating an earlier evaluation on 421 coding problems, comparing Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct with two versions trained using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). One receives rewards from execution tests (CE-trained), and the other from a strong language-model judge (SLJ-trained). The base model's syntax-failure rate remains stable, but the trained checkpoints produce invalid Python on 63%-67% of attempts. Despite this change, the strong judge's Pass@32 scores fall by only about 3 percentage points, from 96.67% to 93.95% for SLJ-trained. Within the later run, allowing more attempts makes the base and trained checkpoints look increasingly similar under the judge. With 128 attempts per question, the judge accepts at least one SLJ-trained answer on 96.44% of problems, compared with 28.27% under execution tests. We recommend reporting the share of answers that pass Python's syntax check alongside every judge-based score. This check requires no tests or code execution and flags invalid answers that the judge accepts, although it cannot establish functional correctness. Our findings show how a high judge-based score can hide substantial output damage and motivate checking an evaluation's sensitivity to change. Project page and code: https://curiousorbitnotes.github.io/evorobust-replay-audit/

</details>


### [98] [What reviewers call a Long Method is a length: A pre-registered test of coupling divergence as a god-unit signal, and what it settled instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01636)
*Daniel Sagenschneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究发现，通过在复杂度上引入耦合差异权重以区分“神对象”和复杂算法的主张失效，但在方法层面上，固定的26行阈值能持续识别MLCQ审查员定义的长方法，其MCC达0.777，且该阈值似乎源于人类阅读极限而非代码结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为了解决伴随研究中‘复杂度异常值规则’无法区分‘神对象’与复杂算法的问题，尝试通过按单元的耦合差异（其输出数据减去输入数据）对复杂度进行加权来区分两者。

Method: 作者预注册了一项测试，通过计算单元的耦合差异（即其手-off数据减去receive数据）来加权复杂度，并排除叶子单元。耦合通过解析520个MLCQ仓库、500个新鲜Java仓库和73个Qualitas Corpus系统中的所有调用来测量。同时，还在方法层面分析代码长度与审查员判断的关系。

Result: 主要假设失效，加权复杂度形式与单纯复杂度对比MLCQ人类‘Blob’的池化MCC为-0.041 [−0.130, +0.054]，无显著差异，实验欠功效。但在方法层面，在探索集上选取的26行或更多的绝对长度阈值在确认集上达到MCC 0.777，优于最佳的每仓库形式，且每拥有十个或以上正例审查员的非叶子审查中，最佳阈值范围在17至34行之间。内部测量（调用、变量、数据流或切割成本）未能优化该常数。

Conclusion: 该常数代表人类阅读的极限。接下来的构造形式在于将长方法压制在该阈值之下的成本，这也是该构造与架构交汇的地方。

Abstract: A companion study found that a complexity outlier rule shows where complexity concentrates but cannot tell a god unit from a complex algorithm. This paper pre-registered a test of one way to tell them apart: weight a unit's complexity by its coupling divergence, the data it hands on minus the data it receives, so that a leaf is set aside. Coupling was measured by resolving every call in 520 MLCQ repositories, 500 fresh Java repositories and 73 Qualitas Corpus systems. The claim fails: the primary, the pooled MCC of the weighted form minus complexity alone against MLCQ's human Blob, is -0.041 [-0.130, +0.054], no difference resolved and underpowered, and the resolved readings go against it. What the data settle is at method level. What MLCQ's reviewers call a Long Method is an absolute length: a constant of 26 lines or more, chosen on the explore half, reaches MCC 0.777 on the confirm half, +0.080 [+0.016, +0.145] over the best per-repository form; every reviewer with ten or more positives, on their non-leaf reviews, has a best cutoff between 17 and 34 lines; and nothing measured inside a method, its calls, variables, data flow or the cost of cutting it, refines the constant. The constant is a limit of human reading. The next form of the construct is the cost of bringing a long method under it, which is where the construct meets architecture.

</details>


### [99] [Phase-wise MLLM Tuning for Multi-framework WebUI Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01692)
*Haoran Ma,Linxiao Li,Chenyue Wang,Haochen Sui,Jiechao Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多框架（React/Vue/Angular）WebUI 代码生成的监督微调方法，提出了一种基于兼容性和异构性信号的分阶段 MLLM 训练方法。核心包括：(1) 通过归一化映射保留各框架原生语法同时抑制变量命名和字面量噪声；(2) 离线估计跨框架兼容性和异构性，自动推导训练组及其顺序以最大化分阶段目标函数；(3) 执行分阶段适配器微调以减少跨框架干扰。实验表明该方法在生成质量和编译成功率上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在 WebUI 截图转 HTML/CSS 任务上表现良好，但当面对多个框架时容易产生负迁移导致编译失败，这些失败源于框架特定语法的混用和违反框架约束。因此需要一种训练策略来缓解多框架间的干扰问题。

Method: 分为三阶段：第一阶段应用归一化映射，在保留各框架原生语法的同时，抑制变量命名和字面内容的噪声；第二阶段离线估计跨框架兼容性和异构性信号，自动推导训练组及其最优顺序，最大化分阶段目标函数；第三阶段执行分阶段适配器微调，减少跨框架干扰。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，所提方法在生成质量和编译成功率方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种有效的多框架 WebUI 代码生成微调方法，通过兼容性、异构性信号驱动的分阶段训练，成功缓解了负迁移问题，显著提升了跨框架代码生成的质量与成功率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance in translating WebUI screenshots into HTML/CSS. However, when they faced multiple frameworks (React/Vue/Angular), they often suffer from negative transfer that leads to compilation failures. These failures commonly stem from mixing framework specific syntax and violating framework constraints. This paper studies supervised fine-tuning for multi-framework WebUI code generation and proposes a phase-wise MLLM tuning method driven by compatibility and heterogeneity signals across frameworks. We first apply a normalization mapping that preserves each framework's native syntax while suppressing noise from variable naming and literal content. We then estimate cross framework heterogeneity and compatibility offline, and automatically derive training groups and their ordering by maximizing a phased objective. Finally, we perform phase-wise adapter tuning to reduce cross framework interference. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that our proposed method improves both generation quality and compilation success rate compared with baselines.

</details>


### [100] [VeriBase: Automated Construction of Theorem Libraries for Software Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01743)
*Zihao Zhang,Zenan Li,Taolue Chen,Zhe Zhou,Tao Xie,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VeriBase automatically builds reusable software verification libraries by generalizing code and its associated theorem families from successful verification tasks, improving both verification success rates and proof completion times.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manually turning verified code into reusable libraries is costly. Existing verification solutions lack automation to generalize both implementations and their dependent proofs, limiting library reuse and efficiency.

Method: VeriBase treats verified code and its theorem families as declaration families, uses a three-phase pipeline (prove seed tasks, generalize code and theorems, harvest families into shared interfaces), updates dependent declarations on interface changes, and validates the generated code and proofs with Lean.

Result: Evaluated on four benchmarks with three LLMs, VeriBase built a general library from 4,191 independent programs achieving up to +17.0% average task success and up to 21.4% reduction in proof completion time. Constructing benchmark‑specific libraries yields further gains.

Conclusion: VeriBase demonstrates that automated generalization of verification solutions into reusable libraries can significantly boost both the success rate and performance of future verification tasks.

Abstract: Software verification is typically more challenging than coding. Software developers routinely build on mature libraries and APIs, but turning successful verification solutions into reusable libraries still requires substantial expert effort. In this paper, we propose VeriBase, a framework for automatically constructing software-verification libraries by jointly generalizing code and its associated theorems from successful verification solutions. The key challenge is to generalize implementations together with the theorem statements and proofs that depend on these implementations, and reconcile overlapping components across verification tasks. To this end, VeriBase treats code and its associated theorems as declaration families, and designs a three-phase pipeline, which proves seed tasks, generalizes code and associated theorems, and harvests the resulting families into shared interfaces. VeriBase updates dependent declarations as interface change and uses Lean to check the resulting code and proofs. We evaluate VeriBase across four benchmarks with three backend LLMs. The results show that the general-purpose library built from independent 4,191 programs increases the average task success rate by up to 17.0% and meanwhile reduces the proof completion time by up to 21.4%. When VeriBase constructs a benchmark-specific library, further improvement can be observed.

</details>


### [101] [RECEIPT: Measuring and Repairing the Return Assumption of LLM Agents in Web Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01930)
*Boyang Yang,Ziyao Yang,Yu Zhang,Duo Chai,Xiaoming Chen,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了 RECEIPT 机制，解决 LLM 代理在 Web 基础设施操作中因工具调用返回假设失效导致的可靠性问题。通过 Litmus 和 Tripwire 基准测试，发现 11.5% 的工具调用因输入关闭而未执行操作，7/8 工具调用器关闭输入。RECEIPT 通过保持输入打开并读取进程等待状态，显著降低了虚假成功率（减少 73.5%）并使所有受保护任务在第二个工具调用器上全部完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理在执行 Web 基础设施命令时，依赖于 '返回假设'——即每个工具调用在有界时间内完成请求操作。然而，许多命令需要先提出问题，导致代理的运行时要么关闭输入（程序返回状态 0 而不执行操作），要么保持输入打开（等待超时，消耗时间和 token）。这直接影响代理的可靠性和效率。

Method: 研究者收集了 91,469 条生产工具调用和 89,047 条公开代理轨迹，测试了 8 个常用工具调用器。构建了 Litmus（158 个命令行调用的检查目录）和 Tripwire（25 个仅在是否有问题前缀不同的任务对基准测试）。提出了 RECEIPT 机制：保持输入打开，读取每个进程通过系统调用等待什么，并通过模型检查协议将此状态返回给未修改的代理。

Result: 在 Litmus 上，关闭输入导致 11.5% 的测试调用未执行请求操作。7/8 个工具调用器在无终端时关闭输入。未回答的问题在普通终端中使任务的 token 使用量增加 2.9 倍。输入连接使代理从 8 个工具调用器中的第 7 位提升到第 3 位。RECEIPT 在 5 个 LLM 上将虚假成功率降低 73.5%，并在第二个工具调用器上完成所有 25 个受保护任务。

Conclusion: 代理的可靠性不仅取决于 LLM 本身，还取决于其工具调用器暴露给代理的观测信息。研究表明，工具调用器应修复和报告输入连接问题，以便更准确地评估和排名 LLM。

Abstract: LLM agents operate Web infrastructure from the command line and rely on a return assumption, namely that each tool call returns within a bounded time and with its requested action finished. Many commands, however, ask a question before they act, and the agent's harness either closes the program's input or keeps it open as a terminal at which nobody types. With closed input, the program can return status 0 without the requested action. With open input, it waits until a time limit, and the agent pays in time and tokens. To the best of our knowledge, this paper is the first to measure and repair the return assumption. We collect 91,469 production tool calls and 89,047 public agent trajectories, test how 8 widely used harnesses launch commands, and build Litmus, a catalog of 158 checked command-line invocations, and Tripwire, a benchmark of 25 task pairs that differ only in whether a question guards the requested action. We then propose RECEIPT, which keeps the input open, reads what each process waits for from system calls, and returns this state to an unmodified agent through a model-checked protocol.
  On Litmus, closed input causes 11.5% of tested invocations to return status 0 without performing the requested action, and 7 of 8 harnesses close input when no terminal is attached. An unanswered question at a plain terminal multiplies a task's token use by 2.9, and the input connection moves an LLM from 7th to 3rd place among 8. Across 5 LLMs, RECEIPT reduces false claims of success by 73.5% and completes all 25 guarded tasks on a second harness. An agent's reliability therefore depends on what its harness lets it observe as well as on its LLM. Agent builders should report what each tool call did, not only how it ended, and benchmark maintainers should fix and report the input connection before they rank LLMs.

</details>


### [102] [Launder-Bench: Can Repository-Level Agentic Code Laundering Be Detected?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01966)
*Yuntong Hu,Hongji Pu,Zimo Li,Ruomeng Li,Xiaotong Liu,Ziyuan Qin,Qiyue Li,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文定义了一种新的“代理代码洗白”问题，并发布了一个名为 Launder‑Bench 的 repository‑level 基准测试，包含 282 个真实仓库。研究了两个互补的设置：对 repository 对进行来源验证的 Launder‑Bench‑Pair，以及对候选来源进行归属验证的 Launder‑Bench‑Attr。结果显示，现有静态方法在 pairwise 检测方面的 AUROC 仅为 0.66，而仓库编码器最多只能在 22% 的归属查询中将正确的来源排在第一位，但只有 1-2% 的查询获得了校准的归属积分，这表明在代理重写中，相对代码相似性与特定来源证据之间存在着系统性的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的克隆基准测试和功能正确性基准测试无法有效评估代理代码重写后的软件来源可追溯性。鉴于编码代理现在能够重写整个代码库，同时保持已验证的行为，这对代码相似性检测、许可证审计和软件来源追踪提出了新的挑战。

Method: 提出了“代理代码洗白”的概念，并构建了 Launder‑Bench 基准测试，包含 282 个真实仓库，并包括两个互补的子基准测试：Launder‑Bench‑Pair 用于 repository 对的来源验证，Launder‑Bench‑Attr 用于从候选来源中进行来源归属。引入了一种“源隐藏重建协议”，该协议通过执行验证来分解源代码行为、重建模块结构和仓库布局，仅保留经过验证的重写操作。此外，还建立了一个独立仓库集合，用于测量无共享来源时的相似性水平，并为不同的检测方法提供参考区间。

Result: 在 pairwise 检测中，最强大的静态方法仅达到了 0.66 AUROC；在来源归属方面，仓库编码器最多只能在 22% 的查询中将正确的来源排在第一位，但仅有 1-2% 的查询获得了校准的归属积分。独立仓库的测试结果显示，即使在无共享来源的情况下，也会出现相当大的代码相似性，表明存在严重的来源证据与相对相似性之间的差距。

Conclusion: Launder‑Bench 揭示了代理代码重写下软件来源追踪的复杂性，并展示了当前方法存在的显著局限性。它的发布为研究人员提供了一个可重复的目标，有助于缩小相对代码相似性与特定来源证据之间的差距。

Abstract: Coding agents can now rewrite repository-scale codebases while preserving tested behavior, creating a new challenge for plagiarism detection, license auditing, and software provenance. We ask whether such rewrites can still be traced to their source. Existing clone benchmarks emphasize fragments and bounded edits, while repository-transformation benchmarks evaluate functional correctness rather than provenance. Moreover, ranking the most similar source does not establish derivation when independent implementations can be similarly close. We define repository-level agentic code laundering and introduce Launder-Bench, to our knowledge the first repository-level benchmark for provenance under agentic rewriting, constructed from 282 real-world repositories. A source-hidden reconstruction protocol decomposes source behavior, reconstructs modules and repository structure, and retains rewrites only after execution-based verification. We evaluate the same provenance problem in two complementary settings: Launder-Bench-Pair contains 1,128 pairwise instances asking whether two repositories share provenance, while Launder-Bench-Attr contains 229 source-attribution instances asking which candidate is the source, with abstention when evidence is insufficient. Measured same-domain independent repositories establish how much similarity can arise without shared provenance and provide detector-specific reference bands. Existing methods remain far from saturating Launder-Bench: the strongest static method reaches only 0.66 AUROC on pairwise detection, while repository encoders rank the true source first on up to 22% of attribution queries but earn calibrated attribution credit on only 1-2%. These results reveal a systematic gap between relative code similarity and source-specific provenance evidence, providing a reproducible target for provenance detection under agentic rewriting.

</details>


### [103] [Semantic Interoperability in Federated Digital Twin Systems: A Systematic Review Through the Levels of Conceptual Interoperability Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02125)
*Manonmani Sekar,Nasim Nezamoddini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper is a PRISMA 2020 systematic review that identifies a fundamental gap in run-time semantic interoperability between independently owned digital twins. Specifically, no published system in the 32 included studies actually detects or repairs a broken mapping while the twins operate at runtime. All 32 studies fix mappings before operation, and no study combines both cross-company change detection and cross-company repair. The authors derive six requirements for any future system claiming run-time semantic interoperability between independently owned twins.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To establish whether the literature supports systems that detect and repair broken mappings between independently owned digital twins at runtime, thereby ensuring continued semantic interoperability during operation.

Method: PRISMA 2020 systematic review protocol: screened 5,833 records, read 119 full texts, included 32 studies. Analysis focused on how each study handles the mapping between digital twins and whether run-time detection and repair mechanisms exist.

Result: No included study implements both cross-company change detection and cross-company repair. Mappings are fixed before operation, and run-time repair exists only as isolated features—either matching without validation (cross-company) or within-company detection/repair. The gap yields six derived requirements for future systems.

Conclusion: Current published systems do not provide run-time semantic interoperability; only pre-operation fix mappings and isolated repair mechanisms exist. Future research must implement six requirements to claim run-time semantic interoperability.

Abstract: When two companies connect their digital twins, software models of their plants kept up to date with live data, each twin must translate the other's data into its own terms, and that translation rule, called a mapping, can stop being correct when one company changes the meaning of its data without telling the other. This PRISMA 2020 systematic review screened 5,833 records and read 119 full texts to determine whether any published system detects such a broken mapping and repairs it while the twins operate. In all 32 included studies the mapping is fixed before operation, and the two ingredients of run-time repair appear only separately: cross-company matching without any check that the match still holds, and detection and repair of changed meaning within a single company. The review turns this gap into six requirements, each illustrated on one running example, that a system claiming run-time semantic interoperability between independently owned twins should meet.

</details>


### [104] [Built on Broken Assumptions (BOBA): Why Coding Agents Cannot Be Left Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02207)
*Alejandro Cuadron,Arpit Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究构建了BOBA-Bench基准测试，包含来自140个真实开源项目的280条连续20个请求的链，对10个编码代理进行了评估。结果表明，编码代理在单个请求上的完成率高达95%，但在连续链式请求中骤降至51%。主要瓶颈并非代码质量，而是代理无法通过自测和自我审查发现潜在错误。引入外部验证机制可显著提升链式任务的完成率（至59%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估编码代理处理单个、独立请求的能力，而忽略了它们在实际应用中需要处理连续、相互依赖的请求链的场景。由于代理在完成一个请求后可能留下未被发现的错误，这些错误会累积并影响后续请求，因此需要专门的基准来评估链式工作流中代理的可靠性及对验证的依赖性。

Method: 研究构建了BOBA-Bench，包含280条长度为20的真实开源项目请求链。实验对比了10个编码代理在单任务与链式任务中的表现，评估了代理生成代码作为后续基础的质量，测试了代理的自我审查准确率，并探索了引入外部反馈（“那错了”）加一轮修复对链式任务完成率的提升效果。

Result: 1. 编码代理在单个请求上的平均完成率为95%，但在链式请求中仅为51%。2. 代理生成的代码质量并非主要问题，一旦经过检查，其作为后续基础的质量与人类代码相当。3. 链式任务失败的主因是代理自测漏检的错误，导致后续请求失败概率翻倍。4. 代理自我审查能力极差，准确率仅53%。5. 外部验证和修复能将链式完成率从51%提升至59%。

Conclusion: 编码代理的可靠性本质上受限于其可触及的验证机制。在缺乏有效外部验证的情况下，代理无法确保每一步的正确性，导致错误累积。为了使编码代理在长链自主开发任务中更具实用性，必须建立完善的外部验证与即时修复反馈机制。

Abstract: Coding agents are autonomous developers. Given a request, they change the code and report back when done. As such, they can handle not only independent requests but whole chains of them, each request built on the last. Agents are known to report wrong work as done, yet benchmarks still test them one request at a time, on human-written code, checked once. We build BOBA-Bench, 280 chains of 20 consecutive requests drawn from 140 real open-source projects, and evaluate ten coding agents on both. All ten do far worse on chains. On average, they complete 95% of requests taken one at a time, but when requests are chained together, they complete 51%. Worse code is not the cause. Once checked, agent code is nearly as good a base for later requests as human code. The cause is the mistakes agents miss. Coding agents declare a request done once their own tests pass, even when those tests miss a mistake. The next request then builds on broken code and fails about twice as often. The mistake is rarely found later. Nor can agents catch such mistakes by reviewing code. Asked whether an implementation is correct, they are right 53% of the time, barely better than a coin flip. Coding agents are therefore heavily dependent on access to verification. A single external ``that's wrong'' after a failed request and one repair round lift chain completion from 51% to 59%, and a repaired mistake no longer hurts the requests that follow. In simpler terms, coding agents are only as reliable as the checks they can reach: without verification, every request that follows is built on broken assumptions.

</details>


### [105] [Modern Software Engineering with Verification Agents: A Spec-First Architecture for Autonomous Synthesis, Verification IRs, and Organizational Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02285)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种端到端架构，用于自主编码代理同时合成实现代码、形式化规格和机械证明。核心创新在于建立了规格优先的栈反转、零代码审查工作流、验证中间表示（VIR）和高阶抽象语法（HOAS）等技术，实现了形式化规格成为主要的人工审查产物，而源代码和证明脚本作为可丢弃的构建输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当自主编码代理同时合成实现代码、形式化规格和机械证明时，软件工程层级发生反转：形式化规格成为主要的人工审查产物，而源代码和证明脚本成为可丢弃的构建输出。研究旨在解决当前形式化验证与工程实践之间的鸿沟，实现更高效的协作和验证流程。

Method: 论文提出了以下关键技术：1) 逻辑无关的基础架构，结合规格优先的栈反转、零代码审查工作流（由自动化持续集成检查门控）、以及通过薄、可验证的翻译器降低的最小验证中间表示（VIR）；2) 将扩展计算类型理论（CTT）规格层与高阶抽象语法（HOAS）和线性资源类型在VIR中结合，消除合成代理的重写摩擦；3) 利用个人规格副驾驶和共享合成舰队来跨团队协作，检测跨团队语义合并冲突，并将模块化合同融合为零拷贝二进制文件。

Result: 通过在零拷贝认证消息路由器上演示，该架构实现了多团队协作的有效性。与盐（salt）、SaltBench、x86lean和MetaPRL等现有基准相比，提出的组件展示了更好的性能和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作为自主编码代理在形式化验证软件工程中的应用提供了完整的端到端解决方案，实现了规格优先的工作流和高效的跨团队协作，推动了形式化方法在实际软件开发中的落地。

Abstract: When autonomous coding agents synthesize implementation code, formal specifications, and mechanical proofs simultaneously, the software engineering hierarchy inverts: the formal specification becomes the primary human-reviewed artifact, while source code and proof scripts become disposable build outputs. This paper presents an end-to-end architecture for agent-authored, formally verified software engineering. First, we establish a logic-agnostic foundation combining a spec-first stack inversion, a zero-code-review workflow gated by automated continuous integration (CI) checks, and a minimal Verification Intermediate Representation (VIR) lowered via a thin, verified translator. Second, we pair an extensional Computational Type Theory (CTT) specification layer with Higher-Order Abstract Syntax (HOAS) and linear resource typing in the VIR--eliminating rewrite friction for synthesis agents while defining an experiment, its protocol frozen before the first reader, to measure whether removing boundary coercions improves human specification readability. Third, we scale collaboration across multi-team organizations using Personal Spec-Copilots and a Shared Synthesis Fleet that detects cross-team Semantic Merge Conflicts and fuses modular contracts into zero-copy binaries. We illustrate this architecture on a zero-copy authenticated message router and distinguish our existing empirical baselines (salt, SaltBench, x86lean, and MetaPRL) from newly proposed components.

</details>


### [106] [APG: Agentic Static-Analysis Infrastructure on LLVM-IR Graph Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02349)
*Zexin Zhong,Yanjun Zhang,Yulei Sui,Xiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: APG is a semantics‑grounded static analysis infrastructure for LLM coding agents, built on SVF and LLVM, that decomposes compiler analyses into 38 atomic capabilities, achieving high query success and superior repair performance while cutting token usage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM‑based coding agents need reliable external program knowledge; static‑analysis graphs alone are insufficient for selecting appropriate operations or interpreting evidence, so a fine‑grained, semantics‑driven analysis framework is required.

Method: APG extends SVF and LLVM to break down compiler‑derived program analyses into 38 atomic capabilities that LLM agents can select, compose, and orchestrate, with query safeguards and traceable evidence for inspectable provenance.

Result: 35 of 38 capabilities reach >50% first‑attempt query‑generation success (13 at 100%); on 812 C/C++ defects, APG outperforms baselines, improving first‑iteration repair success by 17.6–18.0 percentage points and reducing LLM input tokens by 90.8%.

Conclusion: APG enhances LLM coding agents' program repair abilities by providing precise, composable static analysis primitives, leading to higher success rates and more efficient token usage.

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents increasingly rely on external program knowledge, yet access to static-analysis graphs alone does not ensure appropriate operation selection or evidence interpretation. We present APG, an infrastructure for semantics-grounded static program analysis. Built on SVF and LLVM, it decomposes compiler-derived program analyses into atomic capabilities that LLM agents can select, compose, and orchestrate for goal-directed, cross-graph investigation, with query safeguards and evidence traces preserving inspectable results and provenance. APG provides 38 atomic capabilities. On analysis requests arising during benchmark repair runs, 35 achieve first-attempt query-generation success rates above 50%, including 13 at 100%. Evaluation on 812 C/C++ defects shows higher repair success than the evaluated baselines. Compared with Passive Graph Push, APG improves first-iteration repair success by 17.6 and 18.0 percentage points on the two benchmark collections and reduces average LLM input-token consumption by 90.8%. Our tool is available at https://github.com/JasonZhongZexin/APG-ICSE-Demo and its demonstration video at https://youtu.be/vkuExZUjGnw

</details>


### [107] [Toward an Evidence-Based Template for Agent Instruction Files: An Empirical Study of Adoption, Consistency, and Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02383)
*Mehedi Hasan Shanto,Md. Anaytul Islam,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Alioune Ngom*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper studied 3,387 instruction files from 1,000 GitHub repos to understand how different AI coding agent formats (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, Copilot instructions, Cursor rules) are used, maintained, and whether they provide consistent guidance. Key findings include: 42.2% of high-popularity repos with instruction files have multiple formats; 16.1% of corresponding sections across 386 manual pairs provided divergent guidance; 40.4% of files have only one recorded modification; a new evidence-based template (10 Required + 4 Gap + 2 Recommended categories) raised structural coverage from 18.7% to 82.8%; RAG achieved the best ROUGE-1 F1 score of 0.459; 19.4% of generated instances fully preserved developer intent and 61.2% partially preserved it.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding how AI coding agents are instructed and maintained helps improve their performance and consistency. The study addresses the lack of empirical research on which instruction formats dominate, whether they stay consistent, and how they are maintained over time.

Method: Data collection across four agent-specific instruction formats, manual comparison of corresponding sections for divergence, maintenance pattern analysis, category prevalence and addition ratio analysis to derive a template, an exploratory generation experiment using retrieval-augmented generation evaluated by structural category coverage and content alignment (ROUGE-1 F1), and human evaluation of generated instances preserving developer intent.

Result: 42.2% of higher-popularity repos maintain multiple instruction formats (67.8% have at least two formats); 16.1% divergence rate among 386 manually compared pairs; the derived template improved structural category coverage from 18.7% to 82.8%; RAG achieved highest full-file ROUGE-1 F1 of 0.459 versus other generation configurations; 19.4% full preservation and 61.2% partial preservation of intent in generated instances.

Conclusion: The paper provides empirical findings on instruction-file adoption, maintenance, format consistency, and content structure that inform how AI coding agents can be better instructed and their instruction files more effectively structured and maintained.

Abstract: AI coding agents rely on repository-level instruction files to understand project conventions, development workflows, and constraints. However, different agents support different instruction formats, and limited empirical evidence exists about how these files are adopted, what information they contain, whether coexisting formats provide consistent guidance, and how they are maintained. We conduct an empirical study of 3,387 instruction files from 1,000 GitHub repositories across four formats: AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, GitHub Copilot instructions, and Cursor rules. We find that 42.2% of instruction-bearing repositories in the higher-popularity cohort maintain multiple formats. Among 386 manually examined pairs of corresponding sections, 16.1% provide divergent guidance. Our maintenance analysis further shows that 40.4% of the analyzed files have only one recorded modification event and that maintenance patterns differ across formats. Based on category prevalence and category addition ratios (CAR), we derive an evidence-based template containing ten Required, four Gap, and two Recommended categories. In an exploratory generation experiment, explicit template guidance increases structural category coverage from 18.7% to 82.8%. We further introduce AGIT-Instruct, a benchmark for evaluating instruction-file generation through structural coverage and content alignment. Across four generation configurations, retrieval-augmented generation achieves the highest full-file ROUGE-1 F1 of 0.459. A manual assessment of 278 generated category instances finds that 19.4% fully preserve and 61.2% partially preserve developer-written intent. Our findings provide empirical guidance for organizing and maintaining agent instruction

</details>


### [108] [uTECH Turbo-MOVES: The Intersection of Software Architectural Innovation and Sustainable Mobility Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03321)
*Cafer Avcı,Alireza Yazdiani,Roopak Srinivasan,Megh Khaire,Mohammad Tayarani,H. Oliver Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了uTECH Turbo-MOVES软件架构，旨在不修改MOVES核心计算模块的前提下，通过容器化、内存中间存储和并发编排等技术大幅加速机动车排放模拟器（MOVES）的计算，从而支持更大规模的环境评估与交通政策分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通运输是能源消耗和温室气体排放的主要来源，精准的排放估算对环境规划和政策分析至关重要。但EPA的MOVES模拟器计算需求极高，限制了大规模情景分析的开展，因此亟需提升计算效率。

Method: 提出uTECH Turbo-MOVES架构，通过结合RunSpec分区、容器隔离、内存中间存储和并发工作流编排，在完全不修改MOVES Calculator模块及输入输出结构的前提下，实现计算任务的并行化与高效执行。

Result: 在六个涵盖不同尺度（县、默认、项目）和模式（排放清单、排放率）的基准测试中，该架构在默认尺度工作负载上实现了5.5倍实测和15.3倍估计的单次运行加速，并在八县排放率分析中实现了84.8倍的活动期加速。

Conclusion: uTECH Turbo-MOVES显著提升了MOVES的计算效率，虽然加速比会因工作负载和硬件配置而异，但它为开展更广泛、更深入的环境评估和交通政策评估提供了强有力的技术支撑。

Abstract: Transportation is a major source of energy consumption, greenhouse gas emissions, and criteria air pollutants, making efficient emissions estimation essential for environmental planning and policy analyses. The U.S. Environmental Protection Agency's Motor Vehicle Emission Simulator (MOVES) supports detailed emissions modeling, but its computational demands constrain large-scale scenario analyses. This study introduces uTECH Turbo-MOVES, a software architecture that expedites MOVES computation without modifying its Calculator modules or input/output structure. By combining RunSpec partitioning, container isolation, in-memory intermediate storage, and concurrent workload orchestration, it improves execution efficiency. We evaluate six benchmark cases spanning County, Default, and Project scales and both Emission Inventory and Emission Rates modes. Results demonstrate a measured 5.5x and an estimated 15.3x per-run speedup for Default-scale workloads, and an 84.8x campaign speedup for an eight-county Emission Rates analysis. Although gains vary by workload and hardware configuration, uTECH Turbo-MOVES enables more extensive environmental assessments and transportation policy evaluation.

</details>


### [109] [Fault-Free Profiles and Decision-Level Training for Small-Model Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02416)
*Kyoohyun Lee,Woohyeok Park,Taeyoon Kim,Sungyong Baik,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对微服务系统根因分析中小语言模型易受异常分数偏差影响、难以区分常规波动与真实故障的问题，提出基于历史频率画像与偏差决策纠正的方法，用4B模型在多个数据集上达到最高或并列最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化诊断流程虽能提升SLM能力，但诊断分析不等于正确根因识别。实证发现模型最终决策往往偏向异常分数大的信号，即使传播分析表明否则；且输入未揭示这些信号在正常运行中是否常见，导致误判。

Method: 提出历史频率画像（Historical Frequency Profiling），统计故障期内指标与日志信号被标记为异常的频率，为区分常规偏差与故障证据提供上下文；提出偏差决策纠正（Biased-Decision Correction），识别模型从相同初始分析得出不同结论的决策点，并采用偏好优化使其倾向更准确的诊断。

Result: 与14B OpsAgent及商业模型RCA-Agent等基线相比，4B模型系统在四个数据集上均达到最高或并列最高准确率，Bank数据集达48.7%，显著高于OpsAgent的18.5%。

Conclusion: 历史频率上下文与偏好优化纠偏能有效提升小语言模型在微服务根因分析中的证据评估与根因选择能力，证明小模型在成本与隐私约束下可实现与甚至超过更大模型的诊断性能。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice systems requires distinguishing failure sources from routine fluctuations and propagated symptoms. Small language models (SLMs) offer a practical solution under cost and privacy constraints, and recent approaches improve their capabilities through structured diagnostic procedures. However, performing diagnostic analysis does not ensure correct root-cause identification. Our empirical study shows that final decisions often favor large anomaly scores despite the model's own propagation analysis suggesting otherwise. Moreover, the input does not reveal whether these apparently anomalous signals are common during normal operation. These findings motivate our system, which improves how SLMs assess evidence and select root causes through Historical Frequency Profiling and Biased-Decision Correction. Historical Frequency Profiling quantifies how often metric and log signals are flagged as anomalous in the fault-free period, providing context for distinguishing routine deviations from potential failure evidence. Biased-Decision Correction identifies points where the model reaches different conclusions from the same initial analysis and uses preference optimization to favor more accurate diagnoses. Against baselines including the 14B OpsAgent and RCA-Agent with commercial models, our system with a 4B model achieves the highest or tied-highest accuracy on all four datasets, reaching 48.7% on Bank compared with 18.5% for OpsAgent.

</details>


### [110] [DistDebug-Bench: Can LLM Agents Diagnose Bugs in Distributed Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03523)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Chenglin Liu,John Rose,Mason Miller,Ruthesh Thavamani,Juntao Wu,Yinwei Huang,Jiahao Xu,Peng Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了 DistDebug-Bench 基准测试，用于评估 LLM 代理在代码级别诊断分布式系统复杂故障的能力，包含 120 个来自 11 个真实系统的 bug，并揭示了当前代理存在的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要集中在小规模、自包含的 Python 模块（如竞争编程），而对分布式系统这一关键生产软件类别中的复杂代码级故障诊断缺乏充分评估。

Method: 构建了包含 120 个高质量真实世界 bug 的数据集，源自 11 个广泛使用的分布式系统，涵盖 5 种编程语言和 10 种故障模式，并对先进的 LLM 和代理进行了系统性评估。

Result: 评估发现当前代理与真实调试任务之间存在显著差距，主要表现为因缺乏明确推理结构导致的‘隧道视野’，以及因工具使用范围不一致导致的非确定性问题。

Conclusion: DistDebug-Bench 为分布式系统调试提供了标准化的评估平台，指出了当前代理在推理和工具使用上的局限，为未来研究指明了改进方向，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: LLM agents show strong potential in software engineering tasks such as competitive programming and code synthesis. Existing benchmarks, however, largely focus on small-scale settings such as self-contained Python modules, leaving distributed systems, an essential class of production software, under-examined. We introduce DistDebug-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating agents' ability to diagnose complex-system failures at the code level. It curates 120 high-quality real-world bugs from 11 widely used distributed systems, spanning 5 programming languages and 10 failure patterns. Through an extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs and agents, we identify substantial gaps between current agents and real-world debugging tasks. A recurring gap is tunnel vision due to a lack of explicit reasoning structure and nondeterminism from inconsistent tool-use scope. The benchmark and evaluator are available at https://github.com/OrderLab/distdebug-bench, with agent traces and a leaderboard at https://distdebug.com.

</details>


### [111] [Harvesting SWE Benchmarks from Wild Vibe Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02440)
*Ao Lu,Yuanhao Zeng,Shaocheng Lan,Sijie Xu,Jiacheng Bao,Kuang Yan,Heng Lin,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SessionFarmer is an automated framework that transforms collaborative coding sessions (vibe coding) into self-contained, executable tasks. It processes dialogues and commit histories to isolate relevant work episodes, reconstructs hermetic environments, generates task statements, rubrics, and unit tests, and refines tests using mutants and blind solvers. The framework is evaluated on CropChat (242 tasks from public repos) and CropEnterprise (proprietary logs), showing high developer endorsement, improved test accuracy, and significant variations in solver performance depending on harness configuration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing coding agent evaluations often rely on static repository snapshots or isolated single-turn interactions, which fail to capture the dynamic, multi-session nature of real-world developer-agent collaboration. The need for reproducible, realistic software engineering tasks that reflect evolving requirements and multi-turn conversations drives the development of SessionFarmer.

Method: SessionFarmer operates in several stages: (1) it filters out unrelated sessions from conversational and commit logs; (2) it groups multi-turn, potentially multi-session interactions into coherent work episodes centered on a single engineering goal; (3) for each episode it creates a hermetic execution container; (4) it synthesizes a detailed task statement, engineering-quality rubrics, and repository-grounded unit tests; (5) it refines tests via requirement-breaking mutants and a blind solver to reduce false rejections and false acceptances. The framework produces two benchmarks: CropChat (open, 242 tasks across 61 repos, 14 languages) and CropEnterprise (proprietary logs).

Result: In a CropEnterprise audit, original developers largely endorse the reconstructed episode boundaries, task statements, and quality rubrics. Full-episode context captures substantially more user requirements than single-turn or single-session inputs. Test refinement lowers false-rejection rates and raises balanced accuracy by 7‑9 percentage points across solvers. On CropChat, the top model‑harness configuration achieves 39.3% Pass@1, and harness choice can shift Pass@1 by up to 8.4 percentage points for the same model.

Conclusion: SessionFarmer successfully converts real-world collaborative coding records into executable, reproducible tasks that better reflect actual software engineering workflows. The resulting benchmarks enable more accurate evaluation of coding agents, highlight the importance of multi-session context, and demonstrate that thoughtful test refinement and harness selection can significantly improve model performance.

Abstract: Development teams need reproducible tasks from their own repositories and workflows to evaluate coding agents. Conversational developer-agent collaboration ("vibe coding") produces abundant coding sessions and repository checkpoints, but requirements evolve across turns, unrelated conversations co-occur with commits, and engineering efforts can span multiple sessions. We present SessionFarmer, an automated framework that turns these records into self-contained, executable software engineering tasks. It analyzes dialogue and commit history to filter unrelated sessions and group multi-turn, potentially multi-session interactions into work episodes centered on one engineering goal. For each episode, it reconstructs a hermetic execution container, synthesizes a task statement from the full context, and authors repository-grounded engineering-quality rubrics and unit tests. Closed-loop test refinement uses requirement-breaking mutants and a blind solver to reduce false rejections of valid alternatives and false acceptances of incomplete solutions. We construct CropChat, an open benchmark of 242 tasks across 61 public repositories and 14 programming languages, and CropEnterprise from proprietary enterprise logs. In a CropEnterprise audit, the original developers endorse the vast majority of reconstructed episode boundaries, task statements, and quality rubrics. Full-episode context covers substantially more user requirements than single-turn or single-session inputs. Test refinement cuts false-rejection rates and raises balanced accuracy against reference patch labels by 7-9 percentage points across solvers. Across 12 model-harness configurations on CropChat, the strongest setup achieves 39.3% Pass@1, while harness choice shifts Pass@1 by up to 8.4 percentage points for the same model.

</details>


### [112] [When Neither Side Confirms: Destructive Tool Approval Across MCP Servers and Hosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02527)
*Junwei Yao,Zike Yang,Zhijun Wang,Yongxi Zhou,Wenbo Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Most destructive MCP tools lack server‑side human confirmation and many host SDKs default to no approval, leading to a high risk of unauthorized deletions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The MCP specification requires a human to deny tool calls, but its implementation is split and often missing, creating security gaps that LLM agents can exploit.

Method: Analyzed source code of 1,000 MCP repositories, used a rule‑based extractor to identify destructive tools, evaluated 41 open‑source hosts and SDKs, and performed injection tests with four SDKs and five low‑cost models across 960 runs.

Result: About 90% (95% CI 82‑95) of destructive tools have no server‑side confirmation; 60.7% of tool‑host pairs lack enforced human approval; 479 of 960 injection runs (≈50%) caused deletion, which would drop to 5.4% with per‑tool prompting and 56.7% with universal elicitation.

Conclusion: Applications should configure explicit human‑approval mechanisms for destructive tools to mitigate the risk.

Abstract: LLM agents call tools that delete data or move money through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The specification asks for a human who can deny tool calls but splits the mechanism between the server, which can annotate a tool or ask the user through the host (elicitation), and the host application, which decides whether to prompt. We measure both sides from source code. In a 1,000-repository sample of the official MCP registry, an estimated 90% of destructive tools (95% CI 82-95) have no server-side confirmation, and most servers that elicit proceed, or fall back to a flag the model can set, when the host cannot relay the question. Of 41 popular open-source hosts and agent SDKs, 32 have no approval step by default, including 16 of 25 applications. Pairing sampled destructive tools flagged by a rule-based extractor with these applications, 60.7% of pairs have no enforced human approval by default. Through four agent SDKs, four of five low-cost models executed an injected deletion in 479 of 960 runs. If hosts prompted for every tool not annotated read-only or non-destructive, the unapproved share of these pairs would fall to 5.4%; universal elicitation support would leave 56.7%. Applications should therefore configure approval.

</details>


### [113] [CR-Bench-v2: Evaluating the Real-World Utility of AI Code Review Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02566)
*Kristen Pereira,Neelabh Sinha,Rajat Ghosh,Debojyoti Dutta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CR-Bench是一个面向缺陷的代码评审评估框架，将SWE-Bench问题重构为带完整仓库上下文的pull-request评审任务，包含584个实例，并提出已知缺陷检测率(KDDR)和有用性率两个指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化代码评审需求迫切，但现有基准缺乏标准化，依赖噪声较大的人工评审评论，且基于文本相似度的指标无法准确衡量评审精确性。

Method: 通过筛选可评审的缺陷并关联到单个pull request，构建包含真实修复和分类标签的数据集；使用三个独立LLM作为评判，评估8个代码评审代理及Gemini Code Assist的性能。

Result: 最强系统仅能检测约三分之一的已知缺陷(KDDR≈33%)，Gemini Code Assist的检测率为8.1%。

Conclusion: 当前代码评审代理性能有限，凸显改进空间，强调需要更精确的评估框架来推动自动化代码评审的发展。

Abstract: The rapid growth of automated code generation has created a pressing need for automated code review. Yet code review resists full automation: it depends on context beyond the \textit{diff}, on the author's intent, and on judgments about design and maintainability on which even expert reviewers disagree. Existing code review benchmarks lack standardization and rely largely on noisy human review comments scored with text-similarity metrics, which reward surface overlap and fail to measure precision. We introduce \texttt{CR-Bench}, a defect-centric evaluation framework that recasts SWE-Bench issues as pull-request review tasks with full repository context. After filtering for review-detectable defects and grounding each to a single originating pull request, \texttt{CR-Bench} contains 584 instances, with a 174-instance \texttt{CR-Bench-Verified} subset derived from SWE-Bench Verified. Each instance pairs a pull request with a known defect, its ground-truth fix, and a taxonomy label for bug category, impact, and severity. We propose two complementary metrics: \emph{Known-Defect Detection Rate} (KDDR), which measures whether an agent identifies the labeled defect, and \emph{Usefulness Rate}, which measures the fraction of review comments that are actionable. We evaluate eight code review agents over four backbones, plus commercially available Gemini Code Assist, using three independent LLM judges. Even the strongest systems detect only about one-third of known defects (KDDR $\approx$ 33\%), and Gemini Code Assist detects 8.1\%.

</details>


### [114] [Does the Judge See What the Evaluation Requires? Input Assurance for LLM Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02676)
*Boming Xia,Qinghua Lu,Runze Li,Dino Sejdinovic,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过定义四项输入保证义务并在30条轨迹的管道中进行集成测试，发现结构化工具调用缺失导致大量步骤空白，且不同judge配置下错误标记差异显著，恢复调用后差异显著下降，加入三项控制后大幅阻止故障案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要确定包是否满足评估要求的可靠证据，防止因渲染、检查、复制或读取顺序导致的评估失效。

Method: 提出四项输入保证义务（内容保存、检查覆盖、失败中止、最后读取），在包含30条轨迹的管道中进行实验，使用匹配哈希、检查器、集成测试及控制变量衡量差异和故障阻止率。

Result: 发现结构化调用被省略导致53/60步骤空白，不同配置下错误标记差异达13，恢复调用后差异降至2（每条轨迹降低0.37），三项控制在19个构造的故障案例中阻止18个。

Conclusion: 四项义务可转化为测试用例，需在独立管道中验证其可转移性以提升评估的可靠性。

Abstract: An LLM judge assesses agent steps from text packets. A renderer converts recorded runs into these packets, while software checkers test records and packets against requirements. We ask what evidence shows a packet is what the evaluation requires and propose four input-assurance obligations: required content survives rendering, checkers cover their requirements, a failed check stops later use and the judge reads the bytes last checked. In one pipeline with 30 trajectories, matching hashes, passing checkers and successful staging (copying packets for the judge) coexisted with violations of all four obligations, the last two in integration tests. The renderer omitted every structured tool call, leaving 53 of 60 judged steps blank despite matching hashes. For one judge configuration, later context raised error flags by 13 more on error than on correct steps. Restoring every call reduced this contrast to 2, a reduction of 11 (0.37 per trajectory, 95% cluster-bootstrap interval 0.17 to 0.53). In packets ending at the judged step, that configuration abstained at least once on every blank step, while the other four sometimes abstained but also flagged blank error and correct steps. Both separately written checkers accepted an unmatched tool result. In integration tests, a stub replacing the judge read a failed build's packet and a copy changed after staging. Three added controls stopped 18 of 19 constructed fault cases before the stub read any packet. The remaining stale script never ran. We turn the obligations into four tests and plan to evaluate their transfer to independent pipelines.

</details>


### [115] [IssueLighter: An Issue Type-Aware LLM Agent for Issue Report Validity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02721)
*Jiawei Ran,Yuxia Zhang,Ziyang Zhao,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了三個開源項目中520份issue報告，識別出13個導致issue無效的類型相關原因，並構建了一個基於LLM的智能體框架IssueLighter，通過類型感知策略和實例驗證來動態評估issue的有效性。實驗結果顯示，IssueLighter將F1分數平均提高了20個百分點以上，並生成了結構化但有充分證據支持的解釋，成功引導了對矛盾case進行校對。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究主要關注特定類型的無效issue（如bug和wontfix），導致對OSS issue類型的廣泛性理解不足，且解釋能力有限。在LLM智能體時代，如何綜合理解所有issue類型的無效原因，並構建具有解釋性的有效評估方法，對於緩解maintainers的工作負擔和預防 burnout 至關重要。

Method: 首先，手動標註了pandas, scikit-learn, 和 mypy 三個項目的520份issue，標記出有效/無效狀態。然後對無效issue進行主題分析，提取出13個與issue類型密切相關的無效原因。據此，設計了IssueLighter智能體框架，採用類型感知策略和實例驗證，動態執行命令以評估issue的有效性。

Result: 實驗結果表明，IssueLighter在F1分數上持續且顯著地優於所有baseline，平均提升超過20個百分點。減損實驗驗證了類型感知策略和無效原因分類對性能增益的貢獻。定性分析顯示，IssueLighter生成的解釋與準確識別的無效原因相符，且有證據支持。在四個爭議case中，利用生成的解釋對maintainers的原始判斷提出了質疑，並獲得了全部批准。

Conclusion: 本研究擴展了對OSS issue無效性的類型化理解，提出了IssueLighter框架，該框架不僅在有效性評估上表現優異，且提供具有解釋性的理性，增強了Maintainer的信任和決策能力。此方法有望減輕Maintainer的負擔，降低 burnout 風險，並提高開源項目問題管理的整體質量。

Abstract: Invalid issue reports are prevalent in open source software (OSS) projects, imposing substantial burdens on maintainers who already face a high risk of burnout. Prior research has primarily focused on identifying invalid reports for specific issue categories (e.g., bug and wontfix), leaving the broader landscape of OSS issue types largely unexplored and limiting the generalizability of existing approaches. Moreover, existing methods provide little support for explaining or validating their predictions, reducing their practical usefulness for maintainers. With the rise of LLM-based agents, it remains unclear whether a comprehensive understanding of the causes of invalidity across all issue types can improve validity assessment performance while producing well-grounded rationales. To this end, we first manually annotated the validity status of 520 issue reports from three OSS projects, i.e., pandas, scikit-learn, and mypy, resulting in 257 invalid reports and 263 valid ones. Then, we conducted a thematic analysis on these invalid reports and identified 13 causes of invalidity that are closely specific to issue types. Based on these insights, we propose IssueLighter, an agent-based framework with a type-aware validity assessment strategy that dynamically executes commands. Experimental results show that IssueLighter consistently and substantially outperforms all baseline methods, with an average F1 improvement of over 20 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm that both the type-aware strategy and the taxonomy of causes of invalidity contribute to the performance gains. Qualitative analysis further demonstrates that IssueLighter generates rationales grounded in accurately identified causes of invalidity and supported by evidence. Moreover, leveraging these rationales, we challenged maintainers' original decisions in four disputed cases, and all received approval.

</details>


### [116] [COVE: A Task-Adaptive, Code-Based Ensemble for Proactive Verification of LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03111)
*Anjaney Shrivastava,Dharmala Hrudai Aditya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出COVE，一种任务自适应、基于代码的集成框架，通过生成前预防错误与生成后用确定性纯Python代码验证结构和一致性，无需额外LLM调用即可显著提升文本和图像任务准确率并降低Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有验证方法是反应式的，仅在生成后检测幻觉，且常需额外昂贵的LLM调用进行校验，导致效率低下和成本高昂。

Method: COVE包含六项纯Python的确定性创新，分别用于生成前的错误预防和生成后的结构与一致性验证；文中提供伪代码、精确参数、各组件消融实验及企业部署经验。

Result: 在11个文本数据集（1,020条样本）和4个图像数据集（200条样本）上，文本准确率从45.2%提升至82.1%（相对提升81.7%），图像准确率提升69.5%，Token用量降低48%至96%（模拟成本模型），验证开销仅每输出0.26毫秒（不含LLM调用）。

Conclusion: 基于确定性代码的验证可在企业LLM流水线中以极低延迟和成本大幅降低幻觉，证明其比仅依赖LLM自我修正更实用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now embedded in enterprise workflows, yet their outputs remain prone to hallucinations: plausible-sounding but factually incorrect or malformed content. Most verification approaches are reactive, detecting errors only after generation and often spending additional expensive LLM calls to do so. We present COVE, a task-adaptive, code-based ensemble that takes the opposite stance: it prevents errors before generation and validates structure and consistency afterwards using six deterministic, pure-Python innovations that add no extra LLM calls. On an internal evaluation spanning 11 text datasets (1,020 examples) and 4 image datasets (200 examples), COVE lifts text accuracy from a 45.2% unverified baseline to 82.1% (an 81.7% relative improvement), improves image accuracy by 69.5%, and reduces token usage by 48 to 96% (simulated cost model), at a measured verification overhead of 0.26 ms per output (excluding the LLM call). This article describes the engineering design of each innovation (with pseudocode and the exact parameters we use), the measured impact, an ablation of every component, and the practical lessons learned from deploying code-based verification in enterprise pipelines.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Surface Metrics: Multi-Condition Requirements in Enterprise Maintenance and AI Coding Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03162)
*Heeyong Eun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定的18条标准分类法，对Apache Fineract真实问题与多个AI代码基准测试（如SWE-bench Pro等）进行对比分析，揭示了多条件满足在软件维护任务中的关键作用，发现表面相似的任务背后可能隐藏着截然不同的条件集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级AI编码基准主要衡量任务成功率，却忽略了完成软件维护所需的多条件满足及方向性依赖。本研究旨在量化并对比真实项目与基准测试中任务条件的广度、共现性及方向性关系，以揭示基准测试在捕获真实维护条件方面的局限性。

Method: 研究采用了固定的18条标准分类法，分析了2,925个Apache Fineract问题和944个来自SWE-bench Pro、SWE-PolyBench Java及SWE-EVO的任务。通过匹配1,177对在问题分类、修改文件数和代码变动量上相似的基准任务对，系统评估了条件广度、语料库级共现性以及源码支撑的方向性需求。

Result: 结果显示，匹配任务对的平均Jaccard距离为0.8138，57.4%的任务条件集完全不相交。时间条件与数值条件在Fineract中高度共现（phi系数为+0.430），而在基准测试中几乎不共现（-0.013）；在有时间标记的任务中，Fineract中48.85%包含数值条件，而基准测试中仅为5.81%。研究还在Fineract中记录了16个方向性需求关系，并通过四个基准案例进行了定性说明。

Conclusion: 表面相似的任务轮廓会掩盖实质不同的满足条件集。研究建议在评估基准中单独报告条件广度、共现性和方向性关系。需要注意的是，本研究的语料库对比主要为描述性分析，反映的是领域差异，并不能确立因果关系或生产环境的实际失败率。

Abstract: Repository-level AI coding benchmarks measure task success but do not directly capture the conditions required for software maintenance completion. We study multi-condition fulfillment, where a maintenance task must satisfy multiple operational conditions, sometimes with directional dependencies. Using a fixed 18-criterion taxonomy, we analyze 2,925 Apache Fineract issues and 944 tasks from SWE-bench Pro, SWE-PolyBench Java, and SWE-EVO. We examine condition breadth, corpus-level co-occurrence, and source-grounded directional requirement relationships.
  Among 1,177 benchmark task pairs matched on issue classification, modified-file count, and code churn, the mean fulfillment-set Jaccard distance is 0.8138, and 57.4% have disjoint condition sets. Condition count alone does not explain this contrast: SWE-bench Pro averages 2.47 conditions per task, versus 1.78 for Fineract. Temporal (B4) and numerical (B2) conditions have a phi coefficient of +0.430 in Fineract versus -0.013 in the pooled benchmark corpus. Among temporally tagged tasks, numerical conditions are also specified in 48.85% (254/520) of Fineract cases versus 5.81% (5/86) of benchmark cases.
  We document 16 directional relationships in Fineract requirements involving time, calculation, and state preservation, and inspect four benchmark cases to illustrate how relevant conditions appear in evaluation assertions. These examples do not quantify benchmark-wide oracle omissions. The results show that similar surface task profiles can mask substantially different fulfillment-condition sets. We recommend reporting condition breadth, co-occurrence, and directional relationships separately. The corpus comparisons are descriptive and may reflect domain differences; they do not establish causality or production failure rates.

</details>


### [118] [Finding the Pivots to Repair Broken Source-Test Evolution Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03197)
*Xiao Chen,Songqiang Chen,Jialun Cao,Yun Peng,Chun Yong Chong,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型的测试修复在源码-测试协同演化场景下的有效性进行了系统实证研究，构建了包含500个真实案例的基准，并揭示了关键洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着源码演化，测试用例常失效，需要修复以保证测试套件可靠性；虽然大语言模型有潜力，但有效上下文和修复流程尚不明确，且推理步骤的作用未知。

Method: 从15个高星开源Java项目的提交中收集500个真实源码-测试协同演化案例，设计多种实验设置，分析大语言模型行为，提取推理动作分类。

Result: 发现结合测试失败症状、源码变更和辅助代码上下文具有互补优势，自修复轮次有效；推理动作分类可指导大语言模型更高效修复，降低费用；应用于Codex代理后，困难案例修复成功率提升66.7%。

Conclusion: 本文为大语言模型驱动的测试修复提供了实践指导，强调上下文组合与推理引导的重要性，未来可进一步探索推理机制优化。

Abstract: Repairing obsolete tests in response to source code evolution is critical for test suite reliability and software quality assurance. While large language models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools for automating many software development tasks, the effective supply of context and the orchestration of repair pipelines in this source--test co-evolution scenario remain poorly understood. Moreover, although reasoning is found helpful for LLMs on some complex coding tasks, it remains unclear which reasoning steps actually contribute to effective test repairs and how the insights learned from LLMs' behaviors benefit coding agents. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of test repair for source-test co-evolution empowered by LLMs, investigating the effective setups and typical LLM behaviors. To support this study, we construct a new benchmark of 500 real-world source-test co-evolution cases from commits submitted between May 2024 and April 2026 across 15 highly-starred open-source Java projects. Our experimental results reveal several key insights for effective LLM-based test repair: the complementary benefits of combining test failure symptoms, source code changes, and supportive code context, as well as the effectiveness of self-repair rounds. We further derive a common reasoning action taxonomy from LLMs' reasoning traces, which proves instrumental in guiding LLMs to repair tests more effectively while at a lower financial cost. Applying our findings to the Codex agent further improves its repair success rate on challenging cases by 66.7%.

</details>


### [119] [Can Coding Agents Sustain Continual Evolution without Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03219)
*Shangziqi Zhao,Yitong Zhang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了代理 harness 的持续进化问题，发现跨任务进化会导致先前性能显著下降（灾难性遗忘），并给出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多聚焦单任务 agent 进化，实际场景中编码 agent 需处理多类任务，持续进化中是否出现灾难性遗忘仍是未解问题。

Method: 将三个软件工程任务数据集组织为六种进化方向，应用两种代表性 harness 进化方法进行持续进化实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，后续数据集进化会显著损害先前数据集性能。例如使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731，Meta-Harness 在 SWE-Bench 进化至 94.0%，但随后在 FeatureBench 进化后性能跌至 32.0%，第五轮后相对下降 66%。

Conclusion: 基于实验发现，识别出持续 self-evolution 的几条关键洞察，并指出了未来改进方向。代码已开源于 GitHub。

Abstract: Recently, harness self-evolution has emerged as an important paradigm for improving agent performance. Prior studies show that evolving the agent harness can effectively enhance agent capabilities without modifying the foundation model's parameters. We find that existing approaches largely study agent evolution on a single task. In practice, coding agents may need to handle multiple task types over time. Whether an agent harness can continuously evolve across multiple tasks without suffering from catastrophic forgetting remains an important open question.
  In this paper, we investigate this open question through a comprehensive empirical study of continual harness self-evolution. We organize three software engineering task datasets into six different evolution directions and apply two representative harness evolution methods to perform continual evolution along these directions. Our experimental results show that evolution on a subsequent dataset can substantially undermine the performance on previously seen datasets. For example, with DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, Meta-Harness improves its performance on SWE-Bench to 94.0% after evolution. However, after further evolution on FeatureBench, its performance on SWE-Bench drops to 32.0% by the fifth round, corresponding to a 66% relative decrease. Finally, based on our experimental findings, we identify several insights, providing directions for the continual self-evolution of agent harnesses. Code is available at https://github.com/THU-Agent/continual-harness-evolution.

</details>


### [120] [Specification-Driven Governance for Fairness-by-Design in Agentic Software Engineering: How Far Can We Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03231)
*Alfonso Cannavale,Alessandra Parziale,Valeria Pontillo,Patrizio Pelliccione,Andrea De Lucia,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 代理AI将开发者责任从软件实现转向治理，提高了生产力但挑战了意图保存。研究探讨规范驱动开发是否能在代理机器学习系统中实现公平性始终在场。通过三个代理和四个ML应用，使用渐进式公平性规范元素的提示，分析了公平性需求在生成 artifacts 中的可追踪性、准确-公平权衡以及代理决策。结果显示，代理在规范中捕获了近97.22%的公平性需求，并在代码中保留了82.41%，但模型并未显著变得更公平，公平性被当作事后审计而非训练约束，使得公平性可追踪而非可执行。研究呼吁规范需规定代理在需求未满足时应如何行动，以及使代理委托给开发者的公平性决策显式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI的兴起改变了开发者的角色，但现有方法在代理化的机器学习开发工作流中，对公平性的代表性和传递支持有限。有必要探索规范驱动开发是否能实现公平性始终在场。

Method: 选取三个代理AI系统，构建四个机器学习应用场景，设计四个 progressively operationalize fairness 的提示，涵盖九个公平性规范元素。追踪公平性需求在生成 artifacts 中的传播，分析模型的公平性-准确性权衡，并记录代理的设计决策与 rationale。

Result: 代理在规范中几乎捕获了所有公平性需求（97.22%），并保留了大部分在代码中（82.41%）。然而， resulting models 并未显著更公平，因为代理将公平性作为事后审计，既不影响训练也不阻止模型交付。公平性可追踪但不可执行。

Conclusion: 现有的公平性规范使得公平性可追踪，但难以强制执行。未来需设计规定代理在需求未满足时应如何行动的规范，并使代理委托给开发者的公平性决策显式化，以实现真正的可执行性。

Abstract: Agentic AI is shifting developers' responsibilities from software implementation to software governance. While this paradigm improves productivity, it also raises challenges in ensuring that autonomous coding agents preserve developers' intentions. Among these, operationalizing fairness-by-design is largely unexplored, as existing approaches provide limited support for representing and propagating fairness requirements throughout agentic development workflows. In this paper, we investigate whether specification-driven development can operationalize fairness-by-design in the agentic development of machine learning systems. We study three coding agents on four machine learning applications, using four prompts that progressively operationalize fairness through nine Fairness Specification Elements, and analyze the traceability of fairness requirements across the generated artifacts, the fairness-accuracy trade-off of the resulting models, and the agents' design decisions and rationales. The results show that when fairness is operationalized, agents capture almost all fairness requirements in the specification (97.22%) and preserve most of them in the code (82.41%). However, the resulting models are not significantly fairer, as agents implement fairness as a post-hoc audit that neither shapes training nor prevents model delivery. Specifications thus make fairness traceable, but not enforceable, calling for fairness specifications that state what agents should do when requirements are not met, and for workflows that make the fairness decisions agents delegate to developers explicit.

</details>


### [121] [Supporting Viewpoint Coordination for Compliance by Design with an Artifact Model for Regulatory Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03631)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov,Davide Fucci,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Daniel Mendez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为 AM4RRE 的"用于法规要求的工件模型"，该模型使用模板来协调法律专家和软件工程师之间的观点。该方法接受了专业人士的认可，并在实际测试中提高了需求质量并缩短了编写时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着法律合规需求日益成为软件工程的核心，法规要求的获取需要将法律专家和软件工程团队的视角结合起来。然而，现有的方法很少关注这种协调，导致需求的完整性和质量难以保证。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于模板的工件驱动的方法：AM4RRE。它定义了用于法规要求的工件，并提供了一套操作这些工件的模板，以促进法律和工程团队在需求获取过程中的协作。作者通过向 37 名从业者进行了实地评估，并进行了涉及 34 名参与者的准实验，在其中 10 个小组中使用了 AM4RRE 的指导模板。

Result: 评估结果显示，大多数受访者对 AM4RRE 在协调法规要求获取过程中的表现和支持需求质量表示满意。准实验结果表明，模板引导的协调在首次执行时与更短的总规格时间相关，并且在大多数小组中提高了需求质量。

Conclusion: 工件驱动的方法为法规要求的获取提供了有前景的协调方法，有助于实现合规设计的目标。通过将法律和软件工程专业知识整合到结构化模板中，AM4RRE 能够提高需求的质量和开发效率。

Abstract: Regulatory compliance has become an essential software requirement Legal demands for compliance by design CbD call for requirements engineering RE approaches that derive requirements and support compliance implementation in subsequent software engineering SE processes such as software design The complexity of the legal knowledge demanded in regulatory RE makes it important to involve legal experts and ensure the relevant specifications are valid and usable for them Regulatory requirements must support compliance implementation in software design However there is a lack of approaches bridging legal and SE viewpoints and interaction challenges between the two persist To address engineeringlegal coordination in specification of regulatory requirements we propose the Artifact Model for Regulatory Requirements Engineering AM4RRE operationalized through templates AM4RRE was positively evaluated by most of the 37 practitioners for coordination in regulatory RE and for supporting requirements quality Results from a quasiexperiment with 34 of them in 10 groups indicated that templateguided coordination was associated with on average shorter total specification time when executed first and higher requirements quality in most groups in which it varied Existing RE research and practice provide little attention and support for viewpoint coordination in regulatory RE for CbD Artifactoriented approaches such as AM4RRE have the potential to guide coordination in RE for better RE results and contribute to concretizing the role and contribution of RE to CbD

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [An Agentic OS Kernel for Flexible and Enhanced Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00002)
*Haocheng Wang,Xinyue Sun,Hao Yi,Dong Du,Yubin Xia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Agentic kernel 将单一系统调用拆解为细粒度可组合的微系统调用，由大语言模型根据意图动态生成并缓存执行计划，实现更高灵活性和性能，并在 Linux IntentOS 上展示出显著的延迟降低和吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一系统调用接口僵化，难以演进且会导致应用语义不匹配；现有 eBPF 等定制机制虽灵活但安全、性能受限且需专业知识，缺乏针对抽象层的易用机制。

Method: 设计 IntentOS，将系统调用拆分为细粒度微系统调用，提供 isyscall 接口让应用提交高层意图，LLM 将其分解为微系统调用组合计划，运行时缓存已验证计划以降免除实时 LLM 调用，确保安全性与系统调用级别保障。

Result: 在四个 Linux 内核版本和三个应用领域（约十个意图）上测试，IntentOS 使后台进程创建延迟降低最高 17.55%，多文件操作提升 6.97%，前端 Nginx 在后台触发的内存操作上性能影响下降 75.52%。

Conclusion: Agentic kernel 通过细粒度微系统调用与 LLM 动态编排，实现了比传统内核更高的灵活性和性能，且安全性不依赖 LLM 正确性，展示出在实际场景中的显著收益。

Abstract: Modern OS kernels expose general-purpose but rigid interfaces (e.g., syscalls) that are slow to evolve due to compatibility, performance, and security constraints, leaving apps to pay for mismatched semantics and legacy APIs. Existing kernel mechanism customization approaches (e.g., eBPF) improve flexibility but still face strict safety and performance restrictions and thus require substantial expertise. What's more, they targets customized kernel mechanisms rather than kernel abstractions.
  This paper presents agentic kernel, a new architecture that decomposes monolithic syscall functions into fine-grained, composable kernel-native micro-syscalls, and then enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to orchestrate them dynamically. Agentic kernel introduces a new entry, isyscall (intent syscall), which lets apps submit high-level intents describing their requirements. Then the kernel leverages LLMs to resolve each intent into composition plan of micro-syscalls to satisfy the user's goal. LLMs can further optimize the plan continuously at kernel runtime. To eliminate LLM inference costs on the critical path, agentic kernel caches and reuses verified plans to bypass LLM entirely. Crucially, agentic kernel's safety guarantees are independent of LLM correctness: micro-syscalls follows syscall-level security mechanisms, ensuring OS safety under arbitrary combinations and orchestration.
  We implement agentic kernel in Linux, called IntentOS, and evaluate it across four kernel versions and three application domains (about ten intents each). IntentOS outperforms baseline Linux in real-world applications, achieving up to 17.55% latency reduction for backend processes creation, 6.97% performance gain for multiple files operations, and 75.52% lower performance impact for frontend Nginx server during memory operations triggered by backend tasks.

</details>


### [123] [TenonAgent: Virtualizing Execution Residency for Memory-Oversubscribed LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00051)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Ruixin Huang,Lezhuo Xuan,Dianyu Wang,Siying Sun,Xiaoyi Li,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: TenonAgent 通过将沙箱从物理执行环境中解耦，实现虚拟化沙箱驻留，从而解决了LLM代理在受限内存中并发服务的问题，实现了从20% OOM率到0%的显著降低，以及8.6倍内存超配比时100%任务成功率，最高速度提升5.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM Agent在生命周期各阶段存在巨大资源需求差异的问题：为每个Agent分配其峰值资源利用率低（尤其内存），而过度超订可能引发内存竞争、OOM和昂贵的重试循环，限制受约束主机的并发效率和效率。

Method: （1）虚拟化沙箱驻留：将逻辑Agent生命周期与物理执行环境解耦，允许动态创建/销毁沙箱；（2）双时间尺度控制平面：（a）动作边界层：预测性准入控制 + 关闭失败且重启安全的合约，根据全局内存压力动态materialize或dematerialize沙箱；（b）活跃工具执行层：基于sched_ext内核门控在下一步执行机会上限制沙箱权限，以降低意外的并发内存增长并提供反馈控制。

Result: （1）成功将观察到的OOM失败显著减少；（2）在高内存超配比率达到8.6倍时实现100%任务成功率；（3）相比无管理执行最高提速5.5倍；（4）相比现有agent级和cgroup调度器提速5至37倍；（5）在Docker和Firecracker微虚拟机后端均提供一致的优势。

Conclusion: TenonAgent是首个通过独立设计沙箱驻留管理来有效应对LLM Agent资源需求异质性问题的系统，证明了虚拟化策略在缓解内存压力并维持高吞吐方面有效的实用价值。

Abstract: LLM agents execute as long-lived, multi-stage workflows with substantial resource-demand variation across phases. Provisioning each agent or sandbox for its lifetime peak leads to low resource utilization, particularly for memory, whereas aggressive oversubscription risks memory contention, out-of-memory failures, and costly workflow retries. This tension limits the concurrency and efficiency of agent serving under constrained host memory.
  We present TenonAgent, a runtime system that virtualizes sandbox residency by decoupling the logical agent's lifetime from its physical execution environment. To manage memory oversubscription, TenonAgent employs a dual-timescale control plane. At action boundaries, it combines predictive admission control with a fail-closed, restart-safe contract, dynamically materializing or dematerializing sandboxes based on global memory pressure. During active tool execution, a sched_ext-based kernel gate paces sandbox execution permissions to reduce unexpected concurrent memory growth. The gate provides feedback control over future execution opportunities.
  Evaluations across diverse agentic workloads show that, under the tested workloads and parameter settings, TenonAgent substantially reduces observed OOM failures and achieves a 100% task success rate in bursts reaching an 8.6x memory oversubscription ratio. Overall, it delivers up to a 5.5x speedup over unmanaged execution, outperforms existing agent-level and cgroup-based schedulers by 5x to 37x, and provides consistent benefits across both Docker and Firecracker microVM backends.

</details>


### [124] [Memorix: Managing Memory Selections for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00060)
*Jing Zhang,Xiaguannan Song,Dong Du,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Memorix 提出了一种全新的 AI 代理内存系统，通过将代理在持久化项目中应读取的内容进行预计算和复用，解决了传统动态组装内存成本高、易遗漏、难以追溯及版本易错配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 代理在跨会话的持久化项目中工作时，高度依赖持久化状态。现有系统在运行时动态拼接内存作为一次性负载，导致组装成本高、易遗漏关键信息、失败无法追溯复盘，且代理容易读取到错误版本的文档。核心动机是利用‘代理读取内容主要由项目决定’的洞察，将选择计算前置。

Method: Memorix 通过三个核心机制实现：（1）不可变且版本化的存储（Store），锁定源标识并廉价复现历史布局；（2）将选择（Derivations）与组合（Environment）分离的视图（View）机制，确保结果可复现和共享；（3）Quests 和 Profiles 机制，保证视图随源演进保持可更新和可用。在 SWE-ContextBench 和 LibEvolutionEval 上进行了评估。

Result: 预计算环境（Precomputed Environments）将任务解决率提高了最高 1.5 倍，并在所有版本敏感任务上准确交付正确的库版本。同时，摊销成本降低了最高 100 倍，构建时间缩短了最高 38 倍。

Conclusion: Memorix 证明，将代理内存的选择逻辑从运行时动态拼接转变为基于项目上下文的预计算，能显著提升 AI 代理在长期、多版本项目中的任务解决效率、准确性和经济性，为代理记忆管理提供了全新范式。

Abstract: AI agents now handle a growing share of tasks across sessions within a persistent project. Their behavior depends not only on the model, but heavily on the persistent state they read from the environment. Existing systems assemble this memory at use time, splicing selected files into the prompt as a one-off payload. As a result, per-task assembly is costly and can still miss critical material, failures cannot be traced or replayed, and agents can read documentation written for the wrong version. Our key insight is that what an agent should read is mostly determined by its project, so the selection can be computed once, ahead of time, and reused across tasks.
  We propose Memorix, an agent memory system that realizes this insight. Memorix achieves this via three key mechanisms: (1) an immutable and versioned Store pins every source by identity and reproduces historical layouts cheaply, (2) a View that splits selection (Derivations) from composition (Environment), making the result reproducible and shareable, and (3) Quests and Profiles keep a delivered view refreshable and available inside the project as sources evolve. We implement Memorix and evaluate it on SWE-ContextBench and LibEvolutionEval against mainstream agent memory systems. Precomputed Environments raise resolved rate by up to 1.5X and deliver the correct library version on every version-conditioned task, while cutting amortized cost by up to 100X and build time by up to 38X.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Source Compatibility: WebAssembly as a Stable ABI for ARINC 653 Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00891)
*Wanja Zaeske,Patrick Siegl,Florian Hartung,Fabian Bauriedl,Nicolaus Bär,Umut Durak*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: ARINC 653作为30年前的机载软件接口标准，因数据布局、整数位宽等实现依赖导致跨操作系统移植需重编译改源码。本文通过优化头文件、在WebAssembly上定义OS/架构无关的二进制接口以及构建Wasm-VM无关的Shim库，实现了ARINC 653分区在多处理器架构上的跨运行时无缝执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ARINC 653 API因内存布局、字长及字节序等实现细节导致的跨平台移植困难，限制了集成模块化航空软件的灵活性，本文旨在消除这些依赖，实现目标码级的平台无关性。

Method: 首先优化ARINC 653头文件消除歧义；其次基于WebAssembly定义OS和处理器架构无关的目标码级ABI，解决数据布局、字节序和函数指针表示差异；最后构建Wasm-VM无关的Shim库，将ARINC 653分区编译为遵循新ABI的Wasm字节码，并在两个独立Wasm运行时上进行多架构验证。

Result: 提出的ABI与Shim库使得ARINC 653分区可在不同处理器架构上跨Wasm运行时执行，无需重编译或修改源码，实现了平台无关的分区执行。

Conclusion: 该工程为集成模块化航空软件的跨平台部署提供了可行的技术路径，展示了WebAssembly在传统航空航天软件框架中重用的潜力，并为未来相关ABI标准化工作提供参考。

Abstract: Thirty years ago, ARINC 653 was introduced to provide a vendor-independent platform for software applications in Integrated Modular Avionics (IMA). A platform is characterized by a well-defined interface abstracting over implementation details; in the case of ARINC 653 this is an Application Programming Interface (API), defined on the source-code level. However, several aspects of this API - such as the memory layout and partially also the integer bit width of data types - remain implementation dependent, so portability across Operating Systems (OSs) requires recompilation, and often also source code adaptation. Our contribution is three-fold; first, we refine the ARINC 653 header file, eliminating remaining ambiguities. Second, building on WebAssembly (Wasm), we define an OS- and processor-architecture-agnostic Application Binary Interface (ABI) at the object-code level, resolving differences in data layout, endianness, and function-pointer representation between host and guest environments. Third, we demonstrate a Wasm-VM-agnostic shim-library that executes ARINC 653 partitions, compiled to Wasm bytecode and following our ABI, on multiple processor architectures using two independent Wasm runtimes.

</details>


### [126] [RTP-LLM: Building an Evolvable and Reliable LLM Inference Engine at Alibaba Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00967)
*Jiarui Guo,Zhi Zhang,Boyu Tan,Zongwei Lv,Haobo Sun,Na He,Ziqing Wang,Xinfei Shi,Wei Zhang,Xi Yang,Haosheng Hu,Wending Bao,Zetao Hu,Dong Cao,Yong Li,Lining Wang,Chengjun Yang,Tianpeng Wang,Huanbing Lu,Yidan Wang,Hengyi Bao,Yaxin Yu,Hanyun Guo,Huidong Zhang,Haiyu Zhou,Zefeng Liu,Weixiang Huang,Ziyi Wang,Chenyi Song,Ruixuan Zhang,Zhengkai Ao,Haocheng Luo,Zhichen Xue,Jialin Zhang,Chi Zhang,Lingjun Zhu,Silu Zhou,Zongyuan Wu,Yinghao Yu,Yi Zheng,Miao Sun,Yuxing Yuan,Juncheng Yin,Guoding Li,Nan Wang,Xiyu Wang,Lin Yang,Zechao Zhang,Jianning Zhang,Bo Cai,Xiaolin Wang,Tao Lan,Tong Yang,Kan Liu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: RTP-LLM是一个开源生产推理引擎，通过将模型执行与共享请求处理、状态管理、资源协调分离，显式接口局部化模型变更，支持~100个模型、日均数万万亿Token，兼顾快速演进与生产可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型和推理技术迭代加快，模型启用成为反复生产任务而非一次性集成，阿里云需在快速演进与生产可靠性间取得平衡。

Method: 架构将模型特定执行与共享组件分离，通过显式接口局部化兼容模型变更，实现模型与部署配置复用通用 serving 机制。采用分层、接口局限化的设计来控制失败影响。

Result: 从探索系统演进为共享生产引擎，支持~100个模型，每日处理数万万亿Token。架构和实践有效容纳模型演进并遏制故障影响。

Conclusion: 企业规模下构建可演进、可靠推理引擎的经验：通过接口局部化、共享基础设施分离模型与服务，实现快速迭代与高可用的统一。

Abstract: Large language models and inference techniques evolve rapidly, making model enablement a recurring production task rather than a one-time integration effort. At Alibaba, a shared inference infrastructure must keep pace with new models and serving capabilities while remaining reliable across heterogeneous production environments. To reconcile rapid evolution with production reliability, we present RTP-LLM, an open-source production inference engine developed and deployed at Alibaba. Its architecture separates model-specific execution from shared request processing, state management, and resource coordination. Explicit interfaces localize compatible model changes while allowing models and deployment configurations to reuse common serving mechanisms. RTP-LLM has evolved from an exploratory system into a shared production engine that supports $\sim$100 models and processes hundreds of trillions of tokens per day. We describe how its architecture and production practices accommodate model evolution and contain the impact of failures, and share lessons for building evolvable and reliable inference engines at enterprise scale.

</details>


### [127] [High-Throughput Private Information Retrieval with on-CPU Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02376)
*Gyuyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出 AltoPIR，第一个利用片上 CPU 矩阵加速器（如 Intel AMX）的私有信息检索（PIR）系统，通过高效批处理在无需离散加速器的情况下显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 CPU 的 PIR 方案从批处理中获益有限，因为每个查询仍需独立乘法，且向量指令一次仅能处理少量运算；GPU 等离散加速器虽可加速计算，但需要额外硬件，且受限于显存容量，难以支撑大规模数据库。

Method: AltoPIR 利用片上 CPU 矩阵加速器，通过 CPU 缓存保持加速器高效供数，使每个数据库字节在内存中仅被读取一次以服务批处理中的所有查询，并且从不将中间乘积写回内存，从而实现高效的批处理加速。

Result: 评估表明，AltoPIR 的吞吐量比现有基于 CPU 的方案最高提升 50.1 倍，并能处理大于 GPU 显存容量的数据库。

Conclusion: 无需额外离散加速器，仅利用现有 CPU 片上矩阵加速器即可实现高性能的私有信息检索，兼顾高吞吐量与大规模数据库支持。

Abstract: Private information retrieval (PIR) hides which record a client retrieves by scanning the entire database, and batching is an effective technique to improve throughput by sharing this scan across queries. However, existing CPU-based solutions gain little from batching because each query still requires its own multiplications, and vector instructions perform only a few at a time. GPUs and other discrete accelerators can accelerate these computations, but require extra hardware and can constrain database size due to their limited memory capacity. In this paper, we present AltoPIR, the first PIR system that leverages on-CPU matrix accelerators such as Intel AMX. AltoPIR keeps the accelerator fed from the CPU caches, reading each database byte from memory once for all queries in a batch and never writing intermediate products to memory. This enables high-performance PIR through efficient batching without requiring discrete accelerators.
