Our evaluation shows that AltoPIR achieves up to 50.1x higher throughput than existing CPU-based solutions and serves databases larger than a GPU's memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [mmWave UE Positioning Under Blockage: Limits for ISAC-Assisted Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00453)
*Seyed Bagher Hashemi Natanzi,Bo Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Using a map-aided multipath estimator to localize users from uplink sounding signals in mmWave networks significantly improves position error, reducing median error from 12 to 3.7 cm by handling blockages better than a simple dominant-path estimator; however, it still does not beat the 3GPP baseline at typical link margins.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Proactive handover can prevent handover failures when a body or object blocks the mmWave link, but it requires accurate user localization. The research asks whether radio units can estimate user position enough to trigger timely proactive handovers.

Method: The authors derived position error bounds for two estimators: (1) a simple dominant-path estimator and (2) a map-aided multipath tracker that uses reflected paths from a digital twin model. Both were evaluated through ray-traced simulations in a realistic street canyon scenario with synchronization and array calibration errors included as complicating factors. The comparison is made against the 3GPP baseline handover criterion.

Result: Three key findings emerged: (1) Line-of-sight (LoS) conditions are limited primarily by array calibration rather than bandwidth; (2) blockage degrades the LoS-only bound sharply while known reflections keep map-aided bounds low; (3) the simple dominant-path estimator's errors spike near blockages, making proactive handover worse than the 3GPP baseline, whereas the map-aided tracker reduces median position error from 12 to 3.7 cm and removes the penalty at link margins of 15 dB or above, but does not fully eliminate the penalty against the baseline.

Conclusion: Map-aided multipath localization substantially mitigates but does not completely resolve the proactive handover failure problem in blocked mmWave scenarios, indicating room for further algorithmic improvement.

Abstract: Proactive handover can save a millimeter-wave (mmWave) link before a passing body blocks it, but only if the network knows where the user is. We ask whether the radio units that serve a user can localize it accurately enough from its uplink sounding reference signals. We derive position error bounds that include synchronization and array calibration errors, with and without reflected paths known from a digital twin, and test two estimators in a ray-traced street canyon on held-out scenarios. Three findings stand out. First, with line of sight to both radio units, array calibration, not bandwidth, limits the bound. Second, blockage degrades the line-of-sight-only bound sharply, whereas known reflections keep the map-aided bound low. Third, a simple dominant-path estimator fails where it matters most: its errors grow sharply around blockage, and proactive handover becomes worse than the 3GPP baseline. A map-aided multipath tracker cuts the median error from 12 to 3.7 cm and removes this significant penalty at link margins of 15 dB and above, but does not yet beat the baseline.

</details>


### [129] [From Confounding to Counterfactuals: A Cross-Layer Causal Model for RAN Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00635)
*Artin Elhamirad,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Ali Tajer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨PHY-MAC-网络层的层级结构因果模型（H-SCM），有效分离单元负载混淆与UE响应异质性，通过逆向因果调度实现近似最优资源分配，仿真显示相较于传统观测估计，全PRB响应误差从73.3%降至9.8%，调度接近地面真优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAN数据驱动调度依赖观测关联而忽略跨层混淆与UE个体差异，导致资源分配效果被高估且无法识别受益UE，现有因果推断手段也未建模PHY-MAC-网络层的跨层机制。

Method: 提出跨PHY、MAC、网络层的层级结构因果模型（H-SCM），分离单元负载混淆与UE响应异质性；设计逆向调度问题，利用每UE逆向因果良put而非观测条件平均值；通过受限因果发现、结构方程拟合及逆向-行动预测管线求解。

Result: 仿真表明， naive 观测估计使全PRB响应高估73.3%，调整单元负载混淆后误差降至9.8%； held-out interventions 预测决定系数达0.988；逆向调度总体好耗相对损失低于0.5%，优于神经网络基准2.0-2.9%。

Conclusion: H-SCM通过跨层建模与逆向因果推断，精准解决了RAN调度中的混淆与异质性问题，实现了近乎最优的资源分配性能，为数据驱动的无线网络控制提供了理论与实证双重支撑。

Abstract: Data-driven control in radio access networks (RANs) selects resource allocations using associations between control actions and key performance indicators (KPIs) learned from network telemetry. Because the shared radio environment influences both the scheduler's decisions and the resulting performance, a bias known as confounding, such associations overstate the effect of an allocation and misdirect resources toward user equipment (UEs) that cannot benefit. Moreover, individual UEs respond heterogeneously to the same decision, so even a corrected average effect does not identify which UEs gain from additional resources. While causal inference has recently been applied to RANs, existing approaches do not model the cross-layer mechanisms that connect an allocation to its outcome. In this paper, a hierarchical structural causal model (H-SCM) spanning the PHY, MAC, and network layers is proposed that separates cell-load confounding from UE-specific response heterogeneity. In particular, a counterfactual scheduling problem is formulated in which physical resource block (PRB) allocations are selected using per-UE counterfactual goodput rather than observational conditional averages. This problem is solved via a pipeline combining constrained causal discovery, structural-equation fitting, and abduction-action-prediction. Simulations demonstrate that naive observational estimation overstates the full-PRB response by 73.3%, whereas adjustment for cell-load confounding reduces the relative error to 9.8%. Held-out interventions are predicted with a coefficient of determination of 0.988. Counterfactual scheduling achieves a mean relative loss in total cell goodput below 0.5% against the ground-truth optimum, compared with 2.0-2.9% for a neural-network baseline.

</details>


### [130] [Joint Trajectory, Sensing, and Computation Co-Design for UAV-Assisted Federated Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00790)
*Kaichen Liu,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo,Jianhua Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于四阶段联合轨迹、感知与计算协作设计的UAV辅助联邦边缘视觉方法，以在电池限制下最小化模型训练时间并处理数据异质性，仿真表明其在收敛速度和准确率上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将训练数据视为静态，忽略了动态视觉感知与大量在场计算负荷之间的紧密耦合，需要在电池约束下尽量缩短训练时间。

Method: 提出四阶段联合轨迹、感知与计算协作设计；采用均匀感知概率将联邦收敛要求转化为UAV运动约束；开发两层交替优化算法自适应确定本地数据工作量，平衡训练轮数与在场计算延迟。

Result: 仿真验证了该方法在模型收敛和准确率方面优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该联合设计在电池约束下有效降低预期训练时间，并通过均匀感知和交替优化实现数据异质性的平衡，提升了整体性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates UAV-assisted federated edge vision for training computationally heavy object detection models. Existing studies treat training data as static, ignoring the tight coupling between dynamic visual sensing and massive in place computational burdens. To minimize the expected training time under battery constraints, we propose a four-phase joint trajectory, sensing, and computation co-design. To mitigate data heterogeneity, we use a uniform sensing probability to translate federated convergence requirements into UAV mobility constraints. Furthermore, a two-layer alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to adaptively determine the local data workload, optimally balancing the required training rounds against in-place computation delays. Simulations validate the superiority of our approach in model convergence and accuracy over baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Energy-Efficient Geographic Multicast Routing in Cognitive Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01212)
*Farzan Heidari,Farzaneh Heidari*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出两种认知无线传感网广播路由协议CCast和PCast，分别利用延迟导向的频谱选择和归一化链路功率成本来降低能耗，在仅使用局部邻居信息和目的地坐标、无需完整拓扑图的情况下实现广播，相比GMP能耗分别降低21%和26%，但延迟和路径长度均有增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感网能耗是主要设计关注点，认知无线电组 multicast 路由旨在在利用许可频谱机会的同时提高转发效率，现有方案在能效与频谱利用率方面有提升空间。

Method: CCast 协议结合组树构建与延迟导向的频谱选择指标；PCast 协议使用归一化链路功率成本来选择路由并避免主用户占用的低频段，两者均仅依靠局部邻居信息和目的地坐标，无需完整网络拓扑图。

Result: 仿真对比显示CCast和PCast相较于GMP分别实现21%和26%的能耗降低，但每个目的地的路径长度增加和传输延迟也随之上升。

Conclusion: CCast和PCast在认知无线传感网中实现了显著的能耗降低，展示了在局部信息下的组播路由新方法，但需权衡延迟与路径长度的 trade-off。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a major design concern in wireless sensor networks. Cognitive radio provides opportunistic access to licensed spectrum, while multicast routing allows several destinations to share forwarding paths. In this paper, we propose two geographic multicast routing protocols for cognitive wireless sensor networks, CCast and PCast. CCast combines multicast-tree construction with a latency-oriented spectrum-selection metric. PCast uses a normalized link-power cost to select routes and can avoid regions where primary users occupy lower-frequency bands. Both protocols use local neighbor information and destination coordinates, without requiring a complete network-topology map. Simulations compare the proposed protocols with geographic multicast routing (GMP) and multiple unicast routing. In simulations, CCast and PCast reduce energy consumption by 21\% and 26\% relative to GMP, at the cost of longer per-destination routes and higher delivery delay.

</details>


### [132] [Vehicle and UAV Detection and Tracking with LTE Downlink Reference Signals: A Ray-Traced Evaluation on a Mapped Suburban Site with Licensed Base-Station Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01459)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 被动感知在下行LTE中可在用户设备上直接进行，模拟显示误检率较高且覆盖受限，需进一步扩展至多光源和现场验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不修改网络的情况下，评估利用现有下行参考信号进行车辆追踪的可行性。

Method: 在3GPP LTE频段3的射线追踪信道上，对合成街道峡谷和基于OpenStreetMap的真实街区进行仿真，比较下行参考信号臂与上行 sounding 臂的漏报概率，并分析不同接收机位置、间距及覆盖范围。

Result: 下行臂的漏报概率为0.667，上行臂为0.772；在街区实测中，9个接收机在车辆行进路径上实现29.9%至61.7%的覆盖率，8个间距135 m的接收机可覆盖1140 m街道并探测每个基站的15 m空中目标。

Conclusion: 被动下行感知虽可行但漏报率较高，未来需通过多光源配置和实际测量进一步验证其性能。

Abstract: Passive sensing on the cellular downlink places the receiver at the user equipment and changes nothing in the network. We report simulation results for an LTE band-3 cell-specific reference signal arm on a ray-traced channel. On a synthetic street canyon the arm is compared with an uplink sounding reference signal arm on the same band, target and scorer: missed-detection probabilities of 0.667 and 0.772. On a surveyed site built from OpenStreetMap footprints and the licensed site record, nine receiver positions along the street track the vehicle at coverage between 29.9 % and 61.7 %; a 283 m lap separates target height from trajectory at 1.6 to 30 m; a 137 m lap on the built-up street tracks a vehicle and a 15 m airborne target on every cell, and 8 receivers at 135 m spacing carry one illuminator's coverage over 1140 m of the street. Potential future work includes extending the analysis to multiple illuminators and varying receiver configurations, and confirmation through over-the-air measurements.

</details>


### [133] [MATE: Multi-Agent Traffic Engineering for Topology-Aware Distributed SRv6 Scheduling under Bursty Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01831)
*Yuang Chen,Shuyi Liu,Chengdi Lu,Fengqian Guo,Hancheng Lu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MATE是一种利用历史流量稳定性和路径敏感性的分布式主动流量工程方法，能够在低延迟下实现高效率的流量调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式流量工程因控制循环延迟大、状态聚合和计算分布式化，难以应对动态突发的网络流量。

Method: MATE采用离线集中训练、在线分布式执行的架构，使用共享全局评论员、基于最大链路利用率与变异加权路径敏感性的奖励以及对手特定的反事实奖励进行策略学习，在线时边缘代理依据本地观测生成流量分割策略并通过SRv6实现。

Result: 在CENI实验和Sanet模拟中，MATE在大多数场景下优于MARL基线、COPE、TEAL及FIGRET，实现接近线性规划最优的90.1%实例，故障场景中表现更佳，推理时间约2.05–3.14 ms。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在部分SRv6部署的混合网络中，分布式主动流量工程的可行性和优势，能够在保持低延迟的同时实现高效利用率。

Abstract: As network traffic becomes increasingly dynamic and bursty, conventional centralized traffic engineering (TE) suffers from long control-loop delays caused by network-state aggregation, centralized computation, and policy distribution. In this paper, we propose MATE, a multi-agent TE method for proactive distributed scheduling in hybrid networks with partial deployment of Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6). MATE uses historical traffic stability to characterize the burst risk of each traffic demand and path sensitivity to quantify the ability of candidate paths to accommodate additional traffic, thereby enabling differentiated proactive scheduling tailored to heterogeneous traffic characteristics. MATE adopts an offline-centralized training and online-distributed execution architecture. During training, a shared global critic evaluates joint actions under a reward combining maximum link utilization (MLU) with variability-weighted path sensitivity, while counterfactual difference rewards provide agent-specific credit for actor learning. During online execution, edge agents generate traffic-splitting policies from local observations and apply them through SRv6, avoiding centralized optimization in each scheduling interval. Experiments on the China Environment for Network Innovations (CENI) testbed and simulations on the Sanet topology show that MATE outperforms a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) ablation baseline without agent-specific credit assignment, COPE, and TEAL in most evaluated scenarios and approaches centralized FIGRET on measured traffic traces. On CENI-Abilene, MATE achieves MLU within 10% of the linear-programming optimum in up to 90.1% of test instances. MATE also achieves lower median normalized MLU than FIGRET in most tested failure scenarios, with mean neural-inference times of 2.05--3.14~ms.

</details>


### [134] [Interoperability Assessment of Fronthaul and E2 Interfaces for Multi-Vendor Open RAN Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01862)
*Farhad Mehran,Charles Turyagyenda,Dritan Kaleshi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本论文展示了在SONIC实验室中针对13个多厂商Open RAN系统的Open Fronthaul和E2接口进行互操作性评估的最新发现与经验教训，旨在推动开放、可互操作的无线接入网络发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放无线接入网络（Open RAN）通过开放接口和虚拟化技术变革传统网络架构，但Open Fronthaul和E2接口的集成面临诸多挑战（如IOT配置文件对齐、Near-RT RIC引入的新难题），亟需严格的互操作性测试评估。

Method: 基于O-RAN联盟互操作性测试（IOT）规范，对部署在SONIC实验室的13个多厂商Open RAN系统进行Open Fronthaul和E2接口的实际互操作性测试与评估。

Result: 总结并分享了针对这13个多厂商系统在Open Fronthaul和E2接口集成中的关键最新发现、测试数据和实践教训。

Conclusion: 通过对这些接口的严格评估，本文为解决多厂商环境下的接口对齐与集成难题提供了宝贵参考，对加速Open RAN生态的成熟和规模化部署具有指导意义。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (RANs) aim to transform the existing wireless network architectures by adopting open interfaces, disaggregating and virtualising RAN components, and enabling dynamic control of the system via data-driven methods. To accelerate the development of interoperable RAN solutions, rigorous assessment of Open Fronthaul and E2 interfaces based on O-RAN Alliance Interoperability Testing (IOT) specifications plays a key role as the integration of these interfaces seems to be very challenging, e.g. due to the need for alignment of Fronthaul IOT profiles and unforeseen challenges for E2 interface integration as Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT RIC) is relatively a new component introduced within the RAN architecture. In this paper, selected parts of the latest findings and lessons learned from interoperability assessment of Open Fronthaul and E2 interfaces for 13 multi-vendor Open RAN systems deployed in SmartRAN Open Network Interoperability Centre (SONIC) Labs are presented.

</details>


### [135] [PRISM: RL-Based Rate Control for Low Latency and High Data Rate in Mobile Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02032)
*Hamid Hassani,Staji Wilson,Kishor C. Joshi,Sonia M. Heemstra,Ignas G. Niemegeers,George Exarchakos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: PRISM, a reinforcement‑learning congestion control algorithm that uses peak physical resource block utilization as an early signal, achieves much lower latency and higher goodput‑over‑latency than existing TCP Cubic and BBR flows in highly contested mobile scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delay‑sensitive services such as extended reality and remote operation require congestion control that can keep latency low while maintaining high data rates; existing CCAs rely on delayed or imprecise signals like loss, delay, or ECN, which are unsuitable for mobile networks.

Method: PRISM employs a reinforcement‑learning approach that monitors peak PRB utilization as an early congestion indicator and adjusts the send rate accordingly, enabling rapid reaction to changing mobile conditions.

Result: In NS3 simulations, PRISM achieved a mean one‑way delay of 6.3 ms and a 99th‑percentile delay of 44.6 ms with only 0.02 % packet loss, while coexisting TCP flows saw 60× higher delays and 0.1 % loss; PRISM delivered a 31× improvement in goodput‑over‑latency performance.

Conclusion: PRISM demonstrates that using peak PRB utilization as a congestion signal within a reinforcement‑learning framework provides an effective trade‑off for delay‑sensitive applications in challenging mobile environments.

Abstract: Emerging delay-sensitive interactive services, such as extended reality and remote operation, demand advanced congestion control algorithms (CCAs) capable of maintaining high data rates while keeping latency low in mobile networks. Existing CCAs typically rely on congestion signals such as packet loss, delay, or explicit congestion notification (ECN) marks, which can be delayed, imprecise, or require extensive upgrades to the network infrastructure. To address these limitations, physical resource block (PRB) utilization has recently been proposed as a reliable early congestion signal in mobile networks [1]. This paper introduces PRISM, a reinforcement-learning-based CCA that uses peak PRB utilization to regulate the send rate under challenging mobility and fading conditions. NS3 simulation results show that, in a mobility scenario where two PRISM flows coexist with two TCP Cubic flows and one TCP BBR flow, PRISM maintains a per-packet mean one-way delay (OWD) of 6.3ms and a $p_{99}$ OWD of 44.6ms, with negligible (0.02%) packet loss, over a 20min high-contention interval. However, over the same interval, the coexisting TCP flows experience 60x higher mean OWDs of 371-390ms and $p_{99}$ OWDs of 563-578ms, with 0.1% packet loss. PRISM achieves a 31x improvement in goodput-over-latency performance, indicating a favorable tradeoff for delay-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [136] [QueueTrack: Framework for Detecting the Onset of Queueing in Mobile Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02038)
*Hamid Hassani,Anudeep Karnam,Kishor Joshi,Sonia M. Heemstra,Ignas G. Niemegeers,George Exarchakos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: QueueTrack is an end-host framework that uses packet-level timing statistics to compute a Queue Buildup Indicator (QBI) and detect early queueing at mobile network bottlenecks without extra infrastructure or tight host synchronization. It was evaluated on a 5G testbed with competing TCP/UDP traffic, showing reliable detection (~ 80% F1 score) using simple models like logistic regression.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional congestion signals (loss, RTT, OWD) are limited for early queue detection in mobile networks because end hosts have limited visibility. There is a need for a practical, low‑overhead method to detect queueing onset at path bottlenecks to enable low‑delay congestion control and network monitoring.

Method: QueueTrack builds an ML pipeline: (1) collect endpoint‑observable packet timing data, (2) label data with queue‑onset events, (3) train simple classifiers (e.g., logistic regression) on a 5G OpenAirInterface testbed under various TCP/UDP competing scenarios, and (4) evaluate detection performance without requiring tight host synchronization or network changes.

Result: Experimental results on the 5G testbed demonstrate reliable detection of queue buildup at the base station. In a challenging mixed TCP/UDP scenario, QueueTrack achieves an F1 score of approximately 80% using low‑complexity models.

Conclusion: QueueTrack provides a practical end‑host framework for early queueing detection in mobile networks, offering a viable foundation for low‑delay CCAs, network telemetry, and monitoring without extra infrastructure.

Abstract: In mobile networks, timely and accurate detection of the onset of queueing at path bottlenecks remains challenging for end-to-end congestion control algorithms (CCAs), where end hosts have limited visibility into queue status. Traditional congestion signals, such as packet loss, round-trip time (RTT), and one-way delay (OWD), have well-known limitations for early queue buildup detection. This paper presents QueueTrack, a practical end-host framework to detect the onset of queueing in mobile networks. QueueTrack computes a queue buildup indicator, denoted QBI, from endpoint-observable packet-level timing statistics, without requiring tight synchronization between end hosts or modifications to the existing network infrastructure. We develop and assess the complete QueueTrack machine learning (ML) pipeline, including data collection, labeling, model training, and testing, on a 5G testbed built on the OpenAirInterface platform, under a range of scenarios with competing TCP traffic. The experimental results show that QueueTrack can reliably detect the onset of queueing at the 5G base station using low-complexity classification models such as logistic regression. For example, QueueTrack achieves an F1 score of approximately 80% in a challenging mixed TCP/UDP scenario. These findings confirm that QueueTrack can serve as a practical framework for developing CCAs that target low delay, as well as for network telemetry and network monitoring in mobile networks.

</details>


### [137] [Band-Separated Redundant Communication for a Mars-Analog Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02131)
*Zamiul Rashid,Akid Anis,Tanzim Ahmed,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam,Md. Khalilur Rhaman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文介绍了BRACU Mongol-Tori探测器的通信栈设计，采用分离频段和冗余无线电实现视频/控制链路的高可靠性，实测验证了备份链路的自动切换能力，并揭示了Babel路由协议在链路质量评估上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URC比赛中通信故障是最常见失利原因，探测器需要多摄像头视频带宽和抗单点故障的控制通道。

Method: 视频和RTK修正数据走5.2 GHz主链路，2.4 GHz为备用，Babel路由协议动态选择；控制指令同时在两个亚GHz无线电发送，主链路失效时启用备份。实验包括台架测试（断电、注入丢包）和野外行驶测试（>1 km，视线中断）。

Result: 台架测试中备份链路几秒内接管，主链路在10%丢包下仍维持；野外主链路在771 m失效，备份链路支撑至1 km以上。发现Babel仅根据Hello丢包判断链路，导致在主链路接收端故障时未切换至容量更大的备份链路，且在边缘范围发生抖动。

Conclusion: 分离频段和冗余传输显著提升可靠性；需改进链路度量指标（如引入吞吐量/延迟），优化天线指向和日志记录，以解决Babel协议的局限并增强边缘场景下的稳定性。

Abstract: Teams in the University Rover Challenge (URC) drive their rovers remotely and often out of sight. The organizers name communication failure as the most common reason a team falls short. A rover therefore needs bandwidth for several camera views and a command path that survives the loss of one radio. We describe and field test the communication stack of the BRACU Mongol-Tori rover, which keeps vision and control on separate radios and bands. Video and RTK corrections travel over a 5.2 GHz bridge with a 2.4 GHz backup, and the Babel routing protocol chooses between them. Every command goes out on two sub-gigahertz radios at once, and the rover uses the backup copy only when the primary falls silent. We tested the backhaul on the bench with a power cut and injected packet loss. We also drove the rover more than a kilometer from the base, and line of sight ended about halfway. On the bench, traffic reached the backup within a few seconds. A metric offset kept it on a primary that lost one packet in ten. In the field, the primary failed at 771 m and the backup carried video and corrections to just over a kilometer. The drive also exposed a limit of Babel, which judges a link only by lost Hello messages. It kept traffic on a primary with a faulty receiver, although the backup reported twice the capacity, and it flapped at the edge of range. We close with lessons on antenna aiming, link metrics and logging.

</details>


### [138] [Wireless Networks That See: Integrated Sensing and Communication in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02157)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了利用已知蜂窝信号进行集成感知与通信(ISAC)的教程,解决静态背景干扰、多径虚假目标、单视点盲区等问题,通过信道估计、背景抑制、多径解释、多站点协作提升无人机等目标的感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信中的射频交互可用于环境感知,但强回波不一定可靠,静态背景掩盖运动、反射产生虚假目标、单视点观测不全,需系统方法解决这些挑战。

Method: 利用已知蜂窝信号估计传播、抑制静态背景、解释反射路径、融合多站点观测;采用3GPP随机信道模型和旧金山城市场景射线追踪仿真,展示空间布局、信道剖面、范围-多普勒图前后对比;局部检查减少虚假输出,多站点协作提升定位精度。

Result: 局部检查降低虚警但伴随检测损失;协作感知显著提升被单站点和协作站点同时检测到的无人机定位精度;信号资源、测量上报、应用需求共同决定感知性能。

Conclusion: 为复杂环境下的感知服务提供了实用指导,展示了ISAC在无人机、车辆、工业机器人感知中的潜力与权衡。

Abstract: The same radio interactions that complicate wireless communication can reveal what is happening around a network. Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) turns these interactions into opportunities to observe unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), road vehicles, and industrial mobile robots. Yet a strong echo is not necessarily a reliable object observation: stationary background can conceal motion, reflections can create virtual objects, and one viewpoint can leave part of an object's motion unobserved. This tutorial explains how known cellular signals are used to estimate propagation, suppress stationary background, interpret reflected paths, and combine observations from different sites. UAV examples under a Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) stochastic channel model and ray tracing in a San Francisco urban scene illustrate the mechanisms through spatial layouts, channel profiles, and before/after range--Doppler maps. Local checks reduce false outputs, with some loss of detection. Cooperation improves position accuracy for UAVs detected by both a single site and cooperating sites. The examples show how signal resources, measurement reporting, and application needs shape sensing performance, providing practical guidance for sensing services in complex environments.

</details>


### [139] [S-R3: Secure and Robust RIS-Assisted RSMA under Finite-Resolution Phase Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02662)
*Ibrahima Sory Diallo,Elhadj Moustapha Diallo,Wang Mi,Mamadou Aliou Diallo,Amayika Kakati*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可重构智能表面（RIS）的速率分裂多址（RSMA）下行通信方案，解决了在存在不完美窃听人信道状态信息和有限分辨率RIS相位的情况下，如何在保证安全性的同时平衡公共信息和私有信息的吞吐量。通过构建保守泄漏边界和凸内传送更新，实现了可行的内迭代收敛，并保证了外层更新的有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通信系统的演进，隐私保护和安全性成为关键研究方向。传统的多址通信方案在面对窃听攻击时，往往难以在保证安全性的同时实现高效的吞吐量优化。本文旨在设计一个鲁棒的RSMA方案，能够在有限的RIS相位和不完美的信道信息下，实现公共信息和私有信息的有效分离与传输，解决当前研究中的安全性与效率之间的权衡问题。

Method: 该方法采用了多层次的优化策略。首先，构建保守的泄漏边界来处理不完美的信道状态信息；其次，设计凸内传送更新机制，通过可行性检验和有限的人工噪声和RIS相位候选集来保证收敛性；最后，采用外层更新策略，确保迭代过程的有限性和收敛性。具体包括：制定鲁棒的RSMA设计框架，包含传输预编码、等距人工噪声、公共和私有流速率分配以及RIS相位优化；建立有效通道不确定性边界以应对有限分辨率RIS相位；通过保守泄漏边界和凸内迭代实现可行性检验；采用可行性初始化和有限候选集搜索策略，确保算法收敛。

Result: 研究表明，该方法能够在有限RIS相位和不完美信道条件下，找到可行的传输预编码、等距人工噪声和速率分配方案。内迭代的目标值是非递减的，且迭代变化趋于消失，保证了算法的稳定性和收敛性。外层更新保持可行性，且迭代次数有限，实现了实际可行的优化过程。此外，代码已公开可用，方便进一步研究和应用。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的RSMA安全通信方案，通过保守泄漏边界和凸优化技术，有效解决了隐私保护与通信效率之间的矛盾，为未来智能环境下的安全通信提供了理论和实践指导。

Abstract: This paper investigates a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) downlink with imperfect eavesdropper channel state information and finite-resolution RIS phases. The common stream carries public information, while user-specific private streams carry confidential information. For a conservative security assessment, each eavesdropper is allowed to remove the public stream and is given the other private streams as side information. We formulate a robust design of t he transmit precoders, isotropicartificial noise, common-rate allocations, and RIS phases that balances confidential and public throughput under rate and power constraints. Bounded errors in the direct and cascaded eavesdropper channels yield an effective-channel uncertainty bound valid across RIS phase updates. To address the resulting nonconvex problem, we construct conservative leakage bounds and convex inner transmit updates, initialize through a feasibility procedure, and search finite artificial-noise and RIS-phase candidates with explicit feasibility checks. For a fixed artificialnoise power and RIS configuration, the feasible inner iterates have a nondecreasing objective that converges in value, and their successive changes vanish. Accepted outer updates remain feasible and are finite in number. Our code is available at https://github.com/ABM-ADAM/RIS RSMA-secure.

</details>


### [140] [Multipartite FSO/QKD with N-GHZ States for Scalable Secure Group Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02913)
*Yuma Kaminaga,Hoang D. Le,Cuong T. Nguyen,Anh T. Pham*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于高空平台 (HAP) 的多方量子密钥分发 (QKD) 网络架构，该架构利用 N-GHZ 态协议和 TDMA 方案 over FSO 链路，显著提高了群密钥生成率并降低了延迟，从而为未来的 6G 无线网络提供安全组通信解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的 QKD 协议是点对点设计的，不能直接应用于群体密钥建立，而 6G 网络对安全的群体通信提出了需求，需要抗量子计算机攻击的安全组密钥生成机制。

Method: 设计了一个可扩展的 HAP 辅助多方 QKD 网络，结合 N-GHZ 态协议和 TDMA 方案 over 自由空间光学 (FSO) 信道，并使用基于 Qiskit 的仿真框架评估了系统的性能，模拟了 FSO 链路的实际物理层损耗。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提议的架构相较于传统的双方方法显著提高了群密钥生成率，同时有效降低了整体网络执行延迟。分析还揭示了信道损耗的影响，并提供了如最佳光束发散控制等实用设计建议。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了 HAP 辅助多方 QKD 作为 6G 无线网络安全组通信的有效解决方案，为未来大规模安全群体密钥分配提供了可行的途径。

Abstract: The emergence of sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks is driving the need for secure group key generation mechanisms resilient to quantum-computing attacks. Conventional quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols are designed for point-to-point communication and are therefore not directly applicable to group key establishment. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a scalable High-Altitude Platform (HAP)-assisted multipartite QKD network architecture that integrates $N$-GHZ-state protocols with a Time Division Multiple Access (TDMA) scheme over free-space optical (FSO) channels. The performance of the proposed system is evaluated using a Qiskit-based simulation framework that incorporates realistic physical-layer impairments of FSO links. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed architecture significantly improves the group secret key rate (SKR) compared to conventional bipartite approaches, while effectively reducing overall network execution delay. In addition, the analysis reveals the impact of channel impairments and provides practical design insights, such as optimal beam-waist control. The proposed framework demonstrates the potential of HAP-assisted multipartite QKD as an effective security solution for future large-scale 6G wireless networks supporting secure group communications.

</details>


### [141] [DisC: Incentive Design for Green, Robust, and Fair Client Self-Participation in OTA Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03024)
*Driss Choukri,Essaid Sabir,Abdelkrim Haqiq*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出DisC游戏理论客户选择框架，以实现公平、信道感知和能源高效的OTA-FL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户异质、信道衰落、能源限制和参与不平衡导致公平性和聚合精度下降，现有方案在高权重客户信道差时仍出现大聚合误差。

Method: DisC通过让每个客户根据捕获公平重要性、学习相关性、OTA失真、能耗和延迟的效用函数自行选择参与程度，形成 exact potential game，保证Nash平衡并对应本地最优OTA-FL点。

Result: DisC 承认Nash平衡，可作为 exact potential game，稳定的平衡对应 locally optimal OTA‑FL 操作点，提供分布式、激励兼容的客户选择方案。

Conclusion: 该框架在无线资源受限下实现公平、能效和聚合精度的平衡，是 centralized 客户选择的可行替代。

Abstract: Over-the-Air Federated Learning (OTA-FL) improves communication efficiency by exploiting waveform superposition over wireless Multiple-Access Channels (MAC). However, client heterogeneity, fading, energy constraints, and participation imbalance can degrade both fairness and aggregation accuracy. Existing Pareto-oriented fair OTA-FL schemes may still suffer from large aggregation distortion when highly weighted clients experience poor channel conditions. This paper proposes DisC, a game-theoretic distributed client selection framework for fairness-aware, channel-aware, and energy-efficient OTA-FL. Each client selects its participation level by maximizing a utility that captures Pareto fairness importance, learning relevance, OTA distortion, energy consumption, and delay. We show that DisC admits a Nash equilibrium and can be formulated as an exact potential game, where stable equilibria correspond to locally optimal OTA-FL operating points. DisC therefore offers a distributed, incentive-compatible alternative to centralized client selection under wireless and resource constraints.

</details>


### [142] [Pod-wise Multimodal Federated Learning for Heterogeneous IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03273)
*Joy Okolo,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对物联网传感中联邦学习面临的传感器异构与动态变化挑战，提出了TIDE（Tier-aware Intra-pod Decoupled Encoders）方法。该方法通过为每个传感器设计独立编码器并在“pod”内合并参数，在三个多模态数据集上实现了高精度与低通信开销的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习应用于物联网传感时，设备携带的传感器不同且周期性上下线，导致每轮传感器集合变化。传统平均法会让无该传感器的设备覆盖其参数，单传感器设备也无法学习传感器间的关系。现有方法或假设设备始终在线，或忽略传感器差异进行客户端选择，均无法有效解决此问题。

Method: TIDE为每个传感器配备独立的编码器和辅助头，仅在持有该传感器的设备（即“pod”）内部进行参数合并；而融合层和任务头则由所有设备共享。这种设计实现了传感器特定参数的局部安全聚合与全局任务头的联合优化。

Result: 在三个多模态活动识别数据集上与八个基线对比，TIDE表现优异。在ActionSense上达到78.06%准确率，领先次优方法7.63个百分点。在通信量减半时，TIDE在所有数据集上均达到最准确；在同等通信量下也整体领先。

Conclusion: TIDE通过传感器级解耦与分层聚合，有效应对了动态异构传感器带来的挑战，显著提升了联邦学习在多模态物联网传感任务中的性能与通信效率。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is well suited to Internet of Things (IoT) sensing. However, devices carry different sensors and come online in periodic patterns, and as such, the sensors among the participants change from round to round. Under naive averaging, devices without a sensor overwrite its parameters, and a single-sensor device never learns how its sensor relates to the others. Existing methods either redesign local training for mismatched sensors (assuming always-available devices) or select clients without regard to their sensors. We propose Tier-aware Intra-pod Decoupled Encoders (TIDE). Specifically, each sensor has its own encoder and auxiliary head, whose parameters are merged only within the "pod" (i.e., the devices that hold the sensor), while the fusion layer and the task head remain shared by all devices. On three multimodal activity recognition datasets against eight baselines, TIDE reaches 78.06% on ActionSense, 7.63 points above the next method, is the most accurate on all three datasets at half its own communication, and at equal communication leads on ActionSense and is ahead within seed variation on the other two.

</details>


### [143] [From Reactive to Agentic Resilience: Multi-Timescale AI-RAN Orchestration Under Signaling Storms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03406)
*Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Mohammad Shojafar,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于AI智能体的分层韧性框架，用于协调预测、吸收、适应、恢复和演进等多个控制环节，在Open RAN中有效提前应对大容量附带的突发流量，无需事后补救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Open RAN韧性机制仅针对特定阶段设计，缺乏跨层级协调；需要一种统一智能体编排层来协调不同时间尺度上的控制回路。

Method: 构建离散事件Open RAN附加模拟器，捕捉重试放大、共享计算争用和不即时供应；提出三层框架：服务管理和编排层设定常态化策略，每站点非实时智能体判断风暴并预见计划人群，确定性近实时循环执行决策。实验对比了AI控制器、重分配服务器池、反应性权重设置等方案。

Result: 智能体控制器以四分之一容量达到完全分配资源库的韧性水平；94%的文本指令与未知意图精确匹配；单次指令即可重塑实时行为。提供了关于预测前瞻窗口的理论证明及后续推论结果。

Conclusion: 基于AI智能体的协调化韧性框架在性能上显著优于传统反应性方案，且具备良好的泛化和可扩展能力。

Abstract: Next-generation open radio access network (Open RAN) and AI-RAN deployments turn the RAN into a disaggregated, compute-aware platform whose service continuity depends on many control loops acting at different timescales. Resilience in this setting is best seen as a lifecycle spanning anticipation, absorption, adaptation, recovery, and evolution. Yet, existing mechanisms remain stage-specific and are rarely coordinated across the control hierarchy. This paper argues that agentic AI offers an orchestration layer that coordinates this lifecycle, turning operator intent into coordinated action while keeping slow language-model reasoning off the fast control path. Using mass-attachment signaling storms as a running study, we build a discrete-event Open RAN attach simulator that captures retry amplification, shared-compute contention, and non-instantaneous provisioning, and we propose a three-tier framework in which an orchestrator at the service management and orchestration layer sets a standing policy, a per-site non-real-time agent judges storms and anticipates scheduled crowds, and a deterministic near-real-time loop enforces every decision. Across experiments, the agentic controller approaches the resilience of a fully provisioned pool of servers at a quarter of its capacity by reading a scheduled event from a calendar and provisioning ahead of it. No reactive weight setting recovers this once the surge arrives. Free-text operator intents ground into control directives at 94% exact match on previously unseen intents, and a single instruction reshapes live behavior end to end. We also quantify the latency, cost, and contention limits that bound it. The supplementary material provides analytical proof of the derived Theorem on anticipation horizon, as well as the subsequent Corollary results on reactive provisioning limits.

</details>
